### [1] [GAP3D: Generative Alignment of VLM Latents to Patch-Level Embeddings for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28995)
*Polytimi Anna Gkotsi,Andrii Zadaianchuk,Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAP3D，一种模块化的扩散方法，将VLM生成的潜变量与预训练图像编码器的完整patch级特征空间对齐，使冻结的下游生成模型能够利用VLM作为提示编码器，同时保持空间结构化的条件信号，用于3D资产生成等几何感知任务。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有将VLM作为生成模型条件提示编码器的方法要么依赖昂贵的端到端训练，要么将特征映射到压缩表示中，丢失了3D资产生成等几何感知任务所需的密集空间结构信息。

Method: 提出GAP3D，一种模块化的扩散对齐方法，将VLM生成的潜变量直接对齐到预训练图像编码器的完整patch级特征空间，使下游冻结的生成模型在保持空间结构化条件信号的同时使用VLM作为提示编码器。

Result: 在3D资产生成任务上，方法通过主要训练通用域图像-文本对来绕过大规模3D数据的需要；展现出对多模态提示的涌现零样本能力（尽管仅使用文本输入训练）；在优先高层语义的同时，证明了VLM与图像编码器特征空间之间的表示差距可以通过扩散对齐部分弥合。

Conclusion: GAP3D证明了VLM与图像编码器特征空间之间的表示差距可以通过基于扩散的对齐部分弥合，为通过生成对齐到密集嵌入空间实现基础模型的模块化集成迈出了第一步。

Original Abstract: Recent approaches integrating vision-language models (VLMs) as prompt encoders for generative model conditioning typically rely on expensive end-to-end training or map features to compressed representations, discarding the dense spatial structure required for geometry-aware tasks like 3D asset generation. To address this, we propose GAP3D, a modular, diffusion-based approach that aligns VLM-generated latents directly to the complete, patch-level feature space of a pre-trained image encoder, enabling a frozen downstream generative model to utilize a VLM as prompt encoder while maintaining a spatially structured conditioning signal. Evaluated on 3D asset generation, our method bypasses the need for large-scale 3D data by training mainly on general-domain image-text pairs. It also exhibits emergent zero-shot capabilities for multimodal prompts, despite being trained exclusively on text input. Finally, while currently prioritizing high-level semantics over fine-grained detail, GAP3D demonstrates that the representation gap between VLM and image-encoder feature spaces can be partially bridged through diffusion-based alignment, taking the first steps towards a modular integration of foundation models through generative alignment to dense embedding spaces.

中文摘要翻译: 最近将视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为生成模型条件控制的提示编码器的方法，通常依赖于昂贵的端到端训练，或者将特征映射到压缩表示中，从而丢弃了3D资产生成等几何感知任务所需的密集空间结构。为解决这一问题，我们提出了GAP3D，一种模块化的、基于扩散的方法，将VLM生成的潜变量直接与预训练图像编码器的完整patch级特征空间对齐，使冻结的下游生成模型能够利用VLM作为提示编码器，同时保持空间结构化的条件信号。在3D资产生成任务上的评估表明，我们的方法主要通过训练通用域的图像-文本对来绕过大规模3D数据的需求。尽管仅使用文本输入进行训练，该方法还展现出对多模态提示的涌现零样本能力。最后，虽然目前优先考虑高层语义而非细粒度细节，GAP3D证明了VLM与图像编码器特征空间之间的表示差距可以通过基于扩散的对齐部分弥合，为通过生成对齐到密集嵌入空间实现基础模型的模块化集成迈出了初步尝试。

</details>


### [2] [Auditing Training-Free 3D Shape Retrieval with Diffused Geodesic Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29004)
*Zhicheng Du,Changyue Liu,Wenji Xi,Zhaotian Xie,Zhuo Deng,Ziheng Zhang,Yang Liu,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视无训练形状描述符的评估问题，提出“协议审计”框架，引入扩散测地矩（DGM）作为非谱基线和协议效应隔离工具，并通过可复现的实验揭示输入场和聚合协议对描述符性能的主导作用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无训练形状描述符的评估将局部信号设计、归一化、聚合、码本拟合和度量选择等因素混杂在一起，难以对单个组件进行独立评估。本文旨在通过协议审计的方式解耦这些因素，实现公平且可解释的组件级比较。

Method: 提出扩散测地矩（DGM），一种种子条件化的形状描述符：先计算稀疏隐式热响应，将其转换为距离类场，再通过跨种子和尺度的低阶矩汇总每个顶点的特征。DGM既作为实用的非谱基线，也作为隔离协议效应的分析工具。同时构建了基于热核特征（HKS）的几何矩形状描述符基线（GMSD-HKS）用于对比实验。

Result: 在FAUST-Reg和TOSCA数据集上的聚合匹配实验表明：(1) GMSD-HKS在mAP/top-1上取得最高分（0.621/0.820和0.865/0.963）；(2) 波核签名（WKS）仍是最强的经典信号；(3) DGM在稀疏求解、非谱部署或对称信息种子帧优先的场景下表现有用。更重要的发现是：输入场和聚合协议对描述符性能的影响可能主导矩公式本身的作用。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是方法论的：提供了可复现的协议级联分析框架、用于功能映射兼容性的跨形状对齐诊断工具，以及设计、报告无训练形状描述符的具体建议。研究强调了输入场和聚合协议在描述符评估中的主导地位，呼吁社区关注评估协议本身的规范。

Original Abstract: Reported retrieval scores for training-free shape descriptors conflate local signal design, normalization, aggregation, codebook fitting, and metric choices, making isolated component evaluation difficult. This paper reframes descriptor evaluation as a {\em protocol audit}. We introduce Diffused Geodesic Moments (DGM), a seed-conditioned descriptor that computes sparse implicit heat responses, converts them to distance-like fields, and summarizes each vertex by low-order moments across seeds and scales. DGM is used both as a practical non-spectral baseline and as an instrument for isolating protocol effects. On the registered FAUST benchmark split (FAUST-Reg) and the TOSCA shape collection, aggregation-matched experiments show that an independent Geometric Moment Shape Descriptor baseline built on Heat Kernel Signature features (GMSD-HKS) obtains the highest scores in this implementation ($0.621/0.820$ and $0.865/0.963$ mean average precision (mAP)/top-1), Wave Kernel Signature (WKS) remains a strong classical signal, and DGM is useful mainly when sparse solves, non-spectral deployment, or symmetry-informative seed frames are priorities. The broader finding is methodological: the input field and aggregation protocol can dominate the moment formula. The paper contributes a reproducible protocol-cascade analysis, a cross-shape alignment diagnostic for functional-map compatibility, and concrete recommendations for designing and reporting training-free shape descriptors.

中文摘要翻译: 已报道的无训练形状描述符的检索分数将局部信号设计、归一化、聚合、码本拟合和度量选择混为一谈，使得孤立的组件评估变得困难。本文将描述符评估重新构建为一种协议审计。我们引入了扩散测地矩（DGM），一种种子条件化的描述符，它计算稀疏隐式热响应，将其转换为距离类场，并通过跨种子和尺度的低阶矩汇总每个顶点。DGM既用作实用的非谱基线，也用作隔离协议效应的工具。在已配准的FAUST基准测试子集（FAUST-Reg）和TOSCA形状数据集上，聚合匹配的实验表明：基于热核签名特征构建的独立几何矩形状描述符基线（GMSD-HKS）在此实现中取得了最高分数（平均精度（mAP）/top-1分别为0.621/0.820和0.865/0.963）；波核签名（WKS）仍然是一个强有效的经典信号；而DGM主要用于稀疏求解、非谱部署或需要对称信息种子帧的场景。更广泛的发现是方法论的：输入场和聚合协议可能主导矩公式的作用。本文贡献了可复现的协议级联分析、用于功能映射兼容性的跨形状对齐诊断工具，以及关于设计和报告无训练形状描述符的具体建议。

</details>


### [3] [Embodied3DBench: Benchmarking Low-Level Embodied Spatial Intelligence of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29074)
*Jiyao Zhang,Mingxu Zhang,Yitong Peng,Haoxuan Liu,Chenshuo Wang,Yuxing Long,Haoyang Huang,Dongjiang Li,Nan Duan,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Embodied3DBench，一个面向具身3D环境的机器人中心基准，系统评估当前VLM在低级空间智能（如空间结构理解与交互导向感知）上的能力。实验发现现有模型在交互感知上表现脆弱，为此进一步合成了130万QA对的大规模训练数据集，微调后显著提升了低级空间智能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在2D视觉任务上表现优异，但在复杂3D具身环境中的低级空间理解与交互感知能力尚未被系统评估，缺乏专门的基准来揭示模型在真实机器人交互场景中的能力短板。

Method: 构建了包含6个任务类别（空间结构理解：定位、空间关系预测、多视图对应；交互导向感知： affordance预测、抓取点预测、轨迹预测）、12个子类别、超2.1万QA对的Embodied3DBench基准；评估了13个SOTA模型；针对能力差距合成了包含130万QA对的大规模训练数据集用于微调提升。

Result: 当前VLM在高级空间推理（如物体间位置关系）上表现较强，但交互导向感知（如抓取点、轨迹预测）方面仍脆弱；利用合成的130万QA对进行微调后，模型在低级空间智能上获得显著提升。

Conclusion: Embodied3DBench填补了具身3D环境中VLM低级空间智能系统评估的空白，既提供了评估框架也提供了可扩展的数据方案，为发展交互感知多模态系统指明了方向。

Original Abstract: Are current Vision Language Models (VLMs) ready to comprehend and reason about complex embodied interactions in 3D environments? We introduce Embodied3DBench, a robot-centric benchmark targeting low-level spatial intelligence in embodied 3D environments. To systematically evaluate these foundational perceptual capabilities, the benchmark includes 6 task categories divided into two core groups: Spatial Structural Understanding (Grounding, Spatial Relation Prediction, and Multi-view Correspondence) and Interaction-Oriented Perception (Affordance Prediction, Grasp Point Prediction, and Trajectory Prediction). The benchmark spans 12 subcategories and contains over 21k high-quality question-answer pairs. We evaluate 13 state-of-the-art models, and the results show that while current models exhibit relatively strong high-level spatial reasoning, such as understanding object-to-object positional relations, they remain fragile in interaction-oriented perception, highlighting a significant lack of robust 3D-aware interaction priors. To actively bridge this capability gap revealed by our benchmark, we further synthesize a large-scale training dataset comprising 1.3M QA pairs. Notably, fine-tuning on this dataset yields significant improvements in low-level spatial intelligence. Ultimately, Embodied3DBench fills a critical gap by providing both a systematic evaluation framework and a scalable data solution, setting a clear target for the development of interaction-aware multimodal systems.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的视觉语言模型（VLM）是否已准备好理解和推理3D环境中的复杂具身交互？为此，我们提出了Embodied3DBench——一个面向机器人中心的基准，专注于具身3D环境中的低级空间智能。为了系统评估这些基础感知能力，该基准包含6个任务类别，分为两大核心组：空间结构理解（定位、空间关系预测和多视图对应）与交互导向感知（可操作性预测、抓取点预测和轨迹预测）。该基准涵盖12个子类别，包含超过2.1万个高质量问答对。我们评估了13个最先进模型，结果表明：当前模型在高级空间推理方面表现相对较强，例如理解物体间的空间位置关系，但在交互导向感知方面仍然脆弱，凸显了其严重缺乏鲁棒的3D感知交互先验。为了积极弥合该基准所揭示的能力差距，我们进一步合成了一个包含130万问答对的大规模训练数据集。值得注意的是，在该数据集上微调后，模型的低级空间智能获得了显著提升。最终，Embodied3DBench通过提供系统化的评估框架和可扩展的数据解决方案填补了一个关键空白，为发展交互感知的多模态系统设定了明确目标。

</details>


### [4] [Low-Magnification SEM May Suffice: Interpretable Deep Learning for Multi-Scale Fracture-Cause Classification in Zirconia-Toughened Alumina](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29798)
*Julian Schmid,Pawel Astankow,Tom Vater,Julius Beck,Robert Cichon,Danny Krautz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的视觉Transformer（ViT）工作流，用于自动分类髋关节和膝关节植入物用氧化铝基复合材料（BIOLOX delta）中骨折原因的识别。在8493张SEM图像数据集上，微调后的ViT在严重类别不平衡下达到0.907的准确率和0.888的宏观F1分数，且低放大倍数性能与高倍相当，Grad-CAM定位符合经典断口特征，表明ViT可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前断口分析工作流耗时、部分主观且依赖高倍扫描电镜（SEM），亟需一种自动化、可解释的方法来可靠识别陶瓷植入物中的断裂起源，以保障质量控制和患者安全。

Method: 构建了一个包含8493张SEM图像的断口图像数据集（50x-10000x），标注为三个制造缺陷类别；微调视觉Transformer（ViT）进行自动分类；采用两阶段感知哈希/SSIM泄露审计确保数据无污染；使用Grad-CAM生成热力图进行可解释性分析。

Result: 微调ViT在分层五折交叉验证中达到0.907的准确率和0.888的宏观F1分数；低放大倍数（50x）性能与高倍（1k-10kx）相当；Grad-CAM定位在镜面、裂纹线、孔隙、加工痕迹等经典断口特征上，与断口学标准一致。

Conclusion: 可解释的ViT可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具，实现低倍预筛选，减少对耗时的高倍检测的依赖。

Original Abstract: Reliable identification of fracture origins in alumina matrix composite hip and knee implants is critical for quality assurance and patient safety, yet current fractographic workflows are time-consuming, partly subjective, and reliant on high-magnification scanning electron microscopy (SEM). We present an interpretable vision-transformer (ViT) workflow for automated classification of fracture causes in an alumina matrix composite (BIOLOX delta, CeramTec GmbH) widely used in total joint replacements. A dataset of 8,493 SEM images (50x-10,000x) was curated from five years of in-production burst and proof tests and annotated into three defect categories defined along the manufacturing chain: green body, hard machining, and material defects. Under severe class imbalance, the fine-tuned ViT reached an accuracy of 0.907 and a macro-F1 of 0.888 in stratified five-fold cross-validation, with a two-stage perceptual-hash/SSIM leakage audit confirming negligible specimen overlap. Notably, performance at low magnification (50x) was comparable to that at high magnification (1k-10kx), indicating that macro-scale features - mirror geometry and hackle line fields - already encode sufficient diagnostic signal. Grad-CAM attributions consistently localised on canonical fractographic cues (mirrors, hackles, pores, machining marks), aligning with established fractographic criteria. Together, these results position interpretable ViTs as a complementary tool for ceramic-implant quality assurance, enabling low-magnification pre-screening and reducing reliance on time-intensive high-magnification inspection.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠识别氧化铝基复合材料髋关节和膝关节植入物中的断裂起源，对于质量保证和患者安全至关重要。然而，当前断口分析工作流程耗时较长，部分主观，且依赖高倍扫描电镜（SEM）。我们提出了一种可解释的视觉Transformer（ViT）工作流，用于自动分类一种广泛用于全关节置换的氧化铝基复合材料（BIOLOX delta，CeramTec GmbH）的断裂原因。我们从五年的在产爆破试验和验证试验中筛选并标注了8493张SEM图像（50倍-10000倍），按照制造链定义了三种缺陷类别：坯体缺陷、硬加工缺陷和材料缺陷。在严重的类别不平衡条件下，微调后的ViT在分层五折交叉验证中达到了0.907的准确率和0.888的宏观F1分数，两阶段感知哈希/SSIM泄漏审计证实样本重叠可忽略不计。值得注意的是，低放大倍数（50倍）下的性能与高放大倍数（1000倍-10000倍）相当，表明宏观尺度特征——镜面几何和裂纹线场——已编码了足够的诊断信号。Grad-CAM归因一致地定位在经典断口学线索（镜面、裂纹线、孔隙、加工痕迹）上，与既定的断口学标准吻合。综合来看，这些结果表明可解释的ViT可作为陶瓷植入物质量保证的补充工具，实现低倍预筛选，并减少对耗时的高倍检测的依赖。

</details>


### [5] [Boosting Image Quality Assessment Performance: Unsupervised Score Fusion by Deep Maximum a Posteriori Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30269)
*Zhongling Wang,Raymond Zhou,Shahrukh Athar,Wenbo Yang,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度最大后验（MAP）估计的无监督IQA分数融合框架，通过细粒度不确定性估计融合多个图像质量评估模型的分数，提升预测精度并降低不确定性，同时具备自动排斥“坏”模型的能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的单个图像质量评估（IQA）模型往往对特定图像内容或失真类型存在偏差，因此直觉上希望融合多个模型的分数以取长补短。本文首次尝试为这一想法寻求最优解。

Method: 提出一种基于深度最大后验（MAP）估计的无监督IQA分数融合框架，在分数层面进行细粒度不确定性估计，以提升融合预测的准确性并降低不确定性。

Result: 实验表明，所提模型在性能上优于单个IQA模型及其他融合方法，并且展现出在融合过程中自动排斥“坏”模型的有趣能力。

Conclusion: 基于深度MAP估计的无监督分数融合框架能够有效整合多个IQA模型的优势，提升预测质量并具备模型选择能力。

Original Abstract: Over the past decades, numerous Image Quality Assessment (IQA) models have emerged, aiming to predict the perceptual quality of images. However, individual models are often biased toward certain types of image content or distortions, depending on the design principle and process. An intuitive idea is to harness the strengths and mitigate the weaknesses of each IQA model, by fusing the scores of multiple models into a stronger one. Here we make one of the first attempts to seek an optimal solution for the idea and propose a general framework for unsupervised IQA score fusion using deep Maximum a Posteriori (MAP) estimation. The proposed model conducts fine-grained uncertainty estimation at the score level to increase the accuracy and reduce the uncertainty in fused predictions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of the proposed model over individual IQA models and other fusion methods. It also exhibits an interesting capability of rejecting ``bad" models in the fusion process.

中文摘要翻译: 在过去的几十年中，涌现出大量图像质量评估（IQA）模型，旨在预测图像的感知质量。然而，由于设计原理和过程的差异，单个模型往往对特定类型的图像内容或失真存在偏差。一个直观的想法是通过融合多个模型的分数，取长补短，从而构建一个更强的模型。本文首次尝试为该想法寻求最优解，并提出一个基于深度最大后验（MAP）估计的无监督IQA分数融合通用框架。该模型在分数层面进行细粒度不确定性估计，以提高融合预测的准确性并降低不确定性。全面的实验表明，所提模型优于单个IQA模型及其他融合方法。此外，它还展现出在融合过程中自动排斥“坏”模型的有趣能力。

</details>


### [6] [GeRaF: Neural Geometry Reconstruction from Radio Frequency Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29097)
*Jiachen Lu,Hailan Shanbhag,Haitham Al Hassanieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeRaF首次利用神经隐式学习从射频信号重建近距3D几何，通过滤波渲染、物理射频体渲染和透镜采样策略克服射频信号低分辨率、高噪声和镜面反射等挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 射频信号虽能穿透遮挡物，但其无透镜成像特性导致分辨率低、噪声大，且信号在空间全向传播带来体渲染立方复杂度；此外射频信号与表面发生镜面反射，需要根本不同的建模方法。

Method: (1) 基于滤波的渲染以抑制无关信号；(2) 物理感知的射频体渲染管线；(3) 无透镜采样与无透镜alpha混合策略，使全空间采样在训练中可行；通过MLP和可训练参数学习符号距离函数、反射率和信号功率。

Result: 在真实场景中从射频信号重建毫米级几何结构，实现了射频信号3D重建的首个神经隐式学习方法。

Conclusion: GeRaF证明了神经隐式学习在射频信号3D几何重建中的可行性，为利用射频信号进行遮挡感知的精细几何重建开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: GeRaF is the first method to use neural implicit learning for near-range 3D geometry reconstruction from radio frequency (RF) signals. Unlike RGB or LiDAR-based methods, RF sensing can see through occlusion but suffers from low resolution and noise due to its lensless imaging nature. While lenses in RGB imaging constrain sampling to 1D rays, RF signals propagate through the entire space, introducing significant noise and leading to cubic complexity in volumetric rendering. Moreover, RF signals interact with surfaces via specular reflections, requiring fundamentally different modeling. To address these challenges, GeRaF (1) introduces filter-based rendering to suppress irrelevant signals, (2) implements a physics-based RF volumetric rendering pipeline, and (3) proposes a novel lensless sampling and lensless alpha blending strategy that makes full-space sampling feasible during training. By learning signed distance functions, reflectiveness, and signal power through MLPs and trainable parameters, GeRaF takes the first step towards reconstructing millimeter-level geometry from RF signals in real-world settings.

中文摘要翻译: GeRaF是首个利用神经隐式学习从射频（RF）信号重建近距3D几何结构的方法。与基于RGB或LiDAR的方法不同，射频传感能够穿透遮挡物，但由于其无透镜成像特性，存在分辨率低和噪声大的问题。RGB成像中的透镜将采样约束到一维光线，而射频信号在整个空间中传播，引入了显著噪声，并导致体渲染中的立方复杂度。此外，射频信号通过镜面反射与表面相互作用，需要根本不同的建模。为应对这些挑战，GeRaF (1) 引入基于滤波的渲染以抑制无关信号，(2) 实现基于物理的射频体渲染管线，(3) 提出一种新颖的无透镜采样和无透镜alpha混合策略，使全空间采样在训练中变得可行。通过MLP和可训练参数学习符号距离函数、反射率和信号功率，GeRaF迈出了在真实场景中从射频信号重建毫米级几何结构的第一步。

</details>


### [7] [Seeing through boxes: Non-Line-of-Sight 3D Reconstruction from Radar Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29098)
*Jiachen Lu,Hailan Shanbhag,Haitham Al Hassanieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeRaF 2.0 是一个统一视距（LoS）与非视距（NLoS）的神经几何重建框架，通过利用外部LoS几何约束来引导RF信号传播建模，实现了对可见与隐藏几何的一致、稳定重建，在RF几何重建任务上达到了新最优水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NLoS 3D神经重建方法在封闭环境中恢复粗糙表面时，存在优化不稳定、表面几何噪声大、表面模糊等问题，无法从有符号距离场（SDF）中产生精确的零级集。这些局限的根本原因在于忽视了封闭区域外部LoS几何的作用，而LoS几何能为信号传播建模提供有价值的物理约束。

Method: 提出了GeRaF 2.0统一框架，将外部LoS几何先验融入神经场公式中，利用LoS几何来建模和引导RF信号从LoS区域向NLoS区域的传播，实现了LoS与NLoS区域的联合几何重建。

Result: 实验证明GeRaF 2.0实现了稳定的训练过程，以及对可见和隐藏几何的物理一致重建，在基于RF的几何重建任务上达到了新的最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过整合视觉LoS先验，GeRaF 2.0有效解决了NLoS神经重建中的优化不稳定和表面模糊问题，为RF信号下的几何重建设立了新标杆。

Original Abstract: Reconstructing object geometry from radio frequency (RF) signals is fundamentally challenging due to the lensless imaging nature of RF sensing, which leads to low spatial resolution and high noise. Unlike light signals, RF signals can penetrate occlusions and thus capture information about hidden scenes. Existing Non-Line-of-Sight (NLoS) 3D neural reconstruction methods can recover coarse surfaces inside enclosed environments but often suffer from unstable optimization, noisy surface geometry, and surface ambiguity, failing to produce accurate zero-level sets from the signed distance field (SDF). These limitations largely stem from neglecting the role of Line-of-Sight (LoS) geometry outside the enclosed region, which provides valuable physical constraints for modeling signal propagation. In this paper, we introduce a Unified LoS and NLoS neural geometry reconstruction framework GeRaF 2.0 that leverages the outside LoS geometry to model and guide RF propagation from the LoS region into the NLoS region. By integrating visual LoS priors into the neural field formulation, GeRaF 2.0 achieves stable training and physically consistent reconstruction of both visible and hidden geometry, setting a new state-of-the-art in RF-based geometry reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 从射频（RF）信号重建物体几何因RF传感的无透镜成像特性而面临根本性挑战，这导致空间分辨率低、噪声高。与光信号不同，RF信号能够穿透遮挡物，从而捕捉隐藏场景的信息。现有的非视距（NLoS）3D神经重建方法可以恢复封闭环境内部的粗糙表面，但常常面临优化不稳定、表面几何噪声大以及表面模糊等问题，无法从有符号距离场（SDF）中生成精确的零级集。这些局限很大程度上源于忽略了封闭区域外部视距（LoS）几何的作用，而LoS几何为信号传播建模提供了有价值的物理约束。本文提出了一种统一的LoS与NLoS神经几何重建框架GeRaF 2.0，它利用外部LoS几何来建模并引导RF信号从LoS区域传播到NLoS区域。通过将视觉LoS先验整合到神经场公式中，GeRaF 2.0实现了对可见和隐藏几何的稳定训练与物理一致重建，在基于RF的几何重建领域树立了新的最优水平。

</details>


### [8] [Robust Cross-Domain Generalization Using Unlabeled Target Data with Source-Domain Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29122)
*Yuyue Zhou,Shrimanti Ghosh,Michael,Xie,Justin JY Kim,Jessica Knight,Steel McDonald,Vincent Man,Jacob L. Jaremko,Abhilash Hareendranathan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种目标信息引导的自监督预训练与模型集成策略，在无需目标域标签且数据严格分离的条件下，显著提升了儿科腕部骨折POCUS AI模型在跨设备（不同超声探头）场景下的骨结构分割性能（Dice提升>6%），实现了一种标签高效、隐私保护的跨设备鲁棒超声AI框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学影像AI模型在不同超声设备或临床站点间存在严重的域偏移问题，导致模型跨设备泛化性能差；而为新设备重新标注数据成本高昂且存在隐私顾虑，因此亟需一种无需目标域标签的跨设备泛化方法。

Method: 提出目标信息引导的自监督预训练与模型集成策略：结合掩码图像建模（MIM）和对比学习在无标签目标域数据上学习结构表征；设计置信度感知的融合头（confidence-aware infusion head）自适应整合预测结果；源域（Philips Lumify探头）带密集标签数据用于有监督训练，目标域（TeleMED便携探头）无标签数据仅用于预训练，全程数据严格分离。

Result: 在来自62个儿科POCUS视频的318张图像上，该方法在目标域上的Dice指标相比基线提升超过6%，显著改善了跨设备分割性能。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了一种标签高效且隐私保护的跨设备鲁棒超声AI框架，可扩展至多中心研究或联邦学习场景。

Original Abstract: It is often desirable to generalize medical imaging AI models trained with dense annotations to data acquired from different ultrasound scanners or clinical sites; however, retraining these models with new annotations is often difficult and costly. We examine this challenge in pediatric wrist fracture assessment using point-of-care ultrasound (POCUS), where fractures are common and can be effectively triaged via ultrasound. AI has shown radiologist-level performance for fracture detection, often aided by high-quality bony structure segmentation. However, due to significant domain shifts, models perform poorly on data from other centers or probes, and obtaining segmentation labels across devices is impractical due to manual annotation effort and data privacy concerns. To address this, we propose a target-informed self-supervised pretraining and model-ensemble strategy. Specifically, our approach combines masked image modeling (MIM) and contrastive learning to learn target-domain structural representations without labels, and introduces a confidence-aware infusion head to adaptively integrate predictions. The source dataset, collected with a Philips Lumify probe, contained dense labels, while the target dataset, acquired with a TeleMED portable probe, was unlabeled. The datasets were kept strictly separate throughout the entire process. Our method used labeled source data for supervised training and leveraged target-domain pretraining to improve generalization. On 318 images from 62 pediatric POCUS videos, this approach significantly improved cross-device performance, achieving over 6% Dice improvement on the target domain versus the baseline. These results demonstrate a label-efficient and privacy-preserving approach for cross-device-robust ultrasound AI, offering a framework that can be extended to multi-center studies or federated learning setups.

中文摘要翻译: 将具有密集标注的医学影像AI模型泛化到来自不同超声扫描仪或临床站点的数据通常是人们期望的；然而，用新标注重新训练这些模型往往困难且成本高昂。我们在儿科腕部骨折评估中，使用床旁超声（POCUS）检验了这一挑战——骨折在此类场景中常见，且可通过超声有效进行分诊。AI在骨折检测中已展现出放射科医生级别的性能，这通常得益于高质量的骨结构分割。然而，由于显著的域偏移，模型在其他中心或探头的数据上表现不佳，且由于人工标注工作量大和数据隐私问题，跨设备获取分割标签并不现实。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种目标信息引导的自监督预训练与模型集成策略。具体而言，我们的方法结合了掩码图像建模（MIM）和对比学习，以在无需标签的情况下学习目标域的结构表征，并引入了一个置信度感知的融合头来自适应地整合预测结果。源数据集（使用Philips Lumify探头采集）包含密集标签，而目标数据集（使用TeleMED便携探头采集）则无标签。两个数据集在整个过程中严格分离。我们的方法使用带标签的源数据进行有监督训练，并利用目标域预训练来提升泛化能力。在来自62个儿科POCUS视频的318张图像上，该方法显著提升了跨设备性能，相比基线在目标域上实现了超过6%的Dice改进。这些结果展示了一种标签高效且隐私保护的方法，用于实现跨设备鲁棒的超声AI，并提供了一个可扩展至多中心研究或联邦学习设置的框架。

</details>


### [9] [Uni-RCM: Unified Reference-guided Cross-modal Mapping for Multi-Class Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29455)
*Yangchen Wu,Huiqiang Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Uni-RCM统一框架，通过可学习的参考引导块和离线残差量化器，解决多类别工业异常检测中类别间干扰和特征流形混淆问题，在MVTec-3D AD数据集上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态工业异常检测方法通常为每个产品类别单独建模，限制了实际可扩展性；统一范式下类别间干扰和特征流形混淆导致检测精度下降。

Method: 提出统一参考引导跨模态映射框架Uni-RCM，包含两个核心模块：(1) 参考引导块，通过引入可学习参考特征动态滤除类别特定噪声，捕获跨模态共性；(2) 离线残差量化器，通过多个级联码本刻画正常分布。

Result: 在MVTec-3D AD数据集上的大量评估表明，在挑战性的多类别设置下，该方法在图像级别检测和像素级别定位方面均取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: Uni-RCM通过统一框架有效解决了多类别工业异常检测中的类别间干扰问题，在多类别设置下取得了SOTA性能，具有良好的实际可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Multi-modal industrial anomaly detection typically relies on separate models for each product category, fundamentally limiting practical scalability. When shifting to a unified paradigm that handles diverse classes simultaneously, detection accuracy often degrades due to inter-class interference and feature manifold confusion. To overcome these challenges, we propose a Unified Reference guided Cross-modal Mapping framework, named Uni-RCM. At its core, we propose a reference guide block to dynamically filter out category-specific noise by introducing a learnable reference feature, which captures the commonalities across different modalities. Besides, an offline residual quantizer is proposed to characterize the normal distribution by multiple cascaded codebooks. Extensive evaluations on the MVTec-3D AD dataset demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance in the challenging multi-class setting and in terms of image-level detection and pixel-level localization.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态工业异常检测通常依赖针对每个产品类别的单独模型，这从根本上限制了实际的可扩展性。当转向同时处理多种类别的统一范式时，由于类别间干扰和特征流形混淆，检测精度往往会下降。为克服这些挑战，我们提出了一种统一参考引导的跨模态映射框架，命名为Uni-RCM。其核心是，我们提出一个参考引导块，通过引入可学习的参考特征来动态滤除类别特定噪声，从而捕获不同模态之间的共性。此外，我们提出一个离线残差量化器，通过多个级联码本刻画正常分布。在MVTec-3D AD数据集上的大量评估表明，该方法在具有挑战性的多类别设置下，以及在图像级别检测和像素级别定位方面，均取得了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [10] [Guidance Contrastive Token Credit Assignment for Discrete Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29198)
*Shufan Li,Konstantinos Kallidromitis,Akash Gokul Yusuke Kato,Kazuki Kozuka,Aditya Grover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GCPO算法，通过正负提示对比实现token级信用分配，在文本到图像生成和思维链推理任务上优于GRPO和DAPO。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于群体优势的强化学习方法（如GRPO、DAPO）使用样本级奖励，对所有token进行统一信用分配，无法捕捉细粒度的token级贡献。

Method: 提出引导对比策略优化（GCPO），通过对比正负提示下的模型预测差异，为每个token分配与对比预测差值成比例的token级优势，实现精准的逐token信用分配。

Result: GCPO在文本到图像生成中关注与文本提示对齐的视觉区域，在思维链任务中关注关键推理关键词；在两类任务上均持续优于GRPO和DAPO基线。

Conclusion: GCPO是一种通用且可扩展的离散策略学习优化策略，通过token级信用分配有效提升了策略学习性能。

Original Abstract: Group-advantage-based reinforcement learning methods, such as GRPO and DAPO, have demonstrated strong performance across diverse domains, including mathematical reasoning and text-to-image generation. However, their reliance on sample-level rewards introduces a key limitation as uniform credit assignment across all tokens fails to capture fine-grained, token-level contributions. To address this issue, we propose Guidance Contrastive Policy Optimization (GCPO), a novel algorithm that enables per-token credit assignment by contrasting model predictions under positive and negative prompts. Rather than uniformly broadcasting sample-level advantages, GCPO assigns token-level advantages proportional to the difference between these contrastive predictions, allowing more precise and informative learning signals. Empirically, we find that GCPO emphasizes semantically relevant regions such as visual areas aligned with textual prompts in text-to-image generation, and critical keywords within reasoning traces for chain-of-thought tasks. Through extensive experiments, GCPO consistently outperforms GRPO and DAPO baselines on both text-to-image generation and chain-of-thought reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness as a general and scalable optimization strategy for discrete policy learning.

中文摘要翻译: 基于群体优势的强化学习方法（如GRPO和DAPO）在数学推理和文本到图像生成等多个领域展现出强大性能。然而，它们依赖样本级奖励带来了一个关键局限性，即对所有token进行统一信用分配，无法捕捉细粒度的token级贡献。为解决这一问题，我们提出引导对比策略优化（GCPO），一种通过对比正负提示下的模型预测来实现逐token信用分配的新算法。GCPO不采用统一广播样本级优势，而是为每个token分配与这些对比预测差值成比例的优势，从而提供更精确且信息量更大的学习信号。实验发现，GCPO在文本到图像生成中强调与文本提示对齐的语义相关视觉区域，在思维链任务中强调推理轨迹中的关键关键词。通过大量实验，GCPO在文本到图像生成和思维链推理基准上均持续优于GRPO和DAPO基线，证明了其作为离散策略学习中通用且可扩展的优化策略的有效性。

</details>


### [11] [Towards the automated segmentation of epicardial and mediastinal fats: A multi-manufacturer approach using intersubject registration and random forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29217)
*É. O. Rodrigues,A. Conci,F. F. C. Morais,M. G. Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数据挖掘分类算法，在CT图像上全自动分割心包膜内外两种心脏脂肪组织的方法，达到了98.4%的准确率和96.8%的Dice相似系数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 心脏周围脂肪量与多种健康风险因素相关，且独立于全身脂肪量，因此定量分析心脏脂肪组织至关重要。现有方法需要大量人工干预，本文旨在提出一种全自动、高可重复性的分割方法。

Method: 方法包括三个步骤：(1) 配准，将输入图像粗略调整至标准空间；(2) 提取像素及其邻域的特征；(3) 基于数据挖掘分类算法的分割步骤，判断每个像素所属的组织类型。

Result: 心外膜和纵隔脂肪分割的平均准确率为98.4%，平均真阳性率为96.2%，平均Dice相似系数为96.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够全自动分割两种心脏脂肪组织，人工干预极少，易于复现，且达到了很高的分割精度和相似度指标。

Original Abstract: The amount of fat on the surroundings of the heart is correlated to several health risk factors such as carotid stiffness, coronary artery calcification, atrial fibrillation, atherosclerosis, cancer incidence and others. Furthermore, the cardiac fat varies unrelated to the overall fat of the subject, and, therefore, it reinforces the quantitative analysis of these adipose tissues as being essential. Clinical decision support systems are computer programs capable of evaluating information and providing a corresponding diagnosis or data to complement the physicists' analyses. The aim of this work is to propose a method capable of fully automatically segmenting two types of cardiac adipose tissues that stand apart from each other by the pericardium on CT images obtained by the standard acquisition protocol used for coronary calcium scoring. Much effort was devoted to promote minimal user intervention and ease of reproducibility. The methodology proposed in this work consists of a registration, which will roughly adjust input images to a standard, an extraction of features related to pixels and their surrounding area and a segmentation step based on data mining classification algorithms that define if an incoming pixel is of a certain type. Experimentations showed that the achieved mean accuracy for the epicardial and mediastinal fats was 98.4% with a mean true positive rate of 96.2%. In average, the Dice similarity index was equal to 96.8%.

中文摘要翻译: 心脏周围脂肪量与多种健康风险因素相关，如颈动脉僵硬度、冠状动脉钙化、心房颤动、动脉粥样硬化、癌症发病率等。此外，心脏脂肪量的变化与受试者的全身脂肪量无关，因此对这些脂肪组织进行定量分析至关重要。临床决策支持系统是能够评估信息并提供相应诊断或数据的计算机程序，以辅助物理师的分析。本文旨在提出一种方法，能够在通过冠状动脉钙化评分标准采集协议获得的CT图像上，全自动分割由心包膜分隔的两种心脏脂肪组织。研究致力于最大程度减少人工干预并提高可重复性。所提出的方法包括以下步骤：配准，将输入图像大致调整至标准空间；提取像素及其周围区域的特征；以及基于数据挖掘分类算法的分割步骤，判断输入像素是否属于某一特定类型。实验表明，心外膜和纵隔脂肪的平均准确率为98.4%，平均真阳性率为96.2%。平均Dice相似系数为96.8%。

</details>


### [12] [Motion-guided sparse correction enables expert-quality point tracking across diverse microscopy regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29220)
*Leonidas Zimianitis,Pasindu Thenahandi,Kai Buckhalter,Dineth Jayakody,Julian O. Kimura,Xinyue Liang,Karen Cunningham,Azeem Ahmad,Balpreet S. Ahluwalia,Sampath Jayarathna,Nikos Chrisochoides,Brandon Weissbourd,Dushan N. Wadduwage*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIPPLE提出了一种基于稀疏纠正的轨迹标注方法，用户只需点击起始点即可生成完整轨迹，仅在轨迹偏移时进行干预，在五个显微视频数据集上以3-25倍的点击量减少达到与全手动标注相当的质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非规范生物系统的显微视频中目标跟踪一直存在挑战，现有方法依赖专家标注数据进行评估和适配，但穷举式手动标注难以扩展到实际需要的视频规模。

Method: RIPPLE将标注任务重构为稀疏纠正过程：用户点击起始点后系统提出完整轨迹，用户仅在轨迹漂移处进行干预纠正。

Result: 在五个挑战性显微数据集（四个来自透明水母Clytia hemisphaerica，一个追踪快速运动精子）上，RIPPLE达到与全手动标注相当的质量，同时将手动点击量减少3到25倍。

Conclusion: RIPPLE填补了手动标注与全自动跟踪之间的空白，能够实现对生物动力学的即时量化、方法基准测试，并生成适应未来自动化显微跟踪器所需的金标准数据。

Original Abstract: Tracking the dynamics of non-canonical biological systems in microscopy videos remains a persistent challenge. Both classical and learning-based trackers depend on expert-reviewed data to be evaluated and adapted, yet exhaustive manual annotation rarely scales to the videos where these tools are needed most. We developed RIPPLE (Refinement Interpolation Platform for Point Location Estimation), which recasts annotation as sparse correction: a user clicks a starting point, RIPPLE proposes a full trajectory, and the user intervenes only where the trajectory drifts. We tested RIPPLE on five challenging microscopy datasets from our laboratories, four from the transparent jellyfish Clytia hemisphaerica and one tracking landmarks on rapidly moving sperm. Across these, RIPPLE matched the quality of exhaustive manual annotation while reducing manual clicks by 3 to 25 times across datasets. RIPPLE thereby fills a missing layer between manual annotation and fully automated tracking, enabling immediate quantification of biological dynamics, method benchmarking, and the production of the gold-standard data needed to adapt future automated microscopy trackers.

中文摘要翻译: 在显微视频中追踪非规范生物系统的动力学仍然是一个长期存在的挑战。经典方法和基于学习的跟踪器都依赖于经过专家审核的数据进行评估和适配，然而穷举式手动标注很少能扩展到最需要这些工具的视频规模。我们开发了RIPPLE（点位置估计的精炼插值平台），将标注重构为稀疏纠正过程：用户点击一个起始点，RIPPLE提出完整的轨迹，用户仅在轨迹漂移处进行干预。我们在来自实验室的五个具有挑战性的显微数据集上测试了RIPPLE，其中四个来自透明水母Clytia hemisphaerica，另一个追踪快速移动精子上的关键点。在这些数据集上，RIPPLE匹配了穷举手动标注的质量，同时将手动点击量减少了3到25倍。因此，RIPPLE填补了手动标注与全自动跟踪之间的缺失层次，能够实现对生物动力学的即时量化、方法基准测试，以及生成适应未来自动化显微跟踪器所需的金标准数据。

</details>


### [13] [An Approach for Thyroid Nodule Analysis Using Thermographic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29221)
*J. R. González,É. O. Rodrigues,C. P. Damião,C. A. P. Fontes,A. C. Silva,A. C. Paiva,H. Li,C. Du,A. Conci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了热成像技术在甲状腺癌检测中的应用，提出了图像采集协议与自动配准方法，并开展了特征提取、图像处理及健康/非健康分类的初步分析，作为大学医院肿瘤检测试点项目的一部分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 甲状腺癌在女性中发病率高且呈上升趋势，早期检测对提高生存率至关重要。热成像作为一种无创诊断工具已被用于癌症检测，但医学诊断信息量大、医生分析时间有限，亟需自动化的图像分析与分类方法来辅助临床决策。

Method: 对甲状腺热成像进行了综述研究，提出了图像采集协议和自主图像配准方法，并开展了包括特征提取、图像处理以及健康/非健康患者的分类方法探索。

Result: 本文给出了一个大学医院肿瘤检测的试点项目方案，尚未提交伦理委员会审批，属于初步研究阶段。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个基于热成像的甲状腺肿瘤检测试点项目，通过协议制定、自动配准和分类分析，旨在支持内分泌科的预防性医疗行动；项目将在未来调整后提交伦理审查。

Original Abstract: Thyroid cancer is said to be the second most common type of cancer in female individuals and the third in males by 2030, according to projections. In general, detecting cancer in its early stages improves the chance of survival of the individual. Thermography is a diagnostic tool that has been increasingly used to detect cancer and abnormalities, including that of thyroid. Various methods to segment and detect hot regions in thermograms and, consequently, to detect suspicious tissues present in these images have been proposed. It is well known that medical diagnosis yields a great deal of information. Thus, physicians have to comprehensively analyse and evaluate this information in a short period of time, which is infeasible in most cases. In this work, we perform a general review of thermography , focusing on the thyroid analysis. We propose protocols for image acquisiton and an autonomous registration for thyroid images. We also perform analyses of the image data, which include feature extraction, image processing, and a possible approach for classification of healthy or unhealthy patients. In summary, this work presents a pilot project for detection of tumors in our university hospital, which is part of an effort to support preventive medical actions in our endocrinology department. Under some future adjustments, this project will be submitted for approval by the ethics and research committee of Hospital Universitário Antonio Pedro at Universidade Federal Fluminense (HUAP-UFF) and to the Brazilian Ministry of Health Ethical committee under the name: Evaluation of the importance of thermography to aid diagnosis of thyroid nodules of patients in HUAP-UFF (in Portuguese: Avaliação da importância da termografia no auxílio à investigação diagnóstica de nódulos tireoidianos em pacientes acompanhados no HUAP-UFF).

中文摘要翻译: 据预测，到2030年，甲状腺癌将成为女性第二常见的癌症类型，男性中则为第三。一般而言，在癌症早期进行检测能提高患者的生存几率。热成像作为一种诊断工具，已被越来越多地用于检测癌症和异常情况，包括甲状腺病变。已有多种方法被提出用于分割和检测热成像图中的热区，进而识别图像中存在的可疑组织。众所周知，医学诊断会产生大量信息，因此医生需要在短时间内全面分析和评估这些信息，而这在大多数情况下是难以实现的。本文对热成像技术进行了综述，重点关注甲状腺分析。我们提出了甲状腺图像的采集协议和自主配准方法，并对图像数据进行了分析，包括特征提取、图像处理，以及健康与非健康患者分类的一种可能方法。总之，本研究提出了一个在我们大学医院进行的肿瘤检测试点项目，这是支持我院内分泌科预防性医疗行动的一部分。经未来调整后，该项目将提交至弗鲁米嫩塞联邦大学安东尼奥·佩德罗大学医院（HUAP-UFF）伦理与研究委员会以及巴西卫生部伦理委员会审批，项目名称为：评估热成像对HUAP-UFF患者甲状腺结节诊断辅助的重要性（葡萄牙语：Avaliação da importância da termografia no auxílio à investigação diagnóstica de nódulos tireoidianos em pacientes acompanhados no HUAP-UFF）。

</details>


### [14] [Toward Ethical Facial Age Estimation: A Generalized Zero-Shot Benchmark Without Training on Children's Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29230)
*Caio Petrucci,Leo Sampaio Ferraz Ribeiro,Sandra Avila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用零样本面部年龄估计基准，在训练时明确排除儿童数据，但仍评估模型对年轻人群的表现。实验表明所有现有方法均无法泛化到未见过的年龄组，平均性能下降46.4%，且存在已知类别偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有面部年龄估计方法通常依赖包含未成年人图像的数据进行训练，这引发了严重的伦理、法律和隐私问题。本文旨在解决这一矛盾，在排除儿童训练数据的同时仍能评估模型对年轻人群的年龄估计能力。

Method: 提出了一个通用零样本年龄估计基准：对六个广泛使用的数据集进行标准化划分，严格按年龄组分离——18-59岁用于训练、验证和测试；18岁以下仅用于零样本评估；60岁以上作为分布偏移下的未见验证集。对于含身份注释的数据集，采用主体互斥划分以防止身份泄露。

Result: 评估了九种最先进的年龄估计方法，所有方法均无法泛化到未见过的年龄组，性能平均下降46.4%，最高达52.8%。模型并非简单退化，而是系统性地将未见年龄的预测锚定到附近的已见类别，表现出已知类别偏差。

Conclusion: 本文通过将无儿童数据的年龄估计形式化为现有数据集上的通用零样本学习基准，揭示了当前建模实践与真实世界伦理约束之间的关键差距，为受限数据场景下的模型评估提供了原则性基础，并鼓励开发对分布偏移鲁棒且符合负责任数据使用的方法。

Original Abstract: Age estimation from facial images typically relies on training data that includes images of minors, a practice that raises serious ethical, legal, and privacy concerns. In this work, we propose a generalized zero-shot benchmark for facial age estimation that explicitly excludes children's data during training while still assessing model performance on younger populations. We revisit six widely used datasets and introduce standardized splits with strict age-group separation: samples aged 18-59 for training, validation, and testing; samples under 18 reserved exclusively for zero-shot evaluation; and samples 60+ as an unseen validation set for model selection under distribution shift. For datasets with identity annotations, subject-exclusive splits prevent identity leakage and better reflect real-world deployment conditions. Evaluating nine state-of-the-art age estimation methods under this protocol reveals that all evaluated methods consistently fail to generalize to unseen age groups, suffering substantial performance degradation -- on average 46.4%, and up to 52.8% -- relative to the supervised baseline. Moreover, models do not simply degrade: they systematically anchor predictions for unseen ages to nearby seen classes, a manifestation of the well-known seen-class bias in generalized zero-shot learning. By formalizing age estimation without children's data as a generalized zero-shot benchmark on existing datasets, this work highlights a critical gap between current modeling practices and real-world ethical constraints. Our benchmark provides a principled basis for evaluating models under restricted data regimes and encourages the development of methods that are robust to distribution shift and aligned with responsible data use.

中文摘要翻译: 基于面部图像进行年龄估计通常依赖于包含未成年人图像的训练数据，这种做法引发了严重的伦理、法律和隐私问题。在本工作中，我们提出了一种面向面部年龄估计的通用零样本基准，该基准在训练时明确排除儿童数据，同时仍能评估模型在年轻人群上的表现。我们重新审视了六个广泛使用的数据集，并引入了具有严格年龄组分离的标准化划分：18-59岁的样本用于训练、验证和测试；18岁以下的样本仅保留用于零样本评估；60岁及以上的样本作为分布偏移下的未见验证集用于模型选择。对于包含身份注释的数据集，主体互斥划分防止了身份泄露，并能更好地反映真实世界的部署条件。在该协议下评估九种最先进的年龄估计方法发现，所有被评估的方法均无法泛化到未见过的年龄组，性能遭受严重下降——相对于有监督基线平均下降46.4%，最高达52.8%。此外，模型并非简单退化：它们系统性地将未见年龄的预测锚定到附近的已见类别，这是通用零样本学习中已知类别偏差的典型表现。通过将无儿童数据的年龄估计形式化为现有数据集上的通用零样本学习基准，本工作揭示了当前建模实践与真实世界伦理约束之间的关键差距。我们的基准为受限数据场景下的模型评估提供了原则性基础，并鼓励开发对分布偏移鲁棒且符合负责任数据使用的方法。

</details>


### [15] [Pocket-Dentist: On-Device Dental Image Understanding via Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29299)
*Kai Bian,Xucheng Guo,Bin Chen,Lingyan Ruan,Yiran Shen,Ting Dang,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Pocket-Dentist，一个面向牙科多模态问答的效率感知基准，发现紧凑型VLM（2B参数）在牙科图像理解中准确率更高且计算成本更低，在iPhone 17 Pro上部署延迟降低4.9倍、内存降低2.3倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有牙科视觉语言模型评估分散在不同数据集、任务定义和指标上，且忽略了计算成本，限制了其在专科中心之外的广泛部署，而实际临床预筛查需要及时推理、有限硬件和本地处理患者图像以保护隐私。

Method: 构建Pocket-Dentist基准，汇集3个数据集（约1159名患者）、5种任务类型和7个指标，在14个典型VLM上进行评估，并对紧凑型VLM进行微调部署到iPhone 17 Pro上。

Result: 紧凑型VLM（如2B参数模型）在准确性上优于更大的VLM，且计算成本显著更低；微调后的Pocket-Dentist-2B在iPhone 17 Pro上每样本处理4.31秒，相比7B基线延迟降低4.9倍，内存使用降低2.3倍。

Conclusion: 在牙科图像理解中，紧凑型VLM在准确性和计算效率方面均优于大型VLM，更适合资源受限的本地部署场景，有助于实现隐私保护的临床预筛查。

Original Abstract: Evaluations of dental vision-language models remain fragmented across datasets, task definitions and metrics, and often ignore their computational cost. This limits their widespread deployment for dental screening outside specialist centres, where timely inference, limited hardware, and local handling of patient images are vital for practical, privacy-preserving clinical prescreening. Here we present Pocket-Dentist, an efficiency-aware benchmark for dental multimodal question answering that brings together three datasets spanning approximately 1,159 patients, five task types and seven metrics. Across typical 14 VLMs, our results reveals an interesting observation: compact VLMs (e.g., 2B-parameter models) outperform larger VLMs in accuracy while requiring substantially lower computational costs in dental image understanding. Deployed locally on an iPhone 17 Pro, our finetuned compact VLM Pocket-Dentist-2B processed each sample in 4.31 s, reducing latency by 4.9-fold and memory use by 2.3-fold compared with a 7B baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 牙科视觉语言模型的评估目前仍分散在不同的数据集、任务定义和指标中，且常常忽略其计算成本。这限制了它们在专科中心之外广泛部署用于牙科筛查，而及时推理、有限硬件及本地化处理患者图像对于实用且保护隐私的临床预筛查至关重要。本文提出了Pocket-Dentist，一个面向牙科多模态问答的效率感知基准，汇集了三个数据集，涵盖约1,159名患者、五种任务类型和七个指标。在14个典型VLM上的评估揭示了一个有趣的发现：紧凑型VLM（如2B参数模型）在牙科图像理解中不仅准确率更高，而且计算成本显著更低。在iPhone 17 Pro上本地部署后，我们微调后的紧凑型VLM Pocket-Dentist-2B处理每个样本仅需4.31秒，与7B基线相比，延迟降低了4.9倍，内存使用减少了2.3倍。

</details>


### [16] [CapTalk: Text-Guided Stylization and Speech-Driven 3D Head Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29316)
*Xuangeng Chu,Yuan Gan,Ziteng Cui,Shuhong Liu,Jian Wang,Bing Zhou,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文本描述的可分离控制说话风格与情感的音频驱动3D面部动画框架，支持实时生成与音频同步的唇部和表情动画，并实现动态情感控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音频驱动3D面部动画方法依赖预定义的身份或风格潜特征，用户难以自由控制说话风格；且对整段音频应用固定风格或身份会导致面部动画风格无法适应音频的情感内容。

Method: 构建了包含风格和情感文本描述的大规模数据集；提出了一种新颖的说话头生成框架，将说话风格和角色情感的文本描述与驱动音频流共同作为输入，实现对风格和情感的分离控制；支持推理过程中的动态情感控制。

Result: 模型能够实时生成与输入描述高度匹配的唇部同步动作和面部表情；支持音频中目标情感变化时的动态情感控制。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视风格与情感的纠缠关系，并引入文本描述作为控制信号，所提框架有效实现了对说话风格和情感的独立控制，提升了面部动画对音频情感内容的适应性。

Original Abstract: Audio-driven 3D facial animation aims to generate synchronized lip movements and vivid facial expressions from arbitrary audio clips. While existing methods can produce synchronized lip motions, they often rely on predefined identity or style latent features, which limits users' ability to freely control speaking styles. Moreover, applying a fixed style or identity to an entire audio segment typically results in facial animation styles that do not adapt to the emotional content of the audio. To address these challenges, we revisit the entanglement between style and emotion, construct a large-scale dataset with textual descriptions of both style and emotion, and propose a novel talking head generation framework that enables separate control over style and emotion. Our model takes as input both textual descriptions of speaking style and character emotion, as well as the driving audio stream, enabling real-time generation of highly synchronized lip movements and facial expressions that match the provided descriptions. Furthermore, our model supports dynamic emotion control during inference, allowing it to handle scenarios where the target emotion changes throughout the speech.

中文摘要翻译: 音频驱动的3D面部动画旨在从任意音频片段中生成同步的唇部运动和生动的面部表情。现有方法虽然能够产生同步的唇部动作，但往往依赖于预定义的身份或风格潜特征，这限制了用户自由控制说话风格的能力。此外，对整段音频应用固定的风格或身份通常会导致面部动画风格无法适应音频的情感内容。为解决这些挑战，我们重新审视了风格与情感之间的纠缠关系，构建了一个包含风格和情感文本描述的大规模数据集，并提出了一种新颖的说话头生成框架，能够实现对风格和情感的分离控制。我们的模型将说话风格和角色情感的文本描述以及驱动音频流作为输入，能够实时生成与给定描述高度匹配的高度同步的唇部运动和面部表情。此外，我们的模型在推理过程中支持动态情感控制，使其能够处理目标情感在说话过程中变化的场景。

</details>


### [17] [Multi-Stage VLM Pipeline for Zero-Shot Traffic Accident Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29325)
*Fumiya Tatematsu,Fumihiko Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ACCIDENT挑战赛的第一名解决方案，该方案基于冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct和235B Mixture-of-Experts模型，通过三级流水线（全视频联合预测、时间精细化和单帧撞击中心定位）实现零样本事故预测，最终在公开榜和私有榜上分别达到0.55469和0.57080，比最强基线Molmo-7B提升约0.21。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ACCIDENT挑战赛要求从CCTV监控视频中零样本预测事故时间、撞击中心和碰撞类型，现有方法性能有限，作者旨在构建一个高效的零样本事故预测流水线，在冻结大规模视觉语言模型的前提下取得最佳性能。

Method: 基于冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct检查点，构建三级流水线：(1) 全视频联合预测事故时间、中心点和碰撞类型；(2) 时间精细化模块修正时间预测；(3) 单帧撞击中心定位。然后将相同的流水线在235B Mixture-of-Experts模型上运行一次，将两个输出以9:1的比例融合，最后将每个预测点对齐到最近的车辆检测结果上。

Result: 公开LB上达到0.55469，私有LB上达到0.57080，相比最强基线Molmo-7B（0.358）提升约0.21，赢得挑战赛第一名。

Conclusion: 所提出的三级流水线结合大模型融合策略在零样本事故预测任务上显著优于现有方法，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性，负结果分析为最终设计提供了指导。

Original Abstract: We present the 1st-place solution to the ACCIDENT challenge at the CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop, which asks for zero-shot prediction of accident timing, impact centroid, and collision type from CCTV footage. On a frozen Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct checkpoint we build a three-stage pipeline (full-video joint prediction, time refinement, and single-frame grounding of the impact centroid), run the same pipeline a second time on a 235B Mixture-of-Experts sibling, blend the two outputs 9:1, and finally snap each predicted point onto the nearest vehicle detection. The final system reaches Public LB 0.55469 / Private LB 0.57080, roughly +0.21 over the strongest host baseline (Molmo-7B, 0.358) and wins the challenge. We ablate each component, report the negative results that shaped the final design, and release the code at https://github.com/fuumin621/cvpr2026-accident-1st-place-solution.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop中ACCIDENT挑战赛的第一名解决方案，该任务要求从CCTV监控画面中零样本预测事故时间、撞击中心点和碰撞类型。在冻结的Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct检查点基础上，我们构建了一个三级流水线（全视频联合预测、时间精细化、以及撞击中心点的单帧定位），然后在235B的混合专家模型上重复运行该流水线，将两个输出以9:1的比例进行融合，最后将每个预测点对齐到最近的车辆检测结果上。最终系统在公开榜上达到0.55469，私有榜上达到0.57080，相比最强的主办方基线Molmo-7B（0.358）提升约0.21，赢得了挑战赛。我们对每个组件进行了消融实验，报告了影响最终设计的负面结果，并在https://github.com/fuumin621/cvpr2026-accident-1st-place-solution公开了代码。

</details>


### [18] [EarthShift: a benchmark for measuring robustness to real-world distribution shifts in Earth observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29330)
*Kelsey Doerksen,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EarthShift是首个公开的遥感领域鲁棒性基准测试平台，用于评估模型在多种真实分布偏移（时间、地理、尺度、传感器）下的表现。实验发现8种地理空间基础模型在分布外场景下性能平均下降15-20%，且鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型及全监督模型相当，揭示当前模型缺乏分布鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有地球观测基准仅评估模型在分布内（in-distribution）的泛化能力，但实际部署时模型需应对大量分布外（out-of-distribution）场景（如新时间、新地理区域、新尺度、新传感器）。缺乏一个统一、公开的基准来系统评估遥感模型在多种真实分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建EarthShift测试平台：收集来自不同源、时间窗口、地理位置和传感器的配对数据集，通过对比模型在分布内和分布外的性能差异来衡量分布鲁棒性。在8种地理空间基础模型（GFMs）和11个任务上覆盖5种偏移类型进行系统实验。

Result: 1) 所有GFMs在分布外场景下性能平均下降15-20%，且该现象与模型架构、规模、预训练或微调策略无关。2) GFMs的鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型及全监督模型相似，没有明显优势。3) 当前模型在分布鲁棒性方面存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 未来研究应不仅追求性能提升，更应致力于改进模型的分布鲁棒性。EarthShift可作为标准测试平台，指导构建在真实应用中更鲁棒、更可靠的基础模型。

Original Abstract: Current Earth observation benchmarks focus on measuring performance on diverse tasks and applications, typically measuring generalization in-distribution. But when models are deployed, they must generalize to myriad out-of-distribution scenarios, such as new time periods, geographies, scales, and sensors. We introduce EarthShift: the first public testbed for benchmarking robustness across multiple realistic distribution shifts encountered in remote sensing. EarthShift enables users to measure distributional robustness by comparing performance in- and out-of-distribution using datasets from paired datasets from different sources, temporal windows, geographic locations, and sensors. Our experiments on 8 geospatial foundation models (GFMs) and 11 tasks covering 5 shift types show that GFMs consistently perform 15-20% worse out-of-distribution on average regardless of model architecture, size, pre-training or fine-tuning strategy. We show that GFM robustness is similar to that of generic vision foundation models, and even fully-supervised models. This highlights a need for future research to strive for improvements in distributional robustness, not just performance, which can be benchmarked using EarthShift. We release our code and datasets to provide a testbed to guide future work to create foundation models that are robust and reliable in real-world applications. Code and data for EarthShift are available at: https://earthshift.github.io

中文摘要翻译: 当前的地球观测基准主要关注模型在多样任务和应用上的性能表现，通常衡量的是分布内（in-distribution）的泛化能力。然而，当模型部署到实际场景中时，它们必须泛化到大量的分布外（out-of-distribution）情况，例如新的时间段、地理区域、尺度以及传感器。我们提出了EarthShift：首个用于衡量遥感领域中多种真实分布偏移下模型鲁棒性的公开测试平台。EarthShift通过使用来自不同数据源、时间窗口、地理位置和传感器的配对数据集，对比模型在分布内和分布外的性能差异，从而使用户能够衡量模型的分布鲁棒性。我们在8种地理空间基础模型（GFMs）和覆盖5种偏移类型的11个任务上进行的实验表明，无论模型架构、规模、预训练或微调策略如何，GFMs在分布外场景下的性能平均下降15-20%。我们发现，GFMs的鲁棒性与通用视觉基础模型甚至全监督模型相当。这凸显了未来研究不仅应追求性能提升，更应致力于改进分布鲁棒性的需求，而这可以通过使用EarthShift进行基准测试来实现。我们公开发布了代码和数据集，以提供一个测试平台，指导未来工作创建在真实应用中鲁棒且可靠的基础模型。EarthShift的代码和数据可在https://earthshift.github.io获取。

</details>


### [19] [Rethinking FID Through the Geometry of the Reference Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29335)
*Yunghee Lee,Byeonghyun Pak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现FID指标与图像生成质量之间的不一致性部分源于参考数据集的几何特性（密度和有效秩），并系统验证了该结论。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: FID广泛用于评估图像生成器，但较低的FID并不总是对应更好的样本质量，这种不匹配的原因尚不明确，需要探究其根本因素。

Method: 在六个数据集上进行对照研究，分析分布密度和有效秩如何解释FID随样本质量提升的变化；通过归因到精确率与召回率、以及使用替代特征空间和距离进行消融实验来支持结论。

Result: 分布密度和有效秩显著解释了FID随样本质量提升的变化。密集的数据集倾向于产生更有利的FID趋势，而更分散的数据集可能使FID在样本质量提升时反而恶化。

Conclusion: 分布度量指标应结合参考数据集的几何特性进行解读，以实现更可靠的基准评估。

Original Abstract: Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) is widely used to evaluate image generators, yet lower FID does not always correspond to better sample quality. We show that this mismatch depends in part on the geometry of the reference dataset. In a controlled study across six datasets, distributional density and effective rank significantly explain how FID changes as sample quality improves. Concentrated datasets tend to yield more favorable FID trends, whereas more dispersed datasets can make FID worsen despite better samples. Attribution to precision and recall and ablations with alternative feature spaces and distances support the same conclusion. These results suggest that distributional metrics should be interpreted together with the geometry of the reference dataset for more reliable benchmarking.

中文摘要翻译: Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) 被广泛用于评估图像生成器，然而较低的FID并不总是对应更好的样本质量。我们表明，这种不匹配部分取决于参考数据集的几何特性。在六个数据集上的对照研究中，分布密度和有效秩显著解释了FID随样本质量提升而变化的方式。密集的数据集往往产生更有利的FID趋势，而更分散的数据集可能使FID在样本质量提升时反而恶化。归因到精确率与召回率以及使用替代特征空间和距离的消融实验均支持相同的结论。这些结果表明，分布度量指标应结合参考数据集的几何特性进行解读，以实现更可靠的基准评估。

</details>


### [20] [DMC-CF: Dynamic Multimodal CounterFactual QA benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29339)
*Junzhe Zhang,Huixuan Zhang,Guirong Wang,Xingyao Zhang,Pei Liu,Lin Qu,Hu Wei,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有多模态因果推理数据集规模有限、基于非真实数据等问题，构建了基于真实视频的大规模反事实推理基准DMC-CF-Static，并提出动态图干预框架DGI构建动态评估基准DMC-CF-Dynamic，实验表明当前多模态大模型在真实场景中的因果推理能力仍有待提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态因果推理数据集存在规模有限、数据来源非真实（如合成图像/视频、卡通内容等）的局限，无法有效评估多模态大模型在真实世界场景下的因果关系理解能力。

Method: 收集真实世界视频构建大规模静态基准DMC-CF-Static；利用因果图表示因果事件，提出动态图干预（DGI）框架，从静态基准构建动态评估基准DMC-CF-Dynamic。

Result: 在包含静态与动态评估的DMC-CF基准上的实验结果表明，当前多模态大语言模型在真实场景中的多模态因果推理能力仍需大幅提升。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在真实世界多模态因果反事实推理任务上表现不足，需要进一步改进其因果理解能力。

Original Abstract: With the rapid advancement of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), models have demonstrated increasingly powerful multimodal capabilities. However, whether MLLMs trained through statistical learning can truly understand the causal relationships underlying the real world remains a key research question. In recent years, numerous multimodal causal reasoning datasets have been proposed. Nevertheless, these datasets are either limited in scale or constructed from synthetic images and videos, cartoon-based content, or other non-realistic multimodal sources. To address these limitations, we collect real-world videos and construct DMC-CF-Static, a large-scale benchmark for multimodal causal counterfactual reasoning. Furthermore, to mitigate issues such as data contamination in traditional static evaluation, we represent causal events using causal graphs and propose the Dynamic Graph Intervention (DGI) framework to build the dynamic evaluation benchmark DMC-CF-Dynamic from DMC-CF-Static. Experimental results on the overall DMC-CF, which includes both static and dynamic evaluation benchmarks, demonstrate that the multimodal causal reasoning capabilities of current multimodal large language models in real-world scenarios still require substantial improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的快速发展，模型已展现出日益强大的多模态能力。然而，通过统计学习训练的MLLMs是否能够真正理解现实世界背后的因果关系，仍是一个关键研究问题。近年来，大量多模态因果推理数据集被提出。然而，这些数据集要么规模有限，要么基于合成图像与视频、卡通内容或其他非真实多模态来源构建。为解决上述局限，我们收集真实世界视频，构建了大规模多模态因果反事实推理基准DMC-CF-Static。此外，为缓解传统静态评估中存在的数据污染等问题，我们使用因果图表示因果事件，并提出动态图干预（DGI）框架，从DMC-CF-Static构建动态评估基准DMC-CF-Dynamic。在整体DMC-CF（包含静态与动态评估基准）上的实验结果表明，当前多模态大语言模型在真实场景下的多模态因果推理能力仍需大幅提升。

</details>


### [21] [Orthogonal Negative Guidance in Attention Feature Space for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29390)
*Jungmin Ko,Jungwon Park,Jimyeong Kim,Changin Choi,Wonseok Lee,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于注意力特征空间的正交负向引导方法，无需训练即可在MM-DiT架构的T2I模型中实现精准概念抑制，在FLUX模型上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法（如提示词否定、后处理编辑、负向引导）在显式概念抑制任务中效果不足，要么无法彻底移除目标概念，要么降低了整体图像质量。

Method: 在MM-DiT基T2I变压器的注意力输出空间中，将负向提示的注意力特征相对于正向提示特征进行正交化，仅减去正交分量，从而在抑制不需要概念的同时保留期望语义。

Result: 在FLUX-dev和FLUX-schnell上的实验表明，该方法在概念抑制、提示对齐和图像质量之间取得了良好平衡；人工评估中比第二名基线高出18.78%；支持多概念抑制和可调节概念抑制。

Conclusion: 提出的正交负向引导方法是一种有效的训练免费概念抑制方法，在多个维度上均优于现有基线，且具有灵活性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have become increasingly capable of generating high-quality images. Yet, enforcing the explicit absence of a specified object or attribute remains a fundamentally challenging problem. Existing approaches, including prompt negation, post-hoc editing, and negative guidance, remain insufficient for explicit concept suppression, often failing to remove the target concept or degrading overall image quality. To this end, we propose Orthogonal Negative Guidance in attention feature space, a training-free method that operates in the attention output space of MM-DiT-based T2I transformers. Our method orthogonalizes negative-prompt attention features with respect to positive-prompt features and subtracts only the orthogonal component, suppressing unwanted concepts while preserving desired semantics. Experiments on FLUX-dev and FLUX-schnell show that our method achieves favorable trade-offs between concept suppression, prompt alignment, and image quality. In human evaluation, our method outperforms the second-best baseline by 18.78%. We further show that our method supports multi-concept suppression and adjustable concept suppression.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像（T2I）模型在生成高质量图像方面已日益强大。然而，强制性地显式去除指定对象或属性仍然是一个根本性的难题。现有方法，包括提示词否定、后处理编辑和负向引导，在显式概念抑制方面仍显不足，往往无法移除目标概念或导致整体图像质量下降。为此，我们提出了注意力特征空间中的正交负向引导方法，这是一种基于MM-DiT架构T2I变压器的无训练方法，作用于注意力输出空间。该方法将负向提示的注意力特征相对于正向提示的特征进行正交化，并仅减去正交分量，从而在抑制不想要的语义概念的同时保留期望语义。在FLUX-dev和FLUX-schnell上的实验表明，我们的方法在概念抑制、提示对齐和图像质量之间取得了有利的权衡。在人工评估中，我们的方法比第二名基线高出18.78%。我们进一步展示了该方法支持多概念抑制和可调节的概念抑制。

</details>


### [22] [Semantic and Visual Evidence for Efficient Long-Video Reasoning: A Solution for the HD-EPIC VQA Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29402)
*Yinsong Xu,Wei Jing,Liuxin Zhang,Wanjun Lv,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为语义证据和视觉证据，通过查询条件检索和动态集成两种证据，有效提升了多模态大模型在长视频理解上的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在处理长距离第一人称视频时，受限于上下文长度和对细粒度视觉细节的接地能力不足，导致在HD-EPIC等视频问答基准上表现不佳。

Method: 提出统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为两种互补证据：语义证据（通过粗到细的提取管道捕获全局过程结构）和以对象为中心的视觉证据（通过边界框和视觉嵌入保留细粒度接地）。推理时，将推理形式化为查询条件下的证据检索与集成过程，动态选择两种来源的相关信息。

Result: 在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战赛中，该方法在多个任务类别上取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 明确结构化、检索和集成语义证据与视觉证据对于多模态大模型有效理解长视频至关重要。

Original Abstract: Understanding long-form egocentric videos remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) due to limited context length and insufficient grounding of fine-grained visual details. The recently proposed HD-EPIC benchmark highlights these limitations: even strong long-context models achieve relatively low performance across diverse video question answering tasks. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that decouples long-video reasoning into two complementary forms of evidence: semantic evidence and visual evidence. Semantic evidence captures global procedural structure through a coarse-to-fine extraction pipeline, while object-centric visual evidence preserves fine-grained grounding through bounding boxes and visual embeddings. During inference, we formulate reasoning as a query-conditioned evidence retrieval and integration process, dynamically selecting relevant information from both sources. Our approach achieves competitive performance in the HD-EPIC-VQA Challenge across multiple task categories. More broadly, our results demonstrate that explicitly structuring, retrieving, and integrating semantic and visual evidence is critical for effective long-video understanding with MLLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 理解长距离第一人称视频对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）仍然具有挑战性，原因是有限的上下文长度和对细粒度视觉细节的接地不足。最近提出的HD-EPIC基准凸显了这些局限性：即使是强大的长上下文模型，在多种视频问答任务中的表现也相对较低。在本文中，我们提出了一种统一框架，将长视频推理解耦为两种互补的证据形式：语义证据和视觉证据。语义证据通过一种粗到细的提取管道捕获全局过程结构，而以对象为中心的视觉证据通过边界框和视觉嵌入保留细粒度的接地。在推理阶段，我们将推理形式化为查询条件驱动的证据检索与集成过程，动态地从两种来源中选择相关信息。我们的方法在HD-EPIC-VQA挑战赛的多个任务类别中取得了具有竞争力的性能。更广泛地说，我们的结果证明，明确地结构化、检索和集成语义证据与视觉证据，对于多模态大语言模型有效理解长视频至关重要。

</details>


### [23] [One Click per Cell Type Suffices: Training-free Group Interaction for Cell Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29429)
*Sanghyun Jo,Seo Jin Lee,Seohyung Hong,Yoorim Gang,Hyeongsub Kim,Hyungseok Seo,Kyungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Group Prompting新范式，将交互式分割从每实例O(N)变为每类型O(T)，并设计了无需训练的Chain-of-Prompts (CoP)框架，利用SAM图像编码器对同类型细胞的聚类特性，通过递归扩展单次用户点击实现全类型分割，在多个基准上性能接近甚至超越全监督方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有细胞实例分割模型在跨分布细胞类型上性能严重下降，而交互式基础模型虽能解决此问题但需逐实例标注，成本极高（组织病理学图像包含成百上千个密集实例）。为将交互成本从每实例O(N)降至每类型O(T)（即每种细胞类型只需一次点击即可分割所有同类实例）。

Method: 核心方法Chain-of-Prompts (CoP)：1) 利用SAM冻结图像编码器天然将同类型细胞在特征空间中聚类的特性；2) 通过非参数门控机制融合多尺度编码器特征，识别可靠的同类型位置；3) 选择空间距离最远的可靠点作为下一个提示，以最大化覆盖范围，递归扩展单次用户点击。

Result: 在3个细胞类型标注基准上，每类型一次点击的CoP保留了超过90%的逐实例分割性能，且无需任何额外训练即超越全监督方法；在4个形态同质基准上，单次点击保留超过99%的性能。

Conclusion: Group Prompting范式有效解决了交互式分割在密集细胞图像中的成本瓶颈，证明了利用基础模型隐式聚类特性可实现高效的按类型分割，且CoP作为无需训练的框架具有极强的实用性和泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Cell instance segmentation models trained on cell-specific datasets suffer severe performance drops on out-of-distribution cell types, while interactive foundation models overcome this through per-instance prompting at a cost that is prohibitively expensive for histopathology images containing hundreds to thousands of densely packed instances. We introduce Group Prompting, a new paradigm that shifts interactive segmentation from per-instance $O(N)$ to per-type $O(T)$, where a single click per cell type suffices to segment all instances of that type. Our key observation is that the frozen image encoder of the Segment Anything Model (SAM) already clusters same-type cells in its feature space before any prompt is given. Exploiting this property, we propose Chain-of-Prompts (CoP), a training-free framework that recursively expands a single user click by (1) identifying reliable same-type locations through non-parametric gating of multi-scale encoder features, and (2) selecting the most spatially distant reliable point as the next prompt to maximize coverage. On three cell-type-annotated benchmarks, CoP with one click per type retains over 90% of per-instance performance and surpasses fully-supervised methods without any additional training. On four morphologically homogeneous benchmarks, a single click retains over 99%. Project Page: https://shjo-april.github.io/Chain-of-Prompts/

中文摘要翻译: 针对特定细胞类型训练的细胞实例分割模型在跨分布细胞类型上性能严重下降，而交互式基础模型虽能通过逐实例提示克服此问题，但对于包含成百上千个密集实例的组织病理学图像而言，其成本高得难以承受。本文提出群组提示（Group Prompting）这一新范式，将交互式分割从每实例O(N)转变为每类型O(T)——每种细胞类型只需一次点击即可分割该类型的所有实例。我们的关键发现是：分割一切模型（Segment Anything Model, SAM）的冻结图像编码器在给出任何提示之前，已在其特征空间中将同类型细胞聚类。利用这一特性，我们提出提示链（Chain-of-Prompts, CoP），一个无需训练的框架，通过以下方式递归扩展单次用户点击：(1) 通过多尺度编码器特征的非参数门控识别可靠的同类型位置，(2) 选择空间距离最远的可靠点作为下一个提示以最大化覆盖范围。在三个细胞类型标注基准上，每类型一次点击的CoP保留了超过90%的逐实例分割性能，且无需任何额外训练即超越全监督方法。在四个形态同质基准上，单次点击保留超过99%的性能。项目页面：https://shjo-april.github.io/Chain-of-Prompts/

</details>


### [24] [Recovering Policy-Induced Errors: Benchmarking and Trajectory Synthesis for Robust GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29447)
*Tianpeng Bu,Xin Liu,Qihua Chen,Hao Jiang,Shurui Li,Hongtao Duan,Lu Jiang,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GUI-RobustEval评估基准和RoTS数据合成框架，通过树状结构主动发现错误模式并合成恢复步骤，训练出RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B模型，在GUI鲁棒性和整体性能上均取得显著提升，其中RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到47.4%的成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GUI智能体在执行任务时缺乏从自身错误中恢复的能力，严重阻碍了实际部署。目前缺乏系统性地评估和提升GUI智能体错误恢复能力的基准与数据。

Method: 提出RoTS（Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis），一种可扩展的合成框架，通过基于树的流水线主动发现多样化的错误模式，并合成对应的恢复步骤，生成80万条高质量训练数据。

Result: RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到47.4%的成功率和33.8%的All-Pass@4分数，在GUI-RobustEval和传统GUI基准上均取得显著提升，达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 改进的长程错误恢复能力不仅能增强GUI智能体的鲁棒性，也能提升其整体任务完成性能，证明了错误恢复能力与整体性能之间的正相关性。

Original Abstract: While GUI agents have advanced rapidly, they often lack the robustness to recover from their own errors, hindering real-world deployment. To bridge this gap at both the evaluation and data levels, we introduce GUI-RobustEval and propose Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis. GUI-RobustEval contains $1,216$ executable test cases that systematically measure error recovery capabilities across a broad and realistic spectrum of error modes. At the data level, RoTS is a scalable synthesis framework that creates $800k$ high-quality data via a tree-based pipeline that proactively discovers diverse error modes and synthesizes corresponding recovery steps. Our two models, RoTS-7B and RoTS-32B, fine-tuned on our dataset, both demonstrate significant gains on GUI-RobustEval and traditional GUI benchmarks. Notably, RoTS-32B achieves state-of-the-art performance on OSWorld, with a $47.4\%$ success rate and a $33.8\%$ All-Pass@4 score, suggesting that improved long-horizon error recovery ability contributes to both robustness and overall performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/RoTS.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管GUI智能体取得了快速发展，但它们通常缺乏从自身错误中恢复的鲁棒性，这阻碍了实际部署。为了在评估和数据两个层面弥合这一差距，我们引入了GUI-RobustEval并提出了鲁棒性驱动的轨迹合成（Robustness-driven Trajectory Synthesis, RoTS）。GUI-RobustEval包含1,216个可执行测试用例，能够系统性地衡量在广泛且真实的错误模式范围内的错误恢复能力。在数据层面，RoTS是一个可扩展的合成框架，通过基于树的流水线主动发现多样化的错误模式并合成相应的恢复步骤，创建了80万条高质量数据。我们在该数据集上微调的两个模型RoTS-7B和RoTS-32B，在GUI-RobustEval和传统GUI基准上都表现出显著的性能提升。值得注意的是，RoTS-32B在OSWorld上达到了当前最优性能，成功率为47.4%，All-Pass@4分数为33.8%，这表明改进的长程错误恢复能力有助于提升鲁棒性和整体性能。我们的代码开源在https://github.com/AlibabaResearch/RoTS。

</details>


### [25] [Comparative evaluation of photogrammetric reconstruction methods and 3D Gaussian Splatting for road surface roughness analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29452)
*Marouane Elmegdar,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种基于图像的3D重建管线（COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape、3DGS），评估其利用智能手机图像估计路面粗糙度的能力。结果表明，COLMAP对微观纹理最敏感，Meshroom重建均衡，Metashape几何最平滑，3DGS能捕捉不规则但噪声较大。开源管线在相对粗糙度评估中表现可行，适用于低成本路面监测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于传感器的路面评估技术成本高昂，基于图像的3D重建提供了一种低成本替代方案，但不同重建管线在路面粗糙度估计上的性能差异尚不清楚，亟需系统比较。

Method: 使用智能手机采集路面图像，分别通过COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3DGS四种管线进行三维重建，生成点云。所有点云在CloudCompare中采用统一流程处理：包括方向对齐、分割、法线估计以及分别在0.2、0.4、0.6模型单位邻域半径下计算粗糙度。

Result: COLMAP对微观纹理敏感性最高；Meshroom重建结果均衡，粗糙度变化适中；Metashape因内部滤波产生最平滑的几何结构；3DGS能捕捉可见的不规则特征，但噪声较高且点云密度较低。

Conclusion: 开源重建管线（如COLMAP、Meshroom）可用于路面相对粗糙度评估，为低成本的 pavement 监测提供了一种实用方法。

Original Abstract: Image-based 3D reconstruction offers a low-cost alternative to traditional sensor-based techniques for road surface assessment. This study compares four reconstruction pipelines--COLMAP, Meshroom, Metashape, and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)--to evaluate their ability to estimate road surface roughness from smartphone imagery. All point clouds were processed in CloudCompare using a consistent workflow involving orientation alignment, segmentation, normal estimation, and roughness computation at neighborhood radiuses of 0.2, 0.4, and 0.6 model units. The results show that COLMAP provides the highest sensitivity to micro-texture, while Meshroom yields balanced reconstructions with moderate roughness variation. Metashape produces the smoothest geometry due to its internal filtering, and 3DGS captures visible irregularities but exhibits higher noise and lower density. The comparison demonstrates that open-source pipelines are viable for relative roughness evaluation, offering a practical approach for low-cost pavement monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 基于图像的三维重建为传统的基于传感器的路面评估技术提供了一种低成本的替代方案。本研究比较了四种重建管线——COLMAP、Meshroom、Metashape和3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS），以评估它们从智能手机图像中估计路面粗糙度的能力。所有点云均在CloudCompare中使用统一的流程进行处理，包括方向对齐、分割、法线估计以及在0.2、0.4和0.6模型单位邻域半径下的粗糙度计算。结果表明，COLMAP对微观纹理的敏感性最高，而Meshroom能够生成具有适中粗糙度变化的均衡重建。Metashape由于其内部滤波机制，生成的几何结构最为平滑。3DGS能够捕捉可见的不规则特征，但噪声较高且密度较低。该比较表明，开源管线在相对粗糙度评估中是可行的，为低成本的路面监测提供了一种实用方法。

</details>


### [26] [FlowSeg: Dynamic Semantic Guidance for LLM-Conditioned Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29461)
*Zekang Zhang,Guangyu Gao,Youyun Tang,ChengJing Wu,Xiaochao Qu,Chi Harold Liu,Jianbo Jiao,Yunchao Wei,Luoqi Liu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSeg 提出双向语义流机制，让语言条件在迭代掩码生成过程中动态参与引导，解决现有分割方法中语言条件与视觉掩码语义错位的问题，在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 propose-then-select 分割管线中，虽然能生成高质量掩码候选，但最终预测常与给定语言条件不匹配，原因是语言语义仅作为静态提示或事后匹配信号，未参与迭代掩码生成过程。大量错误源于语义错位而非掩码质量差。

Method: FlowSeg 引入双向语义流机制：在生成过程中，中间解码状态与 LLM 导出的条件嵌入之间进行双向语义交互。语言条件主动引导每一阶段的掩码细化，同时条件嵌入由不断出现的视觉证据逐步更新。此外，引入轻量级边界感知细化模块，选择性增强不确定区域而不扰动置信区域。

Result: 在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上的大量实验表明，FlowSeg 持续改善了语言-掩码对齐度，并取得了最优性能。

Conclusion: FlowSeg 通过让语言条件动态参与掩码生成过程，有效解决了语义错位问题，实现了语义更加一致的掩码表示和视觉对齐的语言条件，从而获得更可靠的匹配与最优性能。

Original Abstract: LLM-conditioned segmentation has recently advanced rapidly by coupling large language models with iterative mask generation frameworks. However, we identify a persistent failure mode in current propose-then-select pipelines. Although high-quality mask candidates are often generated, the final prediction may fail to match the given linguistic condition. This failure arises because language semantics are typically used as static prompts or post-hoc matching signals, rather than participating in the iterative mask generation process. Through systematic analysis, we show that many errors stem from semantic misalignment rather than poor mask quality. To address this issue, we propose FlowSeg, which introduces dynamic semantic guidance via a bidirectional semantic flow between intermediate decoding states and LLM-derived condition embeddings throughout the generation process. Language conditions actively guide mask refinement at each stage, while condition embeddings are progressively updated by emerging visual evidence. This design yields semantically grounded mask representations and visually aligned language conditions, enabling more reliable matching. We further incorporate a lightweight boundary-aware refinement to selectively enhance uncertain regions without perturbing confident interiors. Extensive experiments on referring expression segmentation and reasoning segmentation tasks demonstrate that FlowSeg consistently improves language-mask alignment and achieves state-of-the-art performance. Project page: https://zkzhang98.github.io/FlowSeg_page

中文摘要翻译: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的分割技术近来通过将大语言模型与迭代掩码生成框架结合取得了快速进展。然而，我们发现在当前的"先提议后选择"（propose-then-select）管线中存在一个持久的失败模式：虽然高质量掩码候选常被生成，但最终预测可能与给定的语言条件不匹配。这一失败源于语言语义通常仅作为静态提示或事后匹配信号，而没有参与迭代掩码生成过程。通过系统分析，我们表明许多错误源于语义错位而非掩码质量差。为应对此问题，我们提出 FlowSeg，它在整个生成过程中通过中间解码状态与 LLM 导出的条件嵌入之间的双向语义流引入动态语义引导。语言条件主动引导每一阶段的掩码细化，同时条件嵌入由不断出现的视觉证据逐步更新。这一设计产生了语义一致的掩码表示和视觉对齐的语言条件，从而实现更可靠的匹配。我们进一步引入轻量级边界感知细化模块，选择性增强不确定区域而不扰动置信区域。在指代表达分割和推理分割任务上的大量实验表明，FlowSeg 持续改善了语言-掩码对齐度，并取得了最优性能。项目页面：https://zkzhang98.github.io/FlowSeg_page

</details>


### [27] [Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on CFMME: A Comprehensive Chinese Financial Multimodal Evaluation Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29462)
*Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CFMMME，一个中文金融多模态评估基准，包含6052个实例、8种金融图像模态和4种核心多模态任务，用于评估LVLM在金融业务全流程中的能力。当前最强模型在问答任务上准确率仅66.11%，在检测/识别/信息抽取任务上平均得分77.18，表明LVLM在金融领域仍有较大提升空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LVLM评估缺乏针对中文金融领域全业务流程的综合多模态基准，无法系统评估模型在感知、理解、推理和认知等方面的能力。

Method: 构建了CFMME基准数据集，包含6052个实例，覆盖8种主要金融图像模态（如票据、图表、证件等）和4类核心多模态任务（问答、检测、识别、信息抽取），对代表性LVLM进行全面评估。

Result: 最优模型在问答任务上总体准确率为66.11%，在检测、识别和信息抽取任务上平均得分为77.18。此外还进行了错误原因分析、跨模态能力分析和多方向设置分析。

Conclusion: 当前LVLM在中文金融多模态任务上表现仍有较大提升空间，CFMME基准可为未来研究提供有价值的指导方向。

Original Abstract: The emergence of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) has substantially expanded model capabilities beyond text-only understanding, enabling unified inference across both visual and textual modalities and supporting a broader range of real-world applications. To comprehensively evaluate the perception, understanding, reasoning, and cognition capabilities of LVLMs throughout the entire financial business workflow in Chinese contexts, we introduce CFMME, a novel Chinese financial multimodal evaluation benchmark. CFMME comprises 6,052 instances spanning from fundamental academic knowledge to complex real-world applications, covering eight primary financial image modalities and four core multimodal tasks. On CFMME, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative LVLMs. The results show that the state-of-the-art model attains an overall accuracy of 66.11\% on the question answering task and an average score of 77.18 on the detection, recognition, and information extraction tasks, indicating substantial room for improvement in current LVLMs. In addition, we conduct detailed analyses of error causes, cross-modal capabilities, and multi-orientation settings, yielding valuable insights for future research. We hope that CFMME will spur further progress in LVLMs, especially by improving their performance on multiple multimodal tasks in the financial domain.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型(LVLM)的出现极大地扩展了模型的能力，使其超越了纯文本理解，能够实现跨视觉和文本模态的统一推理，并支持更广泛的真实世界应用。为了全面评估LVLM在中文环境下整个金融业务流程中的感知、理解、推理和认知能力，我们提出了CFMME，一个新颖的中文金融多模态评估基准。CFMME包含6052个实例，涵盖从基础学术知识到复杂现实应用的广泛范围，覆盖八种主要金融图像模态和四类核心多模态任务。在CFMME上，我们对代表性LVLM进行了全面评估。结果表明，当前最先进的模型在问答任务上的总体准确率为66.11%，在检测、识别和信息抽取任务上的平均得分为77.18，表明当前LVLM仍有较大的改进空间。此外，我们还对错误原因、跨模态能力和多方向设置进行了详细分析，为未来研究提供了有价值的见解。我们希望CFMME能够推动LVLM的进一步发展，特别是在金融领域中提升其在多个多模态任务上的表现。

</details>


### [28] [AnyMo: Scaling Any-Modality Conditional Motion Generation with Masked Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29488)
*Yiheng Li,Zhuo Li,Ruibing Hou,Yingjie Chen,Hong Chang,Hao Liu,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniHuMo大规模多模态对齐运动数据集（5000+小时/320万序列）和AnyMo统一框架，通过残差FSQ运动分词器与可扩展掩码建模Transformer，实现任意模态组合下的高质量人体运动生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有条件人体运动生成方法受限于固定的模态配置和任务特定的架构，跨模态交互及多模态条件合成的扩展规律尚未充分探索，且缺乏大规模、模态对齐的运动数据。

Method: 构建OmniHuMo大规模多模态对齐数据集；提出AnyMo统一框架，包含残差FSQ（残差有限标量量化）运动分词器和可扩展的掩码建模Transformer，支持任意模态组合下的高质量运动合成。

Result: 实验表明AnyMo在实现高保真合成的同时，能够对空间属性和风格属性提供灵活的控制。

Conclusion: 通过大规模多模态对齐数据和统一框架，有效解决了跨模态运动合成的数据稀缺和架构限制问题，验证了多模态条件合成中的扩展规律。

Original Abstract: Conditional human motion generation remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision and robotics. Despite significant progress, current methods are often constrained by fixed modality configurations and task-specific architectures, leaving cross-modal interactions and the scaling laws of multimodal-conditioned synthesis largely underexplored. A key bottleneck is the scarcity of large-scale modality-aligned motion data, limiting generalization across diverse control signals. In this work, we introduce OmniHuMo, a large-scale, high-quality dataset comprising over 5,000 hours of motion and 3.2 million sequences with precisely aligned multimodal annotations (e.g., text, speech, music, and trajectory). Leveraging OmniHuMo, we propose AnyMo, a unified multimodal framework combining a Residual FSQ-based motion tokenizer with a scalable masked modeling transformer, enabling high-quality motion synthesis under arbitrary modality combinations. Extensive experiments show that AnyMo achieves high-fidelity synthesis while offering flexible control over both spatial and stylistic attributes.

中文摘要翻译: 条件人体运动生成仍然是计算机视觉和机器人学中的一个基本挑战。尽管已取得显著进展，当前方法往往受限于固定的模态配置和任务特定的架构，跨模态交互及多模态条件合成的扩展规律在很大程度上仍未得到探索。一个关键瓶颈在于缺乏大规模、模态对齐的运动数据，这限制了对多样化控制信号的泛化能力。本文中，我们提出了OmniHuMo——一个大规模、高质量的数据集，包含超过5,000小时的运动数据和320万个序列，并配有精确对齐的多模态标注（如文本、语音、音乐和轨迹）。利用OmniHuMo，我们提出了AnyMo，一个统一的 multimodal 框架，结合了基于残差FSQ的运动分词器与可扩展的掩码建模Transformer，能够在任意模态组合下实现高质量的运动合成。大量实验表明，AnyMo在实现高保真合成的同时，提供了对空间属性和风格属性的灵活控制。

</details>


### [29] [KGEdit: Ambiguity-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Training-Free Precise Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29509)
*Mingshu Cai,Miao Zhang,Chenghe Yang,Yixuan Li,Osamu Yoshie,Yuya Ieiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KGEdit提出了一种基于结构化语义控制的训练-free文本到视频生成框架，通过构建歧义感知知识图谱、结构化语义注入模块和时序感知语义控制模块，解决了复杂文本指令下的语义模糊、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有训练-free视频生成方法在处理复杂文本指令时存在语义歧义、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致等问题，亟需一种更精细的语义控制机制。

Method: 1) 构建歧义感知知识图谱(AAKG)对输入提示进行解耦和消歧，转化为身份、关系、属性、负约束四种结构化语义；2) 设计结构化语义注入模块(SSIM)将语义信号注入扩散Transformer的关键层；3) 引入时序感知语义控制(TASC)模块，根据去噪过程的阶段特征动态调度语义目标。

Result: KGEdit在编辑精度和时间稳定性上优于现有方法，且在文本驱动交互场景中具有更高的效率和可控性。

Conclusion: KGEdit通过结构化语义控制有效提升了视频生成中的语义对齐和时序一致性，为实现更精确、可控的文本到视频生成提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: In recent years, training-free video generation has progressed remarkably. However, when handling complex textual instructions, existing methods still suffer from semantic ambiguity, incorrect concept binding, and cross-frame inconsistency. To address these issues, we propose KGEdit, a structured semantic control framework for text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models. Specifically, we first construct an ambiguity-aware knowledge graph (AAKG) to disentangle and disambiguate the input prompt, converting it into four types of structured semantics: identity, relation, attribute, and negative constraints. We then design a structured semantic injection module (SSIM) to inject these semantic signals into key layers of the diffusion Transformer, enabling fine-grained semantic control. In addition, we introduce a temporal-aware semantic control (TASC) module that dynamically schedules semantic objectives according to the stage-wise characteristics of the denoising process, further improving semantic alignment and temporal consistency. Experiments show that KGEdit outperforms existing methods in editing precision and temporal stability, while offering higher efficiency and controllability in text-driven interaction scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，免训练视频生成取得了显著进展。然而，在处理复杂文本指令时，现有方法仍存在语义模糊、概念绑定错误和跨帧不一致等问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出了KGEdit——一种面向文本到视频扩散模型的结构化语义控制框架。具体而言，我们首先构建一个歧义感知知识图谱，对输入提示进行解耦和消歧，将其转化为四种结构化语义：身份、关系、属性和负约束。然后，我们设计了一个结构化语义注入模块，将这些语义信号注入扩散Transformer的关键层，实现细粒度的语义控制。此外，我们还引入了一个时序感知语义控制模块，该模块根据去噪过程的阶段特征动态调度语义目标，进一步提升了语义对齐和时序一致性。实验表明，KGEdit在编辑精度和时间稳定性方面优于现有方法，同时在文本驱动交互场景中提供了更高的效率和可控性。

</details>


### [30] [GiPL: Generative augmented iterative Pseudo-Labeling for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29539)
*Jiacong Liu,Shu Luo,Yikai Qin,Yaze Zhao,Yongwei Jiang,Yixiong Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GiPL提出了一种高效的双分支训练框架，通过迭代伪标签自训练和基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强，解决了跨域少样本目标检测中支持集利用不足和过拟合问题，在多个基准数据集上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言基础模型在跨域少样本目标检测中展现了良好的零样本泛化能力，但在微调时面临两个关键挑战：一是稀疏的单实例标注导致支持集利用不充分，二是极有限的目标域样本导致严重过拟合。

Method: 提出了GiPL双分支训练框架。第一分支设计迭代伪标签自训练范式：在支持集上进行零样本推理生成可靠伪标注，与真实标签融合后迭代优化模型。第二分支引入基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强流程，合成域对齐、多目标标注的图像以丰富训练样本并抑制过拟合。

Result: 在RUOD、CARPK、CarDD三个跨域少样本目标检测数据集上，在1/5/10-shot设置下，GiPL均一致性地显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的GiPL框架通过双分支设计有效解决了支持集利用不足和过拟合两大问题，显著提升了跨域少样本目标检测的性能。

Original Abstract: Vision-language foundation models have shown promising zero-shot generalization for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection (CD-FSOD). However, they face two critical challenges in fine-tuning: insufficient support set utilization due to sparse single-instance annotations, and severe overfitting under extremely limited target-domain samples. To address these issues, this paper proposes GiPL, an efficient two-branch training framework.In the first branch, we design an iterative pseudo-label self-training paradigm, which performs zero-shot inference on the support set to generate reliable pseudo-annotations, fuses them with ground-truth labels, and iteratively optimizes the model to fully exploit support set data. In the second branch, we introduce generative data augmentation pipeline using large vision-language models, which synthesizes domain-aligned, multi-object annotated images to enrich training samples and suppress overfitting. Extensive experiments on three challenging CD-FSOD datasets (RUOD, CARPK, CarDD) under 1/5/10-shot settings demonstrate that GiPL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods with significant performance gains.Code is available at \href{https://github.com/z-yaz/CDiscover}{CDiscover}.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言基础模型在跨域少样本目标检测中展现了有前景的零样本泛化能力。然而，它们在微调过程中面临两个关键挑战：一是稀疏的单实例标注导致支持集利用不充分，二是在极有限的目标域样本下出现严重过拟合。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了GiPL，一种高效的双分支训练框架。在第一分支中，我们设计了一种迭代伪标签自训练范式，在支持集上进行零样本推理以生成可靠的伪标注，将其与真实标签融合，并通过迭代优化模型以充分利用支持集数据。在第二分支中，我们引入了基于大视觉语言模型的生成式数据增强流程，合成域对齐、多目标标注的图像以丰富训练样本并抑制过拟合。在三个具有挑战性的跨域少样本目标检测数据集（RUOD、CARPK、CarDD）上，在1/5/10-shot设置下的大量实验表明，GiPL一致性地优于现有最先进方法，且性能提升显著。代码可在\href{https://github.com/z-yaz/CDiscover}{CDiscover}获取。

</details>


### [31] [Learning Representations from 3D Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29549)
*Julia Farganus,Krzysztof Żurawicki,Arkadiusz Gaweł,Weronika Jakubowska,Halina Kwaśnicka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）表示的3D场景分类任务进行了多种几何深度学习架构的对比评估，包括基于点和基于图的模型，通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析，揭示了不同架构和输入特征配置对学习有效3DGS表示的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3DGS主要用于视图合成，其在场景理解任务（如分类）中的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统评估不同几何深度学习架构在3DGS表示下的场景分类能力。

Method: 作者对多种几何深度学习架构（包括基于点和基于图的模型）进行了对比实验。场景通过3DGS表示为潜在特征，并通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析三种方式评估表示质量，同时在传统点云数据集和专用3DGS数据集上进行基准测试。

Result: 不同架构族之间在分类性能上存在一致的差异；高斯泼溅特有的属性（如协方差、不透明度等）对表示质量有显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究为选择适用于3DGS表示的场景理解架构提供了指导，强调了高斯特有属性在表示学习中的重要性，并指出了不同几何感知架构的适用性差异。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a recent approach for scene rendering. Although primarily designed for view synthesis, its potential for scene understanding tasks remains underexplored. In this work, we conduct a comparative evaluation of various geometric deep learning architectures for the classification of 3D scenes represented using Gaussian Splatting. We benchmark point-based and graph-based models across both traditional point cloud datasets and dedicated Gaussian Splatting datasets. Scenes are embedded into latent representations, which are evaluated through end-to-end classification, linear probing, and clustering analysis. Our study provides insight into the suitability of different geometry-aware architectures and input feature configurations for learning effective 3D Gaussian Splat representations. The results highlight consistent differences between architectural families and reveal the impact of Gaussian-specific attributes on the quality of representation.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）是一种新兴的场景渲染方法。尽管其主要用于视图合成，但在场景理解任务中的潜力尚未被充分探索。本文对基于高斯泼溅表示的3D场景分类任务进行了多种几何深度学习架构的对比评估。我们在传统点云数据集和专用高斯泼溅数据集上，对基于点和基于图的模型进行了基准测试。场景被嵌入为潜在表示，并通过端到端分类、线性探测和聚类分析进行评估。本研究揭示了不同几何感知架构和输入特征配置在学习有效3D高斯泼溅表示方面的适用性。结果强调了不同架构族之间的一致性差异，并揭示了高斯特有属性对表示质量的影响。

</details>


### [32] [TAE: Target-aware enhancer for nighttime UAV tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29558)
*Yanyan Chen,Ruigang Fu,Yu Song,Ping Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向无人机夜间目标跟踪的感知低光增强框架TAE，并发布了包含268个序列的DarkSOT基准数据集，在低光场景下显著提升了跟踪性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 夜间低光条件下的严重图像退化是制约无人机单目标跟踪全天候应用的核心瓶颈，现有图像增强方法难以区分目标和背景区域，容易放大背景噪声或损害目标特征。

Method: 提出TAE框架：1) 利用跟踪边界框的弱监督信号进行区域感知增强，使操作聚焦于目标区域；2) 采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制，实现不同区域的精细建模和自适应调整。

Result: 在DarkSOT和UAVDark135数据集上，TAE显著提升了低光夜间场景下的跟踪性能，展现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: TAE框架通过目标感知的低光增强有效解决了夜间无人机跟踪中的图像退化问题，DarkSOT基准为相关研究提供了新的评测平台。

Original Abstract: Severe image degradation under low-light nighttime conditions constitutes a core bottleneck preventing all-day applications for UAV-based single object tracking. Existing image enhancement methods often struggle to distinguish between target and background regions, which can easily lead to amplified background noise or compromise target features. To overcome this limitation, we propose TAE, a target-aware low-light enhancement framework tailored for nighttime object tracking. Guided explicitly by weak supervisory signals from tracking bounding boxes, the framework performs region-aware enhancement to ensure operations focus on the target area. It further adopts an adaptive RGB multi-curve fusion mechanism to achieve refined modeling and adaptive adjustment across different regions. To facilitate research in this domain, we also contribute DarkSOT, a new benchmark for nighttime UAV tracking, comprising 268 sequences across 9 target categories. Experimental results on the DarkSOT and UAVDark135 demonstrate that TAE significantly improves tracking performance in low-light nighttime scenarios, exhibiting strong robustness and generalization. The DarkSOT dataset is available at https://github.com/Fu0511/DarkSOT-Dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 低光夜间条件下严重的图像退化是制约无人机单目标跟踪全天候应用的核心瓶颈。现有的图像增强方法往往难以区分目标和背景区域，容易导致背景噪声放大或目标特征受损。为克服这一局限，我们提出TAE——一个面向夜间目标跟踪的感知低光增强框架。该框架在跟踪边界框弱监督信号的显式引导下，执行区域感知增强以确保操作聚焦于目标区域，并进一步采用自适应RGB多曲线融合机制，实现不同区域的精细建模与自适应调整。为促进该领域研究，我们还贡献了DarkSOT——一个新的夜间无人机跟踪基准，包含9个目标类别共268个序列。在DarkSOT和UAVDark135上的实验结果表明，TAE显著提升了低光夜间场景下的跟踪性能，展现出强鲁棒性和泛化能力。DarkSOT数据集可在https://github.com/Fu0511/DarkSOT-Dataset获取。

</details>
