Based on this insight, we propose EarlyTom, a training-free token compression framework that performs early-stage visual token compression inside the vision encoder, enabling significantly better TTFT reduction and higher throughput. In addition, we introduce a decoupled spatial token selection strategy that improves the overall compression effectiveness. EarlyTom reduces TTFT by up to 2.65x and FLOPs by up to 61% on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU for the LLaVA-OneVision-7B model, while maintaining accuracy comparable to the full-token baseline. These improvements substantially enhance the practicality of deploying Video-LLMs in real-world production scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）在视频理解任务中展现出强大的能力。然而，处理大量视觉令牌所带来的低效问题仍然阻碍着它们的实际部署。尽管近期的方法实现了极低的令牌保留率，同时保持了与全令牌基线相当的精度，但大多数方法仅在预填充的后期阶段进行压缩，未能优化视觉编码器的效率。在本文中，我们首先证明视觉编码过程贡献了首令牌延迟（TTFT）的很大一部分。因此，与仅在视觉编码器之后压缩视觉令牌不同，在编码器内部进行压缩仍有大量探索空间。基于这一洞察，我们提出EarlyTom，一种无需训练的令牌压缩框架，在视觉编码器内部执行早期视觉令牌压缩，从而显著降低TTFT并提高吞吐量。此外，我们引入了一种解耦的空间令牌选择策略，提升了整体压缩效果。在LLaVA-OneVision-7B模型上，EarlyTom在单张NVIDIA A100 GPU上将TTFT降低高达2.65倍，FLOPs降低高达61%，同时保持与全令牌基线相当的精度。这些改进显著增强了视频大模型在实际生产场景中的部署实用性。

</details>


### [63] [VisualThink-VLA: Visual Intermediate Reasoning for Effective and Low-Latency Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30011)
*Mingjian Gao,Wenqiao Zhang,Yuqian Yuan,Yang Dai,Binhe Yu,Zheqi Lv,Haoyu Zheng,Jiaqi Zhu,Zhiqi Ge,Zixuan Wan,Siliang Tang,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISUALTHINK-VLA 提出视觉中间推理框架，通过紧凑的视觉证据接口和选择性路由机制，在保持空间精度的同时将推理增强基线的多秒延迟降至亚秒级，在多个基准和真实机器人评估中取得最高成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA策略引入文本形式的思维链（CoT）中间推理存在两个问题：(1) 与动作预测无关或弱相关的文本信息会干扰预测；(2) 自回归文本解码带来高延迟，无法满足实时闭环控制的需求。因此需要一种更适合具身控制的中间推理范式。

Method: (1) 提出视觉证据接口（visual-evidence interface），用紧凑的视觉token替代文本推理，保持空间精度的同时避免解码开销；(2) 设计选择性路由机制（selective routing mechanism）学习视觉证据token，实现低延迟推理并保留高容量专用性；(3) 构建VisualEvidence-Kit资源包，包含VisualEvidence-Agent生成的754.7k条VLA指令的VisualEvidence-Set，用于路由监督和反事实忠实性测试。

Result: 在多个基准和真实机器人评估中取得最高成功率；在BridgeData V2上，步延迟从ECoT的8.377秒降至0.367秒，实现22.8倍加速。

Conclusion: 视觉中间推理相比文本思维链更适合具身控制，VISUALTHINK-VLA通过视觉证据和选择性路由实现了高精度与低延迟的兼顾。

Original Abstract: Recent work has begun to equip vision-language-action (VLA) policies with explicit intermediate reasoning. In embodied control, however, textual chain-of-thought is a poor fit: irrelevant or weakly textual information can interfere with action prediction, while autoregressive text decoding adds too much latency for real-time closed-loop execution. We present VISUALTHINK-VLA, a visual intermediate-reasoning framework for accurate, low-latency VLA policies. Our bootstrapping philosophy is to guide action with effective visual thinking: VISUALTHINK-VLA bootstraps action prediction through a compact visual-evidence interface that preserves spatial precision while avoiding decoding overhead. Besides, to further improve performance and efficiency, VISUALTHINK-VLA adopts a tailored selective routing mechanism to learn the visual evidence tokens, enabling low-latency inference while preserving high-capacity specialization. We also introduce VisualEvidence-Kit, a supervision-and-audit resource centered on a VisualEvidence-Agent that constructs a 754.7k VLA instructions VisualEvidence-Set for route supervision and counterfactual faithfulness tests. Across multiple benchmarks and real-robot evaluation, VISUALTHINK-VLA achieves the highest success rate on most benchmarks while reducing the multi-second latency of reasoning-augmented baselines to the sub-second regime. For example, on BridgeData V2, it reduces step latency from 8.377,s with ECoT to 0.367,s, achieving a 22.8 times speedup.

中文摘要翻译: 最近的研究开始为视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略配备显式的中间推理能力。然而在具身控制中，文本形式的思维链并不合适：与动作预测无关或弱相关的文本信息会干扰动作预测，同时自回归文本解码给实时闭环执行带来了过高的延迟。本文提出VISUALTHINK-VLA，一个用于高精度、低延迟VLA策略的视觉中间推理框架。我们的引导哲学是用有效的视觉思考来指导动作：VISUALTHINK-VLA通过一个紧凑的视觉证据接口来引导动作预测，该接口在避免解码开销的同时保留了空间精度。此外，为了进一步提升性能和效率，VISUALTHINK-VLA采用了一种定制的选择性路由机制来学习视觉证据token，在保持高容量专用性的同时实现低延迟推理。我们还引入了VisualEvidence-Kit，这是一个以VisualEvidence-Agent为核心的监督与审计资源，该Agent构建了包含754.7k条VLA指令的VisualEvidence-Set，用于路由监督和反事实忠实性测试。在多个基准测试和真实机器人评估中，VISUALTHINK-VLA在大多数基准上取得了最高成功率，同时将推理增强基线的多秒延迟降至亚秒级。例如，在BridgeData V2上，它将步延迟从ECoT的8.377秒降至0.367秒，实现了22.8倍的加速。

</details>


### [64] [DocRetriever: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Multimodal Document Retrieval with Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30027)
*Ruofan Hu,Menghui Zhu,Jieming Zhu,Bo Chen,Shengyang Xu,Minjie Hong,Xiaoda Yang,Sashuai Zhou,Li Tang,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocRetriever 提出了一种即插即用的多模态文档检索框架，通过布局感知的稀疏嵌入技术实现高效混合编码，并引入推理增强的重排序器以提升少样本场景下的准确性；同时构建了 MultiDocR 基准以支持更全面的评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法面临三个主要问题：(1) 密集视觉嵌入的粗粒度特性模糊了显式语义，未能利用结构上的显著信息；(2) 监督重排序模型泛化能力不足，严重依赖领域特定训练数据；(3) 现有基准缺乏多样化的评估维度和全面的相关性标注，难以进行可靠评估。

Method: DocRetriever 框架包含三个核心贡献：(1) 布局感知的稀疏嵌入技术，可在无需光学字符识别（OCR）的情况下实现有效的混合编码；(2) 基于推理增强的演示和优化采样的可泛化重排序器，提升少样本场景下的准确性；(3) 新构建的 MultiDocR 基准，支持更严格的评估。

Result: 在多个不同基准上的实验验证了 DocRetriever 相比现有最先进方法的优越性。

Conclusion: DocRetriever 通过即插即用的框架有效解决了多模态文档检索中密集嵌入粒度粗、重排序泛化性差及评估基准不全面的问题，在各项基准上均取得了优于现有方法的表现。

Original Abstract: Multimodal documents contain diverse elements, such as tables, figures, and layouts, which can complicate retrieval tasks. While current approaches typically combine dense visual embedding models with supervised rerankers to achieve high-precision retrieval, they face inherent limitations. First, the coarse-grained nature of dense embeddings tends to obfuscate explicit semantics, failing to leverage structurally salient information. Second, supervised reranking models suffer from generalization bottlenecks, as their performance heavily relies on domain-specific training data. Furthermore, existing benchmarks often lack diverse assessment dimensions and comprehensive relevance annotations, limiting reliable evaluation. To address these challenges, we propose DocRetriever, a plug-and-play framework. It enhances visual retrieval via a layout-aware sparse embedding technique, enabling effective hybrid encoding without the overhead of optical character recognition (OCR). We also introduce a generalizable reranker that leverages reasoning-augmented demonstrations and optimized sampling to improve accuracy in few-shot settings. Finally, we construct a new benchmark, MultiDocR, to enable more rigorous evaluation. Experiments across diverse benchmarks validate DocRetriever's superiority over state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态文档包含多种元素，如表格、图形和布局，这些元素可能使检索任务变得复杂。尽管当前方法通常将密集视觉嵌入模型与监督重排序器相结合以实现高精度检索，但它们面临固有的局限性。首先，密集嵌入的粗粒度特性往往会模糊显式语义，无法利用结构上的显著信息。其次，监督重排序模型存在泛化瓶颈，其性能严重依赖于领域特定的训练数据。此外，现有基准往往缺乏多样化的评估维度和全面的相关性标注，限制了可靠的评估。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了 DocRetriever，一个即插即用的框架。它通过布局感知的稀疏嵌入技术增强了视觉检索，能够在无需光学字符识别（OCR）开销的情况下实现有效的混合编码。我们还引入了一个可泛化的重排序器，该重排序器利用推理增强的演示和优化采样来提升少样本场景下的准确性。最后，我们构建了一个新的基准 MultiDocR，以实现更严格的评估。在多个不同基准上的实验验证了 DocRetriever 相较于最先进方法的优越性。

</details>


### [65] [GenEraser: Generalizable Video Object Removal via Balanced Text-Mask Guidance and Decoupled Locator-Preserver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30045)
*Yuqing Chen,Lin Liu,Haisu Wu,Xiaopeng Zhang,Yaowei Wang,Yujiu Yang,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenEraser提出了一种基于扩散变压器的多模态视频物体与效果去除框架，通过多条件混合专家、可学习深度CFG融合机制和解耦专家架构，在跨域场景中同时去除目标物体及其物理效果，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频物体去除方法主要依赖空间掩码，难以捕捉与目标物体弱相关的物理效果（如烟雾、反射、光线、涟漪），且显式文本引导的潜力未被充分挖掘；同时，去除模型在高层语义泛化与精确像素级背景保持之间存在根本性的优化冲突。

Method: 1) 提出多条件混合专家（MC-MoE）配合二分图文本引导，充分利用扩散变压器的多模态先验增强复杂效果识别；2) 提出可学习深度CFG融合机制（LD-CFG），自适应平衡掩码和文本条件在不同场景中的相对主导权；3) 提出解耦专家架构，包含定位器（Locator）和保持器（Preserver），缓解语义泛化与像素对齐之间的固有权衡。

Result: 在ROSE Benchmark和VOR-Eval上分别提升2.16 dB和1.44 dB，在开放世界场景中展现出极强的鲁棒泛化能力，超越当前最新方法。

Conclusion: GenEraser通过多模态条件融合与解耦架构设计，有效解决了视频物体去除中复杂物理效果难以处理以及语义泛化与像素保持之间的冲突，为视频修复任务提供了更通用、高保真的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Video object removal frequently struggles to simultaneously eliminate target objects and their associated physical effects (e.g., smoke, reflections, light, and ripples) in out-of-domain scenarios due to complex spatiotemporal ambiguities. While existing methods primarily rely on spatial masks, they often fail to capture weakly correlated effects, and the potential of explicit textual guidance remains underexplored. Furthermore, a fundamental optimization conflict exists in removal models between high-level semantic generalization and precise pixel-level background preservation. To address these challenges, we propose GenEraser, a novel framework for generalized and high-fidelity video object and effect removal. First, we introduce a Multi-Conditional Mixture-of-Experts (MC-MoE) paired with Bipartite Text guidance to fully exploit the multimodal priors of Diffusion Transformers, significantly enhancing the identification of complex effects. Second, a Learnable Deep ``CFG'' Fusion mechanism (LD-CFG) is developed to adaptively balance the relative dominance of mask and textual conditions across diverse scenarios. Finally, we propose a Decoupled Expert Architecture, comprising a Locator and a Preserver, to mitigate the inherent trade-off between semantic generalization and pixel alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our GenEraser surpasses recent state-of-the-art approaches, achieving significant quantitative improvements (e.g., $2.16$ dB and $1.44$ dB on the ROSE Benchmark and VOR-Eval, respectively) while maintaining exceptionally robust generalization in open-world scenarios. https://cyqii.github.io/GenEraser.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 视频物体去除任务在跨域场景中，由于复杂的时空歧义性，常常难以同时消除目标物体及其相关的物理效果（如烟雾、反射、光和涟漪）。现有方法主要依赖空间掩码，但往往无法捕捉弱相关的效果，且显式文本引导的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。此外，去除模型在高层语义泛化与精确像素级背景保持之间存在根本性的优化冲突。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了GenEraser——一个用于通用且高保真视频物体及效果去除的新型框架。首先，我们引入多条件混合专家（MC-MoE）配合二分图文本引导，充分利用扩散变压器的多模态先验，显著增强复杂效果的识别能力。其次，我们开发了可学习深度CFG融合机制（LD-CFG），以自适应地平衡掩码和文本条件在不同场景中的相对主导权。最后，我们提出解耦专家架构，包含定位器（Locator）和保持器（Preserver），以缓解语义泛化与像素对齐之间的固有权衡。大量实验表明，我们的GenEraser超越了当前最先进的方法，在ROSE Benchmark和VOR-Eval上分别取得了显著的量化提升（分别为2.16 dB和1.44 dB），同时在开放世界场景中保持了极其鲁棒的泛化能力。https://cyqii.github.io/GenEraser.github.io/

</details>


### [66] [Towards Consistent Video Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30060)
*Zhu Yu,Jingnan Gao,Runmin Zhang,Lingteng Qiu,Zhengyi Zhao,Rui Peng,Yichao Yan,Kejie Qiu,Siyu Zhu,Si-Yuan Cao,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViGeo是一个基于纯Transformer架构的前馈基础模型，通过动态分块注意力机制和补全式数据精炼框架，无需任务特定修改即可同时支持流式、全序列和长视频的几何恢复任务（深度、法线、点图），在多个公开数据集上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频几何恢复方法往往针对特定任务设计不同架构，且受限于低质量训练数据和固定的注意力模式，无法在流式、离线、长视频等多种推理场景下统一高效工作。作者希望设计一个单一基础模型，既能适应不同推理模式，又能从低质量标注中生成高质量训练信号。

Method: 1) 动态分块注意力（Dynamic Chunking Attention）：训练时同时暴露双向和因果时序上下文，测试时可自适应调整注意力模式（流式用因果、全序列用双向、长视频用分块双向），无需重新训练。2) 补全式数据精炼框架（Completion-based Data Refinement）：训练一个视频深度补全教师模型，以稀疏噪声标注为条件，利用视频/多视角上下文生成密集、时序一致、几何可靠的训练目标。3) 统一架构预测深度图、点图和表面法线。

Result: 仅在公开数据集上训练，ViGeo在在线深度估计、离线深度估计、长视频深度估计、表面法线估计以及视频点图估计任务上均达到最先进水平（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: ViGeo证明了纯Transformer架构配合动态分块注意力机制和高质量数据精炼策略，能够作为一个统一基础模型，在不同推理模式下同时实现多个视频几何恢复任务的SOTA性能。

Original Abstract: This work presents ViGeo, a feed-forward foundation model for recovering spatially dense and temporally consistent geometry from video sequences. Built upon a plain transformer architecture without task-specific architectural modifications, ViGeo supports streaming, full-sequence, and long-video inference within a unified model. The key design is dynamic chunking attention, which exposes the model to both bidirectional and causal temporal contexts during training and allows it to adapt its attention pattern at test time without retraining. To improve supervision quality, we further introduce a completion-based data refinement framework. This framework trains a video depth completion teacher that conditions on sparse and noisy annotations and exploits video/multi-view context to produce dense, temporally coherent, and geometrically reliable training targets. Beyond depth and point maps, ViGeo also predicts surface normals within the same framework. Trained solely on public datasets, ViGeo achieves state-of-the-art performance across online, offline, and long-video depth estimation, surface normal estimation, and video point map estimation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了ViGeo，一个用于从视频序列中恢复空间密集且时间连续几何结构的前馈基础模型。ViGeo基于纯Transformer架构构建，无需任务特定的架构修改，即可在单一模型内支持流式推理、全序列推理和长视频推理。其关键设计是动态分块注意力机制，该机制在训练阶段让模型同时接触双向和因果时序上下文，并允许模型在测试阶段无需重新训练即可自适应调整注意力模式。为了提升监督质量，我们进一步引入了一个基于补全的数据精炼框架。该框架训练了一个视频深度补全教师模型，其以稀疏且含噪的标注为条件，利用视频/多视角上下文生成密集、时序连贯且几何可靠的训练目标。除深度图和点图外，ViGeo还在同一框架内预测表面法线。仅使用公开数据集训练，ViGeo在在线深度估计、离线深度估计、长视频深度估计、表面法线估计以及视频点图估计任务上均达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [67] [FakeVLM-R1: Internalizing Physical Laws via CoT for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30062)
*Leqi Zhu,Junyan Ye,Kaiqing Lin,Zhiyuan Yan,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FakeVLM-R1，通过GRPO结合批判性思维链（双向辩证推理）增强多模态大模型在合成图像检测中的因果推理能力，构建FakeClue++数据集，实现SOTA性能并解决过度拒绝真实图像的偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于大规模多模态模型的合成图像检测方法仍依赖对伪造数据的模仿学习，缺乏真正的因果推理能力，容易产生解释幻觉。

Method: 在监督微调基础上，引入组相对策略优化（GRPO）与批判性思维链（Critical Thinking CoT）机制；推理时执行“双向辩证推理”：同时提出伪造假设并调用物理常识构建真实性反证；同时构建FakeClue++数据集，引入真实图像的物理规律先验标注。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA性能，实现高精度、逻辑可解释的检测，解决了现有方法对真实图像的过度拒绝偏差，展现出良好的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FakeVLM-R1通过因果推理与批判性思维机制有效克服了合成图像检测中的解释幻觉问题，为可解释伪造检测提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: The development of generative artificial intelligence technologies has propelled the visual realism of synthetic images to an unprecedented level. Although current interpretable detection methods based on Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have made certain progress, they still rely on imitation learning derived from massive volumes of forged data. Consequently, they lack genuine causal reasoning capabilities and are prone to explanatory hallucinations. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose FakeVLM-R1, aiming to endow the model with human-like critical thinking capabilities when performing synthetic detection tasks. Building upon Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), this framework integrates Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a Critical Thinking Chain-of-Thought (CoT) mechanism. During the inference phase, the model executes a "bidirectional dialectical reasoning" process: while proposing a forgery hypothesis, it must simultaneously invoke physical commonsense to construct an authenticity counter-proof. Furthermore, we constructed the FakeClue++ dataset with high-quality samples, which extensively introduces annotations guided by the physical laws of authentic images, providing a unified authenticity anchor for the model. Experiments confirm that FakeVLM-R1 achieves SOTA performance the evaluated models across multiple benchmarks. It not only achieves high-precision, logically interpretable detection but also resolves the over-rejection bias of existing methods against real images, demonstrating generalization and robustness against perturbations.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能技术的发展已将合成图像的视觉真实感推向前所未有的水平。尽管当前基于大规模多模态模型的可解释检测方法取得了一定进展，但它们仍然依赖于从海量伪造数据中获得的模仿学习，因此缺乏真正的因果推理能力，容易产生解释幻觉。为突破这一瓶颈，我们提出FakeVLM-R1，旨在赋予模型在合成检测任务中类似人类的批判性思维能力。该框架在监督微调的基础上，整合了组相对策略优化与批判性思维链机制。在推理阶段，模型执行“双向辩证推理”过程：在提出伪造假设的同时，必须调用物理常识构建真实性反证。此外，我们构建了包含高质量样本的FakeClue++数据集，该数据集广泛引入了基于真实图像物理规律的注释引导，为模型提供了统一的真实性锚点。实验证实，FakeVLM-R1在多个基准上达到评估模型中的最佳性能。它不仅实现了高精度、逻辑可解释的检测，还解决了现有方法对真实图像的过度拒绝偏差，展现出良好的泛化性和抗扰动鲁棒性。

</details>


### [68] [Native Audio-Visual Alignment for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30073)
*Longbin Ji,Guan Wang,Xuan Wei,Chenye Yang,Xiangrui Liu,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Jingzhou He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAVA提出了一种原生音视频对齐框架，通过“先对齐后融合”的MMDiT架构和上下文条件控制，实现高质量的联合音视频生成，仅用6.3B参数即可在视频质量、音画同步和音色可控性上达到领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开源方法存在两个局限：一是双塔设计依赖后验对齐，削弱了音视频的细粒度协同演化；二是全统一三模态设计将语义条件与低级同步耦合在一起。两者均未能很好地实现原生音视频对齐。

Method: 提出NAVA框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) Align-then-Fuse MMDiT架构，从模态感知的音视频对齐过渡到模态共享的联合去噪；(2) Timbre-in-Context Conditioning，将参考音色线索与对应的语音片段相关联，实现可控的语音音色。

Result: 在Verse-Bench和Seed-TTS上的实验及用户研究表明，NAVA仅用6.3B参数即实现了优越的视频质量、精确的音画同步、有竞争力的音频质量以及更强的参考音色可控性。

Conclusion: NAVA通过原生音视频对齐设计有效解决了现有方法的局限，验证了先对齐后融合架构在联合音视频生成任务中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Joint audio-video generation aims to synthesize temporally synchronized and semantically coherent visual-acoustic content. However, existing open-source methods mainly rely on either dual-tower designs with posterior alignment or fully unified tri-modal designs that mix textual context, audio and video in one shared space. The former weakens fine-grained audio-video co-evolution, while the latter couples semantic conditioning with low-level synchronization. To address these limitations, we propose NAVA, a Native Audio-Visual Alignment framework for joint audio-video generation. NAVA is built upon context-conditioned native audio-visual alignment: it first establishes audio-video correspondence in a dedicated interaction space, and then uses external context to condition the joint denoising process. Specifically, NAVA is instantiated with an Align-then-Fuse MMDiT architecture, which transitions from modality-aware audio-video alignment to modality-shared joint denoising. Furthermore, we introduce Timbre-in-Context Conditioning to associate reference timbre cues with corresponding speech spans to achieve controllable speech timbre. Experiments on Verse-Bench and Seed-TTS, together with a user study, demonstrate that NAVA achieves superior video quality, precise audio-visual synchronization, competitive audio quality, and stronger reference-timbre controllability using only 6.3B parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 联合音视频生成旨在合成时序同步且语义连贯的视觉-声学内容。然而，现有的开源方法主要依赖于两种设计：一种是带有后验对齐的双塔设计，另一种是将文本上下文、音频和视频混合在同一个共享空间中的全统一三模态设计。前者削弱了音视频的细粒度协同演化，而后者则将语义条件与低级同步耦合在一起。为了解决这些局限，我们提出NAVA，一种用于联合音视频生成的原生音视频对齐框架。NAVA建立在上下文条件的原生音视频对齐之上：它首先在专用的交互空间中建立音视频对应关系，然后利用外部上下文来条件化联合去噪过程。具体来说，NAVA实例化了一种"先对齐后融合"的MMDiT架构，该架构从模态感知的音视频对齐过渡到模态共享的联合去噪。此外，我们引入了上下文音色条件化机制，将参考音色线索与对应的语音片段相关联，以实现可控的语音音色。在Verse-Bench和Seed-TTS上的实验以及用户研究均表明，NAVA仅使用6.3B参数即可实现优越的视频质量、精确的音画同步、有竞争力的音频质量以及更强的参考音色可控性。

</details>


### [69] [Geometry Matters: 3D Foundation Priors for Learning Semantic Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30093)
*Artur Jesslen,Olaf Dünkel,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种3D感知的后训练框架，利用SAM3D和PartField从2D基础特征中提取3D结构先验，通过几何感知特征图和测地距离过滤对应关系，训练轻量适配器以提升语义对应估计的3D感知能力，减少人工几何标注需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的自监督视觉模型和文生图扩散模型的基础特征主要从2D图像目标中学习，缺乏显式的3D感知能力，容易混淆对称物体侧面、重复部件以及视觉相似但3D不同的结构，导致语义对应估计不准确。

Method: 1) 使用SAM3D估计图像中物体的几何结构和姿态；2) 通过渲染-比较优化精化姿态；3) 将PartField描述子从重建几何渲染到图像平面，生成几何感知特征图；4) 利用重建形状上的测地距离过滤候选对应关系；5) 用过滤后的匹配作为监督信号，在DINO和Stable Diffusion之上训练轻量适配器。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在提升语义对应估计性能的同时，减少了人工几何监督的依赖，优于现有的后训练方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入3D基础模型的先验信息，可以在不需要姿态标注且不依赖粗糙球面几何的前提下，自动获取实例特定的3D结构并指导对应学习，从而有效提升2D基础特征的3D感知能力。

Original Abstract: Foundation features from self-supervised vision models and text-to-image diffusion models have proven effective for semantic correspondence estimation. However, because these features are learned primarily from 2D image objectives, they lack explicit 3D awareness and often confuse symmetric object sides, repeated parts, and visually similar structures that are distinct in 3D. We introduce a 3D-aware post-training framework that goes beyond available 2D foundation features by incorporating priors from 3D foundation models. Given an image, our method uses SAM3D to estimate object geometry and pose, and refines the pose through render-and-compare optimization. Subsequently, we render PartField descriptors from the reconstructed geometry into the image plane based on the estimated object pose. The resulting geometry-aware feature maps complement DINO and Stable Diffusion features, while geodesic distances on the reconstructed shapes enable reliable filtering of candidate correspondences. We use the filtered matches as supervision to train a lightweight adapter on top of DINO and Stable Diffusion for semantic correspondence. In contrast to prior post-training approaches that require pose annotations and rely on coarse spherical geometry, our method automatically obtains instance-specific 3D structure and uses it to guide correspondence learning. Experiments show that our approach improves semantic correspondence over the prior methods while reducing manual geometric supervision. Code and model can be found at https:/github.com/GenIntel/3D-SC.

中文摘要翻译: 来自自监督视觉模型和文生图扩散模型的基础特征已被证明对语义对应估计有效。然而，由于这些特征主要从2D图像目标中学习，它们缺乏显式的3D感知能力，常常混淆对称物体侧面、重复部件以及视觉相似但在3D中不同的结构。我们提出了一种3D感知的后训练框架，通过引入3D基础模型的先验信息，超越了现有的2D基础特征。给定一张图像，我们的方法使用SAM3D估计物体的几何结构和姿态，并通过渲染-比较优化精化姿态。随后，我们根据估计的物体姿态，将PartField描述子从重建几何渲染到图像平面。由此产生的几何感知特征图补充了DINO和Stable Diffusion特征，而重建形状上的测地距离能够可靠地过滤候选对应关系。我们使用过滤后的匹配作为监督信号，在DINO和Stable Diffusion之上训练轻量适配器进行语义对应。与需要姿态标注且依赖粗糙球面几何的现有后训练方法不同，我们的方法自动获取实例特定的3D结构，并用其指导对应学习。实验表明，我们的方法在提升语义对应估计性能的同时减少了人工几何监督。代码和模型可在https://github.com/GenIntel/3D-SC获取。

</details>


### [70] [SGMD: Score Gradient Matching Distillation for Few-Step Video Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30116)
*Zhuguanyu Wu,Ruihao Gong,Yang Yong,Yushi Huang,Xiangyu Fan,Lei Yang,Dahua Lin,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGMD方法，通过分数梯度匹配蒸馏和双势能机制（负残差外环校正+残差收缩内环追踪），在视频扩散模型少步推理中实现约3倍训练加速，并显著提升运动动态质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DMD风格的视频蒸馏面临两个耦合挑战：伪造分数需要追踪持续演变的生成器导致训练成本高昂，而反向KL匹配具有模式寻求性，会保守地保留强运动动态。

Method: 提出分数梯度匹配蒸馏（SGMD），直接优化伪造分数使其趋近教师分数，并使用教师停止梯度的Fisher信息作为稳定的分布匹配目标；引入双势能机制：负残差（NR）用于外环校正，残差收缩（RC）用于内环追踪。

Result: 与DMD2相比，SGMD实现约3倍训练加速，显著提升4步蒸馏模型的运动动态同时保持时间一致性。人工评估表明SGMD在运动质量和总体偏好上更优，视觉质量和文本对齐保持相当。

Conclusion: SGMD通过分数梯度匹配蒸馏和双势能机制有效解决了DMD视频蒸馏中的训练效率低和运动动态保守问题，在加速训练的同时提升了生成质量。

Original Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) is a widely used paradigm for accelerating inference in few-step video diffusion models. However, DMD-style video distillation faces two coupled challenges: the fake score must track a continuously evolving generator, making training costly when frequent updates are required, while reverse-KL-style matching can be mode-seeking and conservative for preserving strong motion dynamics. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Score Gradient Matching Distillation (SGMD)}. SGMD adopts a fake-score perspective by directly optimizing the fake score toward the teacher, while using teacher stop-gradient Fisher as a stable distribution-matching objective. We provide a gradient analysis that motivates this objective choice under ideal tracking. Building on this, SGMD introduces a pair of dual potentials: negative-residual (NR) for outer-loop correction and residual-contraction (RC) for inner-loop tracking. Empirically, compared to DMD2, SGMD achieves an approximately $\sim 3\times$ training speedup and substantially improves motion dynamics for 4-step distilled models while preserving temporal consistency. A human study confirms that SGMD is preferred in motion quality and overall preference, while visual quality and text alignment remain comparable. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V.

中文摘要翻译: 分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）是加速少步视频扩散模型推理的常用范式。然而，DMD风格的视频蒸馏面临两个耦合挑战：伪造分数必须追踪持续演变的生成器，当需要频繁更新时训练成本高昂；而反向KL匹配具有模式寻求性，在保留强运动动态方面趋于保守。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了分数梯度匹配蒸馏（SGMD）。SGMD采用伪造分数视角，直接优化伪造分数使其趋近教师分数，同时使用教师停止梯度的Fisher信息作为稳定的分布匹配目标。我们提供了梯度分析来证明在理想追踪条件下该目标选择的合理性。在此基础上，SGMD引入了一对双势能：负残差（NR）用于外环校正，残差收缩（RC）用于内环追踪。实验表明，与DMD2相比，SGMD实现了约3倍的训练加速，显著提升了4步蒸馏模型的运动动态，同时保持了时间一致性。人工评估证实SGMD在运动质量和总体偏好上更受青睐，视觉质量和文本对齐保持相当。代码见https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V。

</details>


### [71] [PARCEL: Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30126)
*Selim Kuzucu,Alessio Tonioni,Vasile Lup,Bernt Schiele,Federico Tombari,Muhammad Ferjad Naeem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PARCEL架构，通过池化锚点与条件查询重采样解耦空间布局和特征提取，实现弹性视觉token压缩，在27个基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有弹性视觉压缩方法在激进压缩下表现不佳：空间压缩（如嵌套池化）产生频谱混叠丢失细节，查询压缩（如嵌套查询重采样）破坏空间定位能力，缺乏对空间布局与特征提取的有效分工。

Method: PARCEL架构包含两个关键设计：(1) 池化锚点（Pool Anchors）：将空间池化token作为低频布局锚点；(2) 池条件查询重采样（Pool-Conditioned Query Resampling, PCQR）：弹性查询token以池化锚点为条件进行重采样，使其聚焦于互补的视觉特征而非冗余的空间映射。

Result: 在27个基准上的广泛评估表明，PARCEL在性能-效率帕累托前沿上持续优于现有Matryoshka基线方法，支持"一次训练，随处部署"范式。

Conclusion: PARCEL通过解耦空间锚点与弹性查询的分工，有效解决了弹性视觉压缩中空间细节保留与空间定位能力之间的表征冲突，在不同视觉token预算下均取得更优的权衡。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) map visual inputs into dense token sequences, imposing a quadratic computational bottleneck for inference. Elastic visual-token compression addresses this by training a single model that can run at multiple visual-token budgets. However, existing approaches struggle under aggressive compression. Spatial-only compression, as in nested pooling, behaves as an imperfect low-pass filter and induces spectral aliasing that obscures fine-grained detail. Query-only compression, as in nested query resampling, replaces explicit grid-aligned tokens with non-local summaries and substantially degrades spatial grounding. To resolve this representational conflict, we introduce PARCEL (Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding), a visual tokenization architecture that dynamically partitions the labor of feature extraction. PARCEL establishes spatial pool tokens as low-frequency layout anchors and conditions elastic query tokens on these anchors through Pool-Conditioned Query Resampling. This encourages query tokens to focus on complementary visual features rather than redundant spatial mapping. Extensive evaluations across 27 benchmarks show that PARCEL improves the performance-efficiency Pareto frontier, consistently outperforming existing matryoshka baselines across visual-token budgets while preserving the "train once, deploy anywhere" paradigm.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）将视觉输入映射为密集的token序列，带来了推理时的二次计算瓶颈。弹性视觉token压缩通过训练单一模型在多种视觉token预算下运行来应对这一问题。然而，现有方法在激进压缩场景下表现不佳。仅进行空间压缩（如嵌套池化）的行为类似于不完美的低通滤波器，引入频谱混叠从而模糊了细粒度细节。仅进行查询压缩（如嵌套查询重采样）则用非局部摘要替代了显式的网格对齐token，严重削弱了空间定位能力。为了解决这一表征冲突，我们提出了PARCEL（池锚定重采样与条件弹性查询用于高效视觉-语言理解），这是一种动态划分特征提取任务的视觉token化架构。PARCEL将空间池化token建立为低频布局锚点，并通过池条件查询重采样（Pool-Conditioned Query Resampling）使弹性查询token以这些锚点为条件。这促使查询token聚焦于互补的视觉特征，而非冗余的空间映射。在27个基准上的广泛评估表明，PARCEL提升了性能-效率的帕累托前沿，在不同的视觉token预算下持续优于现有的Matryoshka基线方法，同时保留了"一次训练，随处部署"的范式。

</details>


### [72] [AnomalyAgent: Training-Free Agentic Models for Zero-/Few-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30140)
*Yi Zhang,Jiawen Zhu,Lele Fu,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnomalyAgent是一种无需训练、基于智能体框架的异常检测方法，利用多模态大语言模型的推理能力，通过专用工具集和记忆模块实现零样本和少样本异常检测，在简单和复杂异常场景下均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视觉-语言模型的零样本/少样本异常检测方法依赖大量辅助数据集进行训练，且仅基于视觉-文本嵌入相似度计算异常分数，缺乏对需要深度上下文理解的复杂异常进行推理的能力。

Method: 提出AnomalyAgent框架，核心包括：1）综合性的异常中心工具集，支持MLLM驱动的自适应智能体异常推理（零样本设置）；2）定制化记忆模块，通过少样本上下文参考示例增强异常推理能力。

Result: 在广泛基准测试（简单异常如裂纹、凹痕、病灶）以及物流和制造业中逻辑/上下文异常等更丰富场景下，AnomalyAgent相比无需训练的VLM异常检测方法和通用智能体方法均取得了显著更优的性能，展现了卓越的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AnomalyAgent作为一种无需训练、基于MLLM的智能体框架，通过推理驱动的异常检测方式，在零样本和少样本设置下均展现出优越的泛化性能，有效弥补了现有VLM方法缺乏推理能力的不足。

Original Abstract: Benefiting from generalizability of vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP, many zero-/few-shot anomaly detection (AD) approaches have achieved impressive detection performance across various datasets. Nevertheless, they require substantial training on large auxiliary datasets to adapt VLMs to anomaly detection, and their inference largely relies on visual-text embedding similarity-based anomaly scores, lacking reasoning abilities to detect complex anomalies that require in-depth contextual understanding. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{AnomalyAgent}, a novel training-free, agentic framework that leverages the advanced reasoning and generalization capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for anomaly detection. The key ingredients include \textbf{1)} a comprehensive anomaly-centric toolset that enables adaptive MLLM-driven, agentic anomaly reasoning in zero-shot settings, and \textbf{2)} a customized memory module that grounds anomaly reasoning with few-shot, in-context reference examples. We extend evaluation beyond the detection of simple anomalies (e.g., surface defects like cracks and dents and clear lesions) in widely used benchmarks to more diverse types of anomalies such as logical/contextual anomalies in logistics and manufacturing settings. Extensive experiment results demonstrate that our AnomalyAgent achieves substantially better performance compared to training-free VLM-based AD and generic agentic methods, highlighting its superior generalization capability in both zero-shot and few-shot anomaly detection settings. The code implementation can be find at this address.

中文摘要翻译: 得益于视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的泛化能力，许多零样本/少样本异常检测方法已在多种数据集上取得了令人印象深刻的检测性能。然而，这些方法需要在大规模辅助数据集上进行大量训练才能将视觉-语言模型适配到异常检测任务中，并且它们的推理过程主要依赖于基于视觉-文本嵌入相似度的异常分数，缺乏检测需要深度上下文理解的复杂异常的推理能力。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了\textbf{AnomalyAgent}，一种新颖的、无需训练的智能体框架，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的高级推理和泛化能力进行异常检测。其关键要素包括：\textbf{1)}一个综合性的以异常为中心的工具集，支持在零样本设置下进行自适应的MLLM驱动的智能体异常推理；\textbf{2)}一个定制的记忆模块，通过少样本的上下文参考示例来支撑异常推理。我们将评估范围从广泛使用的基准中的简单异常（如裂纹和凹痕等表面缺陷以及明显的病灶）检测，扩展到更多样化的异常类型，例如物流和制造环境中的逻辑/上下文异常。大量实验结果表明，与无需训练的基于VLM的异常检测方法和通用智能体方法相比，我们的AnomalyAgent在零样本和少样本异常检测设置下均取得了显著更优的性能，凸显了其卓越的泛化能力。代码实现可在此地址获取。

</details>


### [73] [Why Far Looks Up: Probing Spatial Representation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30161)
*Cheolhong Min,Jaeyun Jung,Daeun Lee,Hyeonseong Jeon,Yu Su,Jonathan Tremblay,Chan Hee Song,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在空间推理任务中是否真正具备结构化3D理解，还是仅依赖自然图像中的统计捷径。通过构建最小对比对的分析框架，发现模型存在垂直-距离纠缠偏差（将垂直位置与距离混淆），该偏差在数据扩展下加剧，且内部表示结构可预测模型性能。作者提出合成基准SpatialTunnel以隔离这种偏差，验证了纠缠是模型内在的特性，而良好分离的空间表示能提升模型鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理基准上表现优异，但尚不清楚这种能力是源于真正的3D结构化理解，还是仅仅利用了自然图像中存在的统计捷径（如视角偏差）。作者希望深入探究模型内部空间表示的真正性质。

Method: 提出一种表示级分析框架，通过构建最小对比对来测量VLM嵌入中空间轴的组织和解缠程度。同时引入SpatialTunnel合成基准，通过去除自然图像中的常见相关性来暴露空间捷径偏差。

Result: （1）VLM存在一致的垂直-距离纠缠：模型将垂直图像位置与距离混淆，反映了自然照片的视角偏差；（2）这种偏差导致视角一致样本与反启发式样本之间存在显著的准确率差距，且随数据扩展而加剧；（3）基准分数相似的模型内部表示可能不同，且这些差异可预测空间推理的准确性和鲁棒性；（4）SpatialTunnel验证了纠缠是模型内在特性，空间轴分离良好的模型具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLM的空间推理能力并非源于真正结构化3D理解，而是受自然图像统计偏差影响。内部空间表示的解缠程度是预测模型鲁棒性和可靠性的关键指标，构建结构化的空间表示有助于提升跨基准的推理可靠性。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on spatial reasoning benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this reflects structured 3D understanding or reliance on statistical shortcuts in natural images. We introduce a representation-level analysis framework that constructs minimal contrastive pairs to measure how spatial axes are organized and disentangled within VLM embeddings. Our analysis across multiple model families reveals a consistent vertical-distance entanglement: models conflate vertical image position with distance, mirroring the perspective bias of natural photographs. This bias produces a significant accuracy gap between perspective-consistent and counter-heuristic examples, and intensifies under data scaling even as overall benchmark accuracy improves. We further show that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit different internal representations, and that these differences predict accuracy and robustness across diverse spatial reasoning benchmarks. To isolate this bias from evaluation-set skew, we introduce SpatialTunnel, a synthetic benchmark designed to expose spatial shortcut biases by removing common correlations present in natural images. Experiments confirm that the entanglement is model-intrinsic, and that models with well-separated spatial axes exhibit greater robustness, suggesting that well-structured spatial representations lead to more reliable spatial reasoning across diverse benchmarks. Code and benchmark are available on the project page: https://cheolhong0916.github.io/whyfarlooksup.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在空间推理基准上取得了强劲的表现，但这究竟反映了结构化的3D理解，还是对自然图像中统计捷径的依赖，尚不清楚。我们引入了一种表示级分析框架，通过构建最小对比对来测量VLM嵌入中空间轴的组织和解缠程度。跨多个模型家族的分析揭示了一个一致的垂直-距离纠缠现象：模型将垂直图像位置与距离混淆，这反映了自然照片的视角偏差。这种偏差在视角一致样本与反启发式样本之间产生了显著的准确率差距，且随着数据规模的扩大而加剧，即使整体基准准确率在提升。我们进一步表明，基准分数相似的模型可能具有不同的内部表示，并且这些差异能够预测其在多样化空间推理基准上的准确性和鲁棒性。为了将该偏差与评估集偏差分离开，我们引入了SpatialTunnel——一个旨在通过去除自然图像中常见相关性来暴露空间捷径偏差的合成基准。实验证实，这种纠缠是模型内在的，并且空间轴分离良好的模型表现出更强的鲁棒性，这表明结构化的空间表示能带来跨不同基准的更可靠的空间推理。代码和基准可在项目页面获取：https://cheolhong0916.github.io/whyfarlooksup.github.io/。

</details>


### [74] [OmniCD: A Foundational Framework for Remote Sensing Image Change Detection Guided by Multimodal Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30168)
*Chenhao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniCD 是一个统一的多模态遥感变化检测框架，通过融合图像和文本提示（如文字描述、语义地图、地理元数据）以及风格解缠机制，实现了从二元变化检测到零样本语义变化理解的全覆盖，并在大规模多模态数据集 RSITCD（30万+图文对）上取得了 SOTA 性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测方法在跨场景泛化方面存在困难，难以统一处理不同模态输入（如文本描述、语义图、地理元数据）和不同任务类型（二元变化检测到零样本语义变化理解），缺乏一个通用且鲁棒的基础框架。

Method: OmniCD 框架包含三个核心组件：1) 统一架构，整合图像和文本提示；2) 分层场景检索模块，辅助多模态语义对齐；3) 变化检测模块，结合风格解缠机制以增强跨域鲁棒性。此外，构建了包含 30 万+ 图文对的大规模多模态数据集 RSITCD。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，OmniCD 在变化检测任务上取得了最先进的性能，展现出强大的跨场景适应能力。

Conclusion: OmniCD 为遥感领域通用变化检测系统奠定了坚实基础，统一并增强了多模态语义引导下的遥感变化检测能力。

Original Abstract: Change detection (CD) in remote sensing is vital for applications such as urban monitoring and disaster assessment, yet traditional methods struggle with generalization across diverse scenarios. We present OmniCD, a foundational framework that unifies and enhances remote sensing CD through multimodal semantic guidance. OmniCD incorporates image and text prompts -- such as textual descriptions, semantic maps, and geospatial metadata -- into a unified architecture, supporting tasks from binary CD to zero-shot semantic change understanding. The framework integrates a hierarchical scene retrieval module and a change detection module, reinforced by a style disentanglement mechanism for improved cross-domain robustness. We further introduce RSITCD, a large-scale multimodal dataset with 300K+ annotated image-text pairs. Extensive experiments show that OmniCD achieves state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks, demonstrating strong adaptability and setting a solid foundation for general-purpose CD systems in remote sensing.

中文摘要翻译: 遥感中的变化检测（CD）对于城市监测和灾害评估等应用至关重要，然而传统方法在跨多样场景的泛化方面存在困难。我们提出了 OmniCD，这是一个通过多模态语义引导统一并增强遥感变化检测的基础框架。OmniCD 将图像和文本提示——例如文本描述、语义地图和地理空间元数据——整合到一个统一的架构中，支持从二元变化检测到零样本语义变化理解等任务。该框架集成了分层场景检索模块和变化检测模块，并通过风格解缠机制增强了跨域鲁棒性。我们进一步引入了 RSITCD，一个包含 30 万以上带注释图文对的大规模多模态数据集。大量实验表明，OmniCD 在多个基准测试中达到最先进的性能，展现出强大的适应性，为遥感领域的通用变化检测系统奠定了坚实基础。

</details>


### [75] [LiveSVG: Zero-Shot SVG Animation via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30174)
*Matan Levy,Ran Margolin,Bar Cavia,Dvir Samuel,Yael Pritch,Shmuel Peleg,Alex Rav Acha,Ariel Shamir,Dani Lischinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveSVG提出了一种零样本的SVG动画生成方法，通过视频扩散模型生成目标视频，再通过可微渲染将原始SVG拟合到该视频上，无需骨架先验，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SVG动画方法在处理复杂运动时存在局限：基于LLM的代码合成难以表达精细的非刚性贝塞尔变形，而SDS方法梯度噪声大且通常需要类别特定的骨架先验。

Method: 给定输入SVG图像和运动提示，首先使用冻结的图像到视频模型生成目标视频；然后通过可微渲染将原始SVG拟合到该视频上。拟合阶段无需骨架，采用双层运动表示：每组单应性矩阵用于粗略关节运动，每条路径的贝塞尔控制点偏移用于局部变形。为解决颜色导致的像素级对应歧义，引入球体填充重着色策略。

Result: 在AniClipart和ChallengeSVG两个基准上，LiveSVG显著优于现有方法。同时构建了ChallengeSVG基准，包含复杂多目标场景，揭示了先前工作的局限性。

Conclusion: 直接参考视频拟合是一种实用且鲁棒的路径，能够生成与提示对齐且完全可编辑的矢量动画。

Original Abstract: We introduce LiveSVG, a zero-shot approach for generating Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) animations using video diffusion models. Current SVG animation methods struggle with complex motions: LLM-based code synthesis fails to express fine, non-rigid Bézier deformations, while Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) provides noisy gradients and often requires category-specific priors like skeletons. In contrast, LiveSVG fits vector geometry directly to an explicitly generated target video. Given an input SVG image and a motion prompt, we generate a previewable target video using a frozen image-to-video model, then fit the original SVG to this video via differentiable rendering. Our fitting stage is skeleton-free, utilizing a dual-level motion representation that combines per-group homographies for coarse articulation with per-path Bézier control-point offsets for local deformations. To resolve color-induced correspondence ambiguities during pixel-wise fitting, we introduce a novel sphere-packing recolorization strategy. We also present ChallengeSVG, a benchmark of complex, multi-object scenes that exposes the limitations of prior work. Evaluations demonstrate that LiveSVG significantly outperforms existing methods on both AniClipart and ChallengeSVG, establishing direct reference-video fitting as a practical, robust route to prompt-aligned and fully editable vector animation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了LiveSVG，一种利用视频扩散模型生成可缩放矢量图形（SVG）动画的零样本方法。当前的SVG动画方法在处理复杂运动时存在困难：基于大语言模型的代码合成无法表达精细的非刚性贝塞尔变形，而分数蒸馏采样（SDS）提供的梯度含有噪声，并且通常需要类别特定的先验（如骨架）。相比之下，LiveSVG直接将矢量几何拟合到显式生成的目标视频上。给定输入SVG图像和运动提示，我们使用冻结的图像到视频模型生成可预览的目标视频，然后通过可微渲染将原始SVG拟合到该视频上。我们的拟合阶段无需骨架，采用一种双层运动表示，该表示结合了用于粗略关节运动的每组单应性矩阵和用于局部变形的每条路径贝塞尔控制点偏移。为了解决像素级拟合中由颜色引起的对应歧义，我们引入了一种新颖的球体填充重着色策略。我们还提出了ChallengeSVG，一个包含复杂多目标场景的基准测试，揭示了先前工作的局限性。评估结果表明，LiveSVG在AniClipart和ChallengeSVG两个基准上均显著优于现有方法，确立了直接参考视频拟合作为一种实用且鲁棒的途径，用于生成与提示对齐且完全可编辑的矢量动画。

</details>


### [76] [Cycle Consistency in Video Object-Centric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30211)
*Rongzhen Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Ruonan Wei,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式循环一致性（ICC），解决自监督视频目标中心学习（OCL）中显式循环一致性（ECC）导致特征坍缩的问题，通过将约束从潜槽空间转移到连续重建流形，在复杂视频OCL基准上优于ECC基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在OCL中，槽位表征具有随机性和歧义性，直接施加显式循环一致性约束会强制刚性均值对齐，惩罚模型探索其他同样有效的分解方式，导致特征坍缩。需要一种更适合OCL特性的循环一致性机制。

Method: 提出隐式循环一致性（ICC），将循环一致性约束从限制性的潜槽空间转移到连续的图像重建流形，鼓励槽位在集体解释视觉场景时达成软共识，而非强制点对点的特征对齐。

Result: 在复杂视频OCL基准上的大量实验表明，ICC避免了特征坍缩，并在性能上显著优于ECC基线方法。

Conclusion: 隐式循环一致性是一种有效且适合OCL特性的约束方式，能够避免显式循环一致性带来的特征坍缩问题，提升模型在复杂视频场景中的目标中心学习效果。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to discover distinct objects and associate them across time, whereas self-supervised Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) focuses on associating pre-defined object detections or segmentations. Although well-established in MOT, Cycle Consistency (CC) cannot naively or explicitly apply to the latent slot space of OCL. Unlike the deterministic and ideal object representations in MOT, OCL slots are inherently stochastic and ambiguous due to non-unique scene decompositions. Enforcing explicit cycle consistency (ECC) on slots imposes rigid mean seeking. This severely penalizes the model for exploring alternative but equally valid decompositions, thereby driving towards feature collapse. To resolve this dilemma, we propose \textit{Implicit Cycle Consistency (ICC)}, which shifts the cycle-consistency constraint from the restrictive slot space to the continuous reconstruction manifold, encouraging slots to reach a soft consensus on collectively interpreting the visual scene rather than forcing rigid point-to-point feature alignment. Extensive experiments on complex video OCL benchmarks demonstrate that ICC avoids feature collapse and outperforms ECC baselines. Our source code, model checkpoints and training logs are provided on https://github.com/Genera1Z/ICC.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督视频目标中心学习（OCL）旨在发现不同目标并在时间上关联它们，而自监督多目标跟踪（MOT）则侧重于关联预定义的目标检测或分割结果。尽管循环一致性（CC）在MOT中已得到充分验证，但它无法朴素地或显式地应用于OCL的潜槽空间。与MOT中确定性和理想的目标表征不同，OCL的槽位由于场景分解的非唯一性而天然具有随机性和歧义性。在槽位上施加显式循环一致性（ECC）会导致刚性的均值追寻，严重惩罚模型探索其他同样有效的分解方式，从而驱动模型走向特征坍缩。为解决这一困境，我们提出隐式循环一致性（ICC），将循环一致性约束从限制性的槽空间转移到连续的重建流形，鼓励槽位在集体解释视觉场景时达成软共识，而非强制刚性的点对点特征对齐。在复杂视频OCL基准上的大量实验表明，ICC避免了特征坍缩，并在性能上优于ECC基线。我们的源代码、模型检查点和训练日志已在 https://github.com/Genera1Z/ICC 上提供。

</details>


### [77] [Déjà View: Looping Transformers for Multi-View 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30215)
*Alessandro Burzio,Tobias Fischer,Sven Elflein,Qunjie Zhou,Riccardo de Lutio,Jiawei Ren,Jiahui Huang,Shengyu Huang,Marc Pollefeys,Laura Leal-Taixé,Zan Gojcic,Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DéjàView通过循环使用单个Transformer块进行多视图3D重建，以显式迭代替代深层堆叠，在显著减少参数量的同时匹配或超越更大的前馈基线模型，并将推理步数作为可调节的计算旋钮。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建Transformer不断扩大模型规模，但证据表明连续层的行为类似重复操作，深度部分买来的是迭代而非真正的表征能力，参数效率低下。因此，作者主张将迭代显式化到架构中，以更高效的参数利用实现同等甚至更好的重建性能。

Method: 提出DéjàView模型，采用单个循环Transformer块对每视图特征进行K步递归细化。训练一次后，推理时的K值可作为计算旋钮灵活调节。该循环块与具有独立逐层参数的变体在相同训练数据和计算量下进行对比。

Result: 在涵盖室内、室外、以物体为中心和驾驶场景的五个重建基准上，DéjàView以远少于前馈基线的参数和相当或更低的计算量，匹配或超越其性能。循环块方案优于具有相同架构但使用独立每步参数的变体。

Conclusion: 显式迭代不仅是计算高效的容量替代方案，更是多视图3D重建中更强的归纳偏置。将迭代显式化到架构中能够在参数效率与性能之间实现更优的权衡。

Original Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D reconstruction transformers have scaled to over a billion parameters, following the broader trend of increasing model capacity in computer vision. Yet emerging evidence suggests that contiguous transformer layers often behave like repeated applications of similar operations, and multi-view reconstruction transformers refine their predictions progressively across decoder depth. We posit that model depth partially buys iteration, paid for inefficiently in unique parameters, and instead make that iteration explicit in architecture. Our model, DéjàView, applies a single looped transformer block recurrently to per-view features for K refinement steps. Trained once, it exposes K as an inference-time compute knob, matching or outperforming substantially larger feed-forward baselines across five reconstruction benchmarks spanning indoor, outdoor, object-centric, and driving scenes, while using a fraction of their parameters and comparable or lower compute. Importantly, the same looped block formulation outperforms an otherwise identical variant with independent per-step parameters under matched training data and compute, suggesting that explicit iteration is not merely a compute-efficient substitute for capacity but a stronger inductive bias for multi-view 3D reconstruction.

中文摘要翻译: 近期的前馈式3D重建Transformer已扩展到超过十亿参数，遵循了计算机视觉中模型容量不断增大的总体趋势。然而，新出现的证据表明，连续的Transformer层往往表现得像重复应用类似的操作，而多视图重建Transformer在解码器深度上逐步精细化其预测。我们假设模型深度部分买来的是迭代，但以独特的参数低效地支付了代价，因此我们将这种迭代显式化到架构中。我们的模型DéjàView，对每视图特征循环应用单个循环Transformer块，进行K步递归细化。训练一次后，它便将K暴露为推理时的计算旋钮，在涵盖室内、室外、以物体为中心和驾驶场景的五个重建基准上，以远少于前馈基线的参数和相当或更低的计算量，匹配或超越其性能。重要的是，在匹配的训练数据和计算量下，同一个循环块公式优于具有独立每步参数的、其他方面相同的变体，这表明显式迭代不仅是一种计算高效的容量替代方案，更是多视图3D重建中更强的归纳偏置。

</details>


### [78] [Beyond 3D VQAs: Injecting 3D Spatial Priors into Vision-Language Models for Enhanced Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30231)
*Chun-Hsiao Yeh,Shengyi Qian,Manchen Wang,Yi Ma,Joseph Tighe,Fanyi Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GASP框架，通过将几何先验注入LLM的Transformer层来提升视觉-语言模型的3D空间推理能力。该方法利用对应点匹配的对比损失和深度一致性监督进行训练，在无需3D VQA数据的情况下，将层间对应匹配准确率从低于5%提升至超过70%，并在下游空间基准上取得显著提升（All-Angles Bench +18.2%，VSI-Bench +29.0%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型在鲁棒的3D空间推理方面表现不佳。传统方法要么依赖3D VQA数据集微调导致过拟合数据集特定偏差，要么集成专用3D视觉编码器导致灵活性差。作者认为真正的空间理解应来自学习基础几何先验，而非仅依赖高层VQA监督。

Method: 提出GASP框架，通过一个小型对应头（correspondence head）作为深度监督信号注入LLM的所有Transformer层，采用双目标训练：1）基于真实点对应的对比损失，强制2D视角不变性；2）深度一致性监督，解决3D几何歧义。训练数据来自大规模视频场景中的真实几何信息。

Result: 标准VLM的内部对应匹配准确率极低（低于5%）；GASP将峰值层间对应匹配提升至超过70%，时间鲁棒性保持85%以上（基线低于5%）。下游任务上，All-Angles Bench提升18.2%，VSI-Bench提升29.0%，且无需任何3D VQA训练数据。

Conclusion: 从基础几何先验中学习是一种有前景且可泛化的途径，能够使视觉-语言模型具备更可靠的3D空间推理能力。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with robust 3D spatial reasoning. Prevailing methods that rely on fine-tuning with 3D visual question-answering (VQA) datasets may overfit dataset-specific biases, while integrating specialized 3D visual encoders is often inflexible and cumbersome. In this paper, we argue that genuine spatial understanding should emerge from learning fundamental geometric priors, not only from high-level VQA supervision. We propose GASP (Geometric-Aware Spatial Priors), a framework that injects these priors directly into the LLM's transformer layers. GASP employs a small correspondence head, applied as a deep supervision signal across all layers, and is trained with a dual objective leveraging ground-truth geometry from large-scale video scenes: a contrastive loss on ground-truth point correspondences enforces 2D view-invariance, while a depth consistency supervision resolves 3D geometric ambiguities. Our analysis first provides a diagnostic showing that standard VLMs' internal correspondence matching accuracy is very low (often below 5%). We then demonstrate that our training substantially improves this behavior, boosting peak layer-wise correspondence to over 70% and maintaining over 85% temporal robustness while baselines remain below 5%. These internal improvements translate to significant gains on downstream spatial benchmarks including +18.2% on All-Angles Bench and +29.0% on VSI-Bench, all without training on any 3D VQA data. Our findings indicate that learning from fundamental geometric priors is a promising and generalizable pathway towards VLMs with more reliable 3D spatial reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）通常在鲁棒的3D空间推理方面表现不佳。现有的依赖3D视觉问答（VQA）数据集进行微调的方法可能过拟合数据集特有的偏差，而集成专用3D视觉编码器的方法则往往缺乏灵活性且操作繁琐。在本文中，我们认为真正的空间理解应源于学习基础几何先验，而不仅仅来自高层VQA监督。我们提出GASP（几何感知空间先验）框架，该框架将这些先验直接注入LLM的Transformer层中。GASP采用一个小型对应头（correspondence head），将其作为深度监督信号应用于所有层，并利用大规模视频场景中的真实几何信息通过双目标进行训练：基于真实点对应的对比损失强制实现2D视角不变性，同时通过深度一致性监督解决3D几何歧义。我们的分析首先提供一个诊断结果，表明标准VLM的内部对应匹配准确率极低（通常低于5%）。随后我们证明，所提训练方法显著改善了这一问题，将逐层峰值对应匹配提升至超过70%，并保持超过85%的时间鲁棒性，而基线方法仍低于5%。这些内部改进迁移到下游空间基准中，取得了显著提升，包括All-Angles Bench提升18.2%，VSI-Bench提升29.0%，且所有这些均无需使用任何3D VQA数据进行训练。我们的研究结果表明，从基础几何先验中学习是一种有前景且可泛化的途径，能够使视觉-语言模型具备更可靠的3D空间推理能力。

</details>


### [79] [BullingerDB: A Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition and Writer Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30235)
*Marco Peer,Anna-Scius Bertrand,Patricia Scheurer,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BullingerDB是一个基于海因里希·布林格通信的大规模历史文档分析基准数据集，包含20,898页、499,222行文本，由796位作者跨越六十年书写，涵盖多语言内容。TrOCR模型在文本识别任务上取得9.1%的CER；作者检索任务中，时间感知的nDCG指标显示时间连贯性检索可行，但mAP（78.3%）表明长期风格变化带来挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有历史文档分析数据集缺乏大规模、多语言、带有丰富元信息（如作者身份、时间跨度）的基准，难以支撑多语种历史文本识别和时序感知的作者分析研究。

Method: 构建BullingerDB数据集，基于Heinrich Bullinger通信语料，包含796位作者六十年间的多语种文本及元信息；在文本识别任务上评估TrOCR等模型，在作者检索任务上提出时间感知的nDCG指标进行评估。

Result: 文本识别：TrOCR取得最佳性能，CER为9.1%；作者检索：时间感知检索可实现时间连贯性，但mAP为78.3%，表明长期风格变化对检索精度构成挑战。

Conclusion: BullingerDB为多语种历史文本识别和时序感知的作者分析建立了新的基准，其丰富的时间跨度和作者信息有助于推动相关领域研究。

Original Abstract: We present BullingerDB, a large-scale benchmark dataset for historical document analysis based on the correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575). The corpus comprises 20,898 pages and 499,222 text lines written by 796 writers over six decades, featuring stylistic variation, multilingual content (mostly Latin and Early New High German) as well as meta-information such as writer identity and time. We evaluate BullingerDB on text recognition and writer retrieval. TrOCR, the best performing model, achieves a CER of 9.1%. For writer retrieval, we introduce a temporal nDCG metric to assess time-aware retrieval. While temporally coherent retrieval is achievable, mAP (78.3%) scores indicate challenges due to long-term stylistic variation. With BullingerDB, we aim to establish a new benchmark for multilingual historical text recognition and temporally-aware writer analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了BullingerDB，一个基于Heinrich Bullinger（1504-1575）通信的大规模历史文档分析基准数据集。该语料库包含20,898页和499,222行文本，由796位作者在六十年间书写，具有风格多样性、多语言内容（主要为拉丁语和早期新高地德语）以及作者身份和时间等元信息。我们在BullingerDB上评估了文本识别和作者检索任务。表现最佳的模型TrOCR实现了9.1%的字符错误率（CER）。对于作者检索，我们引入了一个时间感知的nDCG指标来评估时间感知的检索性能。虽然可以实现时间连贯的检索，但mAP（78.3%）分数表明长期风格变化带来了挑战。通过BullingerDB，我们旨在为多语种历史文本识别和时序感知的作者分析建立一个新的基准。

</details>


### [80] [SAM3D-Phys: Towards Multi-Object Interactive Simulation in Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30239)
*Xin Dong,Weijian Deng,Lihan Zhang,Tianru Dai,Wenfeng Deng,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAM3D-Phys框架，利用SAM3D的生成式3D先验从多视图重建的不完整场景中恢复完整、可物理仿真的物体几何，并通过物理约束优化和掩码引导外观蒸馏确保场景一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多视图重建方法能生成视觉上精确的场景，但由于遮挡和观测有限，重建的物体几何往往不完整，无法直接用于物理仿真。需要一种方法从部分观测中恢复完整的、可仿真的物体几何，同时保持与原始场景的一致性。

Method: 1) 从多视图图像重建场景几何和物体部分观测；2) 利用SAM3D从部分观测推断完整物体几何；3) 通过物理约束空间优化算法将恢复的物体迭代对齐到原始位置；4) 通过掩码引导的外观蒸馏模块基于观测图像细化纹理保真度。

Result: SAM3D-Phys能生成干净的、适合物理仿真的物体表征，支持在重建场景中同时对多个物体进行一致且物理可信的交互仿真。

Conclusion: 通过恢复完整物体几何并还原其姿态和外观，SAM3D-Phys使重建场景中的物体可用于物理仿真，实现了多物体同时、物理一致的交互式仿真。

Original Abstract: This work addresses the problem of recovering complete, simulatable object geometry from reconstructed real-world scenes, enabling physics-based interaction with objects embedded in the scene. While modern multi-view reconstruction methods can produce visually accurate environments, objects are often incomplete due to occlusions and limited observations, making them unsuitable for physics simulation. To address this limitation, we propose SAM3D-Phys, a framework that integrates scene reconstruction with generative 3D priors of SAM3D to recover physically simulatable objects. Our approach first reconstructs the scene from multi-view images to obtain scene geometry and partial observations of objects. We then leverage SAM3D to infer complete object geometry from these partial observations. To ensure that the recovered objects remain consistent with the reconstructed scene, we restore scene-consistent object states through two complementary strategies: a physics-constrained spatial optimization algorithm that iteratively aligns the recovered object to its original location, and a mask-guided appearance distillation module that refines texture fidelity based on the observed images. By recovering complete object geometry and restoring its pose and appearance within the scene, SAM3D-Phys produces clean object representations suitable for physics-based simulation, enabling simultaneous and physically consistent interactive simulation of multiple objects within a reconstructed scene. Project page: https://chnxindong.github.io/sam3d-phys/

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对从重建的真实世界场景中恢复完整、可仿真的物体几何的问题展开研究，以实现与场景中嵌入物体的基于物理的交互。虽然现代多视图重建方法能够生成视觉上精确的环境，但由于遮挡和有限的观测，物体往往不完整，因此不适合用于物理仿真。为了解决这一局限，我们提出SAM3D-Phys框架，该框架将场景重建与SAM3D的生成式3D先验相结合，以恢复可物理仿真的物体。我们的方法首先从多视图图像重建场景，获得场景几何和物体的部分观测。然后，我们利用SAM3D从这些部分观测中推断出完整的物体几何。为确保恢复的物体与重建场景保持一致，我们通过两种互补策略恢复场景一致的物体状态：一种物理约束的空间优化算法，将恢复的物体迭代对齐到其原始位置；以及一种掩码引导的外观蒸馏模块，基于观测图像细化纹理保真度。通过恢复完整的物体几何并在场景中还原其姿态和外观，SAM3D-Phys生成干净的、适合物理仿真的物体表征，从而能够在重建场景内实现多个物体同时且物理一致的交互式仿真。项目页面：https://chnxindong.github.io/sam3d-phys/

</details>


### [81] [Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30244)
*Ya-Qi Yu,Hao Wang,Fangyu Hong,Xiangyang Qu,Gaojie Wu,Qiaoyu Luo,Nuo Xu,Huixin Wang,Wuheng Xu,Yongxin Liao,Zihao Chen,Haonan Li,Ziming Li,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RLR³，将RLVR从任务级验证扩展到准则级验证，通过双路径执行、最小暴露策略和层次化聚合，在部分可验证的视觉-语言任务中显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR仅适用于可确定性检查的任务，而许多视觉-语言任务仅能部分验证，需要多准则监督（如感知细节、推理步骤和约束）。评分标准（Rubrics）能提供细粒度监督，但其有效性依赖于在线RL中的执行准确性，现有方法存在不足。

Method: RLR³的核心包括：(1) 双路径执行——LLM作为抽取器配合确定性验证器，或LLM作为评判者处理不可验证准则；(2) 最小暴露策略——对抽取器屏蔽真实标签，对评判者屏蔽图像，确保评分忠实性；(3) 层次化聚合——优先考虑必要准则而非附加准则，并缓解滚动组内的分数饱和问题。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B上评估15个基准，RLR³一致优于RLVR，相比基模型提升4.7分，超过了官方的instruct-to-thinking模型差距。受控审计确认确定性验证和最小暴露策略显著减少了可被利用的假阳性。

Conclusion: RLR³通过准则级验证有效扩展了RLVR的适用范围，在部分可验证的视觉-语言任务上取得了显著性能提升，且验证机制的可靠性得到审计确认。

Original Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is effective for deterministically checkable tasks, many vision-language tasks are partially verifiable, demanding multi-criteria supervision (e.g., perceptual details, reasoning steps, and constraints). Rubrics provide a natural interface for this fine-grained supervision, but their effectiveness depends on the execution accuracy during online RL. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards ($\text{RLR}^3$), extending RLVR from task-level verification to criterion-level verification. $\text{RLR}^3$ routes instance-specific rubrics through two execution paths: an LLM-as-an-extractor paired with a deterministic verifier, or an LLM-as-a-Judge for non-verifiable criteria. To ensure faithful scoring, $\text{RLR}^3$ introduce a minimal exposure strategy that masks ground truths from extractors and images from judges. Furthermore, $\text{RLR}^3$ employs hierarchical aggregation to prioritize essential criteria over additional criteria, and mitigates score saturation within rollout groups. Evaluated on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B across 15 benchmarks, $\text{RLR}^3$ consistently outperforms RLVR, yielding a 4.7-point improvement over the base model and exceeding the official instruct-to-thinking model gap. Controlled audits confirm our deterministic verification and minimal exposure significantly reduce exploitable false positives.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）对于可确定性检查的任务是有效的，但许多视觉-语言任务仅能部分验证，需要多准则监督（如感知细节、推理步骤和约束）。评分标准（Rubrics）为这种细粒度监督提供了自然接口，但其有效性依赖于在线强化学习中的执行准确性。我们提出具有鲁棒评分标准奖励的强化学习（$\text{RLR}^3$），将RLVR从任务级验证扩展到准则级验证。$\text{RLR}^3$将实例特定的评分标准通过两条执行路径路由：LLM作为抽取器配合确定性验证器，或LLM作为评判者处理不可验证的准则。为确保评分的忠实性，$\text{RLR}^3$引入了最小暴露策略，该策略对抽取器屏蔽真实标签，对评判者屏蔽图像。此外，$\text{RLR}^3$采用层次化聚合来优先考虑必要准则而非附加准则，并缓解滚动组内的分数饱和问题。在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B上跨15个基准的评估表明，$\text{RLR}^3$一致优于RLVR，相比基模型提升4.7分，超过了官方的instruct-to-thinking模型差距。受控审计确认，我们的确定性验证和最小暴露策略显著减少了可被利用的假阳性。

</details>


### [82] [GenClaw: Code-Driven Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30248)
*Junyan Ye,Jun He,Zilong Huang,Dongzhi Jiang,Xuan Yang,Rui Chen,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenClaw 提出了一种基于代码驱动的智能体图像生成范式，将图像生成从黑盒过程转变为先概念构思、再代码草图、最后纹理着色的三阶段流程，实现了更可控和可解释的视觉生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像生成智能体依赖底层黑盒模型，工作流程局限于重复改写提示词进行生成优化，无法直接操作画布，缺乏对生成过程的精细控制与可解释性。

Method: GenClaw 采用三阶段流程：1）通过搜索与推理构建概念知识和上下文；2）利用代码（SVG、HTML、Three.js）渲染可执行的视觉草图；3）使用图像生成模型补充纹理、材质和逼真度。代码作为可控的中间画布桥接语言推理与像素合成。

Result: 论文提出了一种新型图像生成范式，但未提供具体实验定量结果（该摘要未涉及实验数据）。

Conclusion: GenClaw 将图像生成从黑盒范式转变为类似人类创作的分阶段过程，为实现高度可控和可解释的视觉生成系统迈出了一步。

Original Abstract: Image generation models have evolved from text-conditioned pixel synthesis toward multimodal agents endowed with visual comprehension and tool invocation capabilities. Yet, existing agents remain at the mercy of underlying black-box image models. Their workflow is trapped in a repetitive cycle of prompt rewriting for generation refinement, leaving them with no mechanism to directly manipulate the canvas. In essence, the potential of LLMs to serve as a genuine "brush" for precise visual construction remains largely untapped. In this paper, we propose GenClaw, a code-driven agentic image generation paradigm that empowers the agent to create like a human artist: first conceptualizing, then sketching, and finally coloring. Specifically, the agent first constructs the conceptual knowledge and context through search and reasoning. It then utilizes code (e.g., SVG, HTML, Three.js) to render executable visual sketches. Finally, it employs an image generation model to supplement textures, materials, and photorealism. In this workflow, code serves as a controllable intermediate canvas bridging linguistic reasoning and pixel synthesis, seamlessly integrating programmatic logic with the visual expressiveness of generative models. By transforming image generation from a black-box paradigm into a staged process akin to authentic human creation, GenClaw offers a step toward for highly controllable and interpretable visual generation systems.

中文摘要翻译: 图像生成模型已从基于文本条件的像素合成演变为具备视觉理解和工具调用能力的多模态智能体。然而，现有智能体仍然受制于底层黑盒图像模型。其工作流程陷入为优化生成而不断重写提示词的重复循环中，缺乏直接操作画布的机制。本质上，大语言模型作为真正"画笔"进行精确视觉构建的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。本文提出 GenClaw，一种基于代码驱动的智能体图像生成范式，使智能体能够像人类艺术家一样创作：先构思，再绘制草图，最后着色。具体来说，智能体首先通过搜索和推理构建概念知识和上下文；然后利用代码（如 SVG、HTML、Three.js）渲染可执行的视觉草图；最后使用图像生成模型补充纹理、材质和逼真度。在这一工作流中，代码作为可控的中间画布，桥接了语言推理与像素合成，将程序化逻辑与生成模型的视觉表现力无缝融合。通过将图像生成从黑盒范式转变为类似真实人类创作的分阶段过程，GenClaw 为实现高度可控和可解释的视觉生成系统迈出了一步。

</details>


### [83] [Ambient-robust Inverse Rendering using Active RGB-NIR Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30250)
*Hoon-Gyu Chung,Jinnyeong Kim,Hyunwoo Kang,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于主动RGB-NIR成像的环境鲁棒逆渲染方法，利用近红外闪光照明获取环境无关的点光源着色，通过三阶段逆渲染方法从多视角RGB和NIR图像中重建精确的几何与反射率，实验表明该方法在多环境光照场景下优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有逆渲染方法对环境光照条件敏感，常产生不准确的重建结果，因此需要一种对环境光照具有鲁棒性的逆渲染方法。

Method: 利用人类不可见的近红外闪光照明获取对环境光照不变的稳定点光源着色；设计主动成像系统（RGB-NIR相机+NIR闪光灯+移动基座）；提出三阶段逆渲染方法，利用多视角RGB环境光图像和主动NIR闪光图像，互补地重建精确几何与反射率；采集了首个多环境光照条件下的多视角RGB-NIR逆渲染数据集。

Result: 该方法在多种环境光照场景下均实现了精确的几何与反射率估计，性能优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 主动RGB-NIR成像结合三阶段逆渲染方法能有效克服环境光照变化对逆渲染的影响，实现鲁棒且精确的几何与反射率重建。

Original Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to reconstruct geometry and reflectance of objects from images. Despite recent progress, existing methods often produces inaccurate reconstructions that are sensitive to ambient illumination conditions. Here we introduce an ambient-robust inverse rendering method enabled by active RGB-NIR imaging. Our key insight is to leverage near-infrared (NIR) flash illumination-imperceptible to human observers-to obtain stable point-light shading that is largely invariant to ambient illumination. By using multi-view RGB images illuminated by ambient light and NIR images acquired with active NIR flash illumination, we reconstruct accurate geometry and reflectance by exploiting the complementary benefits of RGB and NIR images via a three-stage inverse rendering method. To enable dense multi-view acquisition, we develop an active imaging system equipped with a RGB-NIR camera and a NIR flash mounted on a mobile base. Using this system, we collect the first multi-view RGB-NIR inverse rendering dataset captured under multiple ambient illumination conditions. Experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms prior approaches, achieving accurate geometry and reflectance estimation across multiple ambient lighting scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 逆渲染旨在从图像中重建物体的几何形状和反射率。尽管近年来取得了进展，现有方法通常会产生不准确的重建结果，且对环境光照条件敏感。本文提出一种基于主动RGB-NIR成像的环境鲁棒逆渲染方法。我们的关键洞察是利用近红外闪光照明——对人类观察者不可见——来获得对环境光照基本不变的稳定点光源着色。通过使用环境光照下的多视角RGB图像和主动NIR闪光照明采集的NIR图像，我们利用RGB和NIR图像的互补优势，通过三阶段逆渲染方法重建精确的几何与反射率。为实现密集多视角采集，我们开发了一个主动成像系统，配备RGB-NIR相机和安装在移动基座上的NIR闪光灯。利用该系统，我们采集了首个在多种环境光照条件下捕获的多视角RGB-NIR逆渲染数据集。实验表明，我们的方法优于现有方法，在多种环境光照场景下实现了精确的几何与反射率估计。

</details>


### [84] [VideoFDB: Evaluating Full-Duplex Vision-Speech Capabilities in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30256)
*Amrita Mazumdar,Seonwook Park,Rajarshi Roy,Nikhil Srihari,Shengze Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Julia Wang,Koki Nagano,Shalini De Mello*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoFDB是首个评估全双工视听对话智能体的基准，包含237个真实世界视频片段和11种非语言对话动态，揭示了现有系统在视觉流利用和全双工非语言线索生成方面的系统性缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全双工对话基准仅评估语音模态，而自然人类对话本质上是全双工且视听多模态的，缺乏同时评估音频和视频模态的全双工基准。

Method: 构建了包含237个真实视频通话双人片段、11种非语言对话动态的数据集；提出区分感知与生成行为的分类体系；设计了基于评分标准的大语言模型作为评判官（LM-as-judge）评估框架，包含可解释的评估轴。

Result: 发现了系统性的故障模式：字幕坍缩和视觉流忽略；当前系统将视觉用于显式视觉问答而非流式视听联合接地；级联语音到头像系统在架构上无法产生全双工非语言线索。

Conclusion: VideoFDB作为首个全双工AV2AV交互基准，为系统评估奠定了基础，有望加速下一代多模态对话智能体的发展。

Original Abstract: Natural human conversation is full-duplex and audio-visual: people simultaneously speak and listen while continuously interpreting and producing nonverbal cues, such as nods, smiles, and gestures. To support successful human-agent interaction, agents must model full-duplex audiovisual conversation; however, existing full-duplex benchmarks evaluate only speech. In this work, we present VideoFDB, the first benchmark to evaluate full-duplex audio-visual-to-audio-visual (AV2AV) conversational agents. VideoFDB contributes (i) 237 dyadic clips spanning 11 nonverbal conversational dynamics from real-world video calls, (ii) a taxonomy separating perception from generation behaviors, and (iii) a rubric-based LM-as-judge evaluation framework with interpretable axes for assessing conversational quality with respect to nonverbal conversational dynamics. Across open- and closed-source vision-speech agents, we find systematic failure modes: captioning collapse and visual-stream ignorance, and we show that current systems exploit vision for explicit visual question answering but not for the streaming joint audiovisual grounding required in natural conversation. We further evaluate cascaded speech-to-avatar systems and find that their architecture fundamentally precludes the production of full-duplex nonverbal cues. As the first benchmark for full-duplex AV2AV interaction, VideoFDB establishes a foundation for systematic evaluation and, we hope, will accelerate the advancement and development of next-generation multimodal conversational agents.

中文摘要翻译: 自然的人类对话是全双工且视听多模态的：人们同时说话和倾听，同时持续地解读并产生非语言线索，例如点头、微笑和手势。为了支持成功的人机交互，智能体必须建模全双工的视听对话；然而，现有的全双工基准仅评估语音模态。在这项工作中，我们提出了VideoFDB，这是首个评估全双工视听到视听（AV2AV）对话智能体的基准。VideoFDB贡献了：（i）来自真实世界视频通话的237个双人片段，涵盖11种非语言对话动态；（ii）一个区分感知行为与生成行为的分类体系；以及（iii）一个基于评分标准的大语言模型作为评判官（LM-as-judge）的评估框架，包含可解释的评估轴，用于评估与非线性对话动态相关的对话质量。在对开源和闭源视觉-语音智能体的评估中，我们发现系统性的故障模式：字幕坍缩和视觉流忽略，并且我们表明当前系统将视觉用于显式的视觉问答，而非自然对话中所需的流式联合视听接地。我们进一步评估了级联式语音到头像系统，发现其架构从根本上阻碍了全双工非语言线索的生成。作为首个全双工AV2AV交互基准，VideoFDB为系统评估奠定了基础，并且我们希望它将加速下一代多模态对话智能体的发展与进步。

</details>


### [85] [Stable-Layers: Fine-Tuning Image Layer Decomposition Models with VLM-Scored Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30257)
*Ciara Rowles,Reshinth Adithyan,Nikhil Pinnaparaju,Vikram Voleti,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stable-Layers 是一个强化学习框架，通过视觉语言模型（VLM）的反馈信号微调预训练图层分解模型，无需配对监督数据，显著提升了图层分离质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 消除图层分解任务中对配对监督数据的依赖，并解决VLM单独评分时评价分数压缩、组内方差不足导致GRPO难以有效学习的问题。

Method: 基于Qwen-Image-Layered模型，采用Flow-GRPO与LoRA适配相结合的方法。对每张图像采样多个候选分解，使用VLM进行评分，并通过组相对优势优化策略。为克服评分压缩问题，设计了两阶段评估流程：第一阶段对每个样本进行基于五项编辑中心准则的结构化评分；第二阶段进行网格校准，让VLM对所有候选进行并排重新评分。

Result: 在Crello数据集上，Stable-Layers相比基线模型实现了更强的图层分离效果，更少的空白或伪影图层，以及更低的逐层重建误差。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段VLM评分机制有效解决了强化学习中奖励信号压缩的问题，证明了无需配对监督即可提升图层分解模型性能的可行性。

Original Abstract: We present Stable-Layers, a reinforcement learning framework that eliminates the need for paired supervision by fine-tuning a pretrained layer decomposition model using only feedback from a vision-language model (VLM). Starting from Qwen-Image-Layered, we apply Flow-GRPO with LoRA adaptation, sampling multiple candidate decompositions per image, scoring them with a VLM, and optimising the policy from group-relative advantages. The key challenge lies in designing a reliable reward signal: VLMs scoring samples in isolation tend to compress their judgements into a narrow band, leaving GRPO with little within-group variance to learn from. We address this with a two-stage evaluation pipeline that pairs structured per-sample scoring across five edit-centric criteria with a grid-based calibration step in which the VLM re-scores all candidates side-by-side. Stable-Layers produces decompositions with stronger layer separation, fewer blank or artifact-heavy layers, and lower per-layer reconstruction error on the Crello dataset compared to the base model.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Stable-Layers，一个强化学习框架，它仅使用来自视觉语言模型（VLM）的反馈信号来微调预训练的图层分解模型，从而消除了对配对监督数据的需求。基于Qwen-Image-Layered模型，我们采用Flow-GRPO算法配合LoRA适配，对每张图像采样多个候选分解结果，使用VLM对其进行评分，并根据组相对优势来优化策略。关键挑战在于设计可靠的奖励信号：VLM单独对样本评分时，倾向于将判断压缩到一个狭窄的区间内，导致GRPO缺乏足够的组内方差来进行有效学习。我们通过一个两阶段评估流程解决了这一问题：首先对每个样本进行基于五项编辑中心准则的结构化评分，然后结合一个基于网格的校准步骤，让VLM对所有候选结果进行并排重新评分。在Crello数据集上，Stable-Layers相比基线模型生成了图层分离更清晰、空白或伪影图层更少、逐层重建误差更低的分解结果。

</details>


### [86] [PhyGenHOI: Physically-Aware 4D Generation of Dynamic Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30268)
*Omer Benishu,Gal Fiebelman,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyGenHOI 提出了一种结合生成式人体运动与显式物理物体模拟的框架，利用3D高斯作为统一可微表示，通过窗口吸引损失、接触驱动重模拟和掩码视频SDS三种机制，从静态3D人体和物体生成物理一致且视觉逼真的4D人-物交互动态场景。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D人-物交互生成方法难以同时保证物理准确性和视觉逼真度，尤其是人体运动与物体动态之间缺乏物理一致性（如碰撞、动量传递等）。本文旨在解决这一挑战，实现根据文本指令驱动的、物理可信的4D交互场景生成。

Method: 将人体建模为由运动扩散模型（MDM）驱动的语义智能体，物体建模为由物质点法（MPM）模拟的物理智能体，以3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）作为统一可微表示。通过三种耦合机制监督交互：(1) 窗口吸引损失实现时间同步；(2) 接触驱动重模拟触发物理一致的动量传递；(3) 掩码视频SDS目标注入视频先验增强接触保真度。

Result: 实验表明，PhyGenHOI能在多种动作、人体和物体上生成物理一致的4D人-物交互，效果优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PhyGenHOI有效耦合了生成式运动模型与显式物理仿真，为4D人-物交互的物理一致性和视觉逼真度提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: We address the task of generating physically accurate and visually faithful 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI). Given a static 3D human and target object represented as 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS), our goal is to synthesize dynamic scenes where the human actively engages with the object through actions, such as punching or kicking, in accordance with a given input text. To this end, we introduce PhyGenHOI, a novel framework that couples generative human motion with an explicit physical object simulation. We model the human as a semantic agent driven by a Motion Diffusion Model (MDM) and the object as a physical agent simulated via the Material Point Method (MPM), utilizing 3D Gaussians as a unified, differentiable representation. We supervise their interaction through three coupled mechanisms: (1) A Windowed Attraction Loss that temporally synchronizes generative motion to intercept the object; (2) A Contact-Driven Re-simulation step that triggers physically consistent momentum transfer upon impact; and (3) A Masked Video-SDS objective that injects video-based priors to enhance contact fidelity. Experiments show PhyGenHOI generates physically consistent 4D HOI across diverse actions, humans, and objects, outperforming baselines. Project page and videos: https://omerbenishu.github.io/PhyGenHOI/

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了生成物理准确且视觉逼真的4D人-物交互（HOI）任务。给定以3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）表示的静态3D人体和目标物体，我们的目标是根据给定的输入文本，合成人体主动与物体交互（如出拳或踢腿）的动态场景。为此，我们提出了PhyGenHOI，一个将生成式人体运动与显式物理物体模拟相结合的新型框架。我们将人体建模为由运动扩散模型（MDM）驱动的语义智能体，将物体建模为通过物质点法（MPM）模拟的物理智能体，并以3D高斯作为统一的可微表示。我们通过三种耦合机制监督它们的交互：（1）窗口吸引损失（Windowed Attraction Loss），在时间上同步生成式运动以拦截物体；（2）接触驱动重模拟步骤（Contact-Driven Re-simulation），在碰撞时触发物理一致的动量传递；（3）掩码视频SDS目标（Masked Video-SDS），注入基于视频的先验以增强接触保真度。实验表明，PhyGenHOI能够在多种动作、人体和物体上生成物理一致的4D人-物交互，效果优于基线方法。项目页面和视频：https://omerbenishu.github.io/PhyGenHOI/

</details>


### [87] [Grounded 3D-Aware Spatial Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30307)
*An-Chieh Cheng,Yang Fu,Yatai Ji,Ligeng Zhu,Guanqi Zhan,Zhuoyang Zhang,Zhaojing Yang,Song Han,Yao Lu,Pavlo Molchanov,Vidya Nariyambut Murali,Jan Kautz,Xiaolong Wang,Hongxu Yin,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GR3D是一个统一的视觉语言模型，在一个框架内集成了显式2D定位、隐式2D定位和单目3D定位三种互补的定位能力。通过隐式定位机制在生成时插入区域令牌，结合区域提示的单目3D定位设计预测3D边界框，有效提升了空间理解能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在空间理解方面能力有限，缺乏有效的定位归纳偏置来将2D感知与3D推理相结合，难以处理复杂的空间理解问题。

Method: 1) 提出隐式定位机制，在生成空间思维链响应时识别实体提及并插入对应区域令牌；2) 设计区域提示的单目3D定位方法，从定位的区域查询预测相机视角下的3D边界框，结合内参感知归一化和密集几何监督；3) 将复杂空间理解问题分解为定位2D感知+3D推理两个阶段。

Result: GR3D在定位和非定位空间基准测试上均实现了一致的性能提升，证明定位作为有效的归纳偏置能够增强VLM的空间理解能力。

Conclusion: 多种定位能力的联合使用不仅完成了定位任务本身，还整体增强了模型的一般空间理解能力，验证了定位机制作为归纳偏置的有效性。

Original Abstract: We present GR3D, a spatial vision language model equipped with three complementary grounding capabilities--explicit 2D grounding, implicit 2D grounding, and monocular 3D grounding--within a single framework. GR3D introduces an implicit grounding mechanism that identifies entity mentions during generation and inserts the corresponding region tokens into the text stream, allowing the model to reference visual evidence on the fly when producing spatial chain-of-thought responses. In parallel, a region-prompted monocular 3D grounding design predicts 3D bounding boxes in the camera view from grounded region queries, supported by intrinsic-aware normalization and dense geometric supervision. Together, these grounding capabilities enable GR3D to decompose complex spatial understanding problems into grounded 2D perception followed by 3D inference. GR3D achieves consistent improvements across grounded and non-grounded spatial benchmarks, demonstrating grounding as an effective inductive bias for strengthening spatial understanding in VLMs. These grounding capabilities collectively enhance general spatial understanding beyond the grounding task itself.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出GR3D，一个空间视觉语言模型，在单一框架内配备了三种互补的定位能力——显式2D定位、隐式2D定位和单目3D定位。GR3D引入了一种隐式定位机制，在生成过程中识别实体提及并将对应的区域令牌插入到文本流中，使得模型在生成空间思维链响应时能够动态引用视觉证据。同时，一种区域提示的单目3D定位设计，从定位区域查询出发预测相机视角下的3D边界框，该设计由内参感知归一化和密集几何监督提供支持。这些定位能力共同使GR3D能够将复杂的空间理解问题分解为定位2D感知后接3D推理。GR3D在定位和非定位空间基准测试上均实现了一致的性能提升，证明了定位作为增强VLM空间理解能力的有效归纳偏置。这些定位能力共同增强了超越定位任务本身的一般空间理解能力。

</details>


### [88] [City-Mesh3R: Simulation-Ready City-Scale 3D Mesh Reconstruction from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30310)
*Sayan Paul,Sourav Ghosh,Siddharth Katageri,Soumyadip Maity,Sanjana Sinha,Brojeshwar Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出City-Mesh3R，一种面向城市场景的端到端可扩展框架，直接从大规模无序图像集合重建水密性表面网格，采用分治策略、拓扑图像聚类、几何感知相机选择和曲率自适应顶点密度重网格化，生成高质量规则网格。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有城市级3D重建方法（基于NeRF、Gaussian Splatting等）因几何不完整、表面不规则噪声而无法恢复适用于仿真的3D网格；小规模方法扩展到大规模场景计算成本极高。

Method: 1）通过拓扑图像聚类、聚类独立稀疏SfM和地图合并重建稀疏城市地图；2）空间划分后进行几何感知相机选择；3）密集表面重建和基于曲率自适应的顶点密度重网格化；4）拼接各分区网格得到全局城市网格。

Result: 在城市级重建数据集上的定性和定量结果表明，该方法能生成高保真水密3D网格，几何规则、表面细节丰富，且因分布式端到端处理可扩展到任意大规模场景。

Conclusion: City-Mesh3R通过端到端图像到网格的分治策略，有效解决了城市级3D重建中网格质量差和可扩展性低的难题，适用于大规模城市场景仿真。

Original Abstract: City-scale 3D surface reconstruction from multiview images for downstream 3D simulation, poses highly challenging problems due to the scale and complexity of urban scenes. Existing city-scale 3D reconstruction methods based on NeRF, Gaussian Splatting etc. often fail to recover 3D meshes ready for simulation due to incomplete/missing geometry and irregular, noisy surfaces. Scaling existing small-scale 3D reconstruction methods to arbitrarily large urban scenes is highly infeasible due to their computational complexity. We present City-Mesh3R, a scalable framework for reconstructing watertight surface meshes directly from large unordered image collections. Unlike recent methods which use global sparse SfM point-cloud initialization followed by a distributed 3D dense reconstruction of large-scale scenes, our method follows an end-to-end images-to-mesh 3D reconstruction approach using a divide-and-conquer strategy. The sparse city map is reconstructed via topological image clustering, cluster-wise independent sparse SfM and map merging, without need for exhaustive image feature matching. Then this map is partitioned spatially to perform geometry-aware camera selection, followed by dense surface reconstruction and surface refinement using curvature-aware adaptive vertex density remeshing. These partition meshes are then stitched together to produce the global mesh of the city. The proposed end-to-end framework is evaluated on city-scale reconstruction datasets. As demonstrated by our qualitative and quantitative results, our proposed method yields high-fidelity watertight 3D meshes with regular geometry, capturing fine surface details, and is suitable for scaling to arbitrarily large scenes owing to the end-to-end processing in a distributed setting.

中文摘要翻译: 针对下游3D仿真任务，从多视图图像进行城市级3D表面重建由于城市场景的规模和复杂性而极具挑战性。现有基于NeRF、Gaussian Splatting等的城市级3D重建方法，常因几何不完整/缺失以及表面不规则、噪声大而无法恢复适用于仿真的3D网格。将现有小规模3D重建方法扩展到任意大规模城市场景因其计算复杂性而高度不可行。我们提出City-Mesh3R，一个直接从大规模无序图像集合重建水密性表面网格的可扩展框架。与近期使用全局稀疏SfM点云初始化后进行大规模场景分布式3D稠密重建的方法不同，我们的方法采用基于分治策略的端到端图像到网格的3D重建方式。通过拓扑图像聚类、聚类独立稀疏SfM和地图合并来重建稀疏城市地图，无需穷举图像特征匹配。然后对该地图进行空间划分，执行几何感知相机选择，接着进行密集表面重建，并使用曲率自适应顶点密度重网格化进行表面细化。最后将这些分区网格拼接在一起，生成城市的全局网格。该端到端框架在城市级重建数据集上进行了评估。定性和定量结果表明，我们的方法能生成具有规则几何的高保真水密3D网格，捕捉精细表面细节，并且由于在分布式环境下进行端到端处理，适用于扩展到任意大规模场景。

</details>


### [89] [Archon: A Unified Multimodal Model for Holistic Digital Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30311)
*Chong Bao,Shichen Liu,Lijun Yu,David Futschik,Stylianos Moschoglou,Shefali Srivastava,Ziqian Bai,Feitong Tan,Guofeng Zhang,Zhaopeng Cui,Sean Fanello,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Archon 是一个完全预训练的、以人为中心的统一多模态模型，用于整体化虚拟人生成。它统一了七种模态，引入语义视频重参数化实现4倍token压缩，并提出“模态思维”机制以分解模糊的跨模态任务，在多种数字人生成任务上达到优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数字人是沉浸式交互的基础，但现有方法缺乏一个能够统一处理文本、音频、运动、视觉等多种模态的整体化生成模型，且高保真说话视频生成面临token爆炸的挑战。

Method: 1) 构建统一多模态模型，包含模态特定的分词器和一个预训练的自回归统一多模态模型，在同步模态和72个多样化任务上进行训练；2) 提出内存高效的语义视频重参数化，实现4倍token缩减同时保留细粒度动态，并搭配语义驱动的视频扩散解码器；3) 提出"Thinking in Modality"机制，将模糊的跨模态任务分解为逐步的模态链式思考，逐步提升保真度和可控性。

Result: 在多种数字人生成任务上，Archon 达到优越或可比的性能，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Archon 通过统一多种模态、高效token压缩和模态链式推理，为整体化数字人生成提供了一个有效的统一框架。

Original Abstract: Digital humans are fundamental to immersive interaction, yet creating a unified model for holistic modalities, including text, audio, motion, and visual content, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we present Archon, a fully pretrained, human-centric unified multimodal model for holistic avatar generation. Archon unifies seven modalities with modality-specific tokenizers, and a native autoregressive unified multimodal model pretrained on synchronized modalities and 72 diverse tasks to model holistic joint distributions. To address the token explosion challenge in high-fidelity talking videos, we introduce a memory-efficient semantic video reparameterization, achieving 4x token reduction while preserving fine-grained dynamics, coupled with a semantic-driven video diffusion decoder. We further propose a "Thinking in Modality" that decomposes ambiguous cross-modal tasks into stepwise thinking in an alternative chain of modality, progressively enhancing fidelity and controllability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Archon achieves superior or comparable performance across diverse digital human generation tasks, validating the effectiveness of our unified framework. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/archon/.

中文摘要翻译: 数字人是沉浸式交互的基础，然而构建一个统一处理包括文本、音频、运动以及视觉内容在内的整体化多模态模型，仍然是一个开放的挑战。在本文中，我们提出 Archon，一个完全预训练的、以人为中心的统一多模态模型，用于整体化虚拟人生成。Archon 通过模态特定的分词器统一了七种模态，并基于一个在同步模态和72个多样化任务上预训练的原生自回归统一多模态模型，对整体联合分布进行建模。为了解决高保真说话视频中面临的 token 爆炸挑战，我们引入了一种内存高效的语义视频重参数化方法，在保留细粒度动态的同时实现了4倍的 token 缩减，并搭配一个语义驱动的视频扩散解码器。我们进一步提出了"模态思维"机制，将模糊的跨模态任务分解为逐步的替代模态链式思考，逐步提升保真度和可控性。大量实验表明，Archon 在多种数字人生成任务上达到了优越或可比的性能，验证了我们统一框架的有效性。项目页面：https://zju3dv.github.io/archon/。

</details>


### [90] [VPG: Visual Prefix Guidance for Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30317)
*Xinyao Liao,Qiyuan He,Yicong Li,Jiayin Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Wei Wei,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉前缀引导（VPG），一种无需额外训练的推理时引导方法，通过对比生成前缀与损坏前缀下的模型输出来改进自回归图像/视频生成中的下一步预测，有效缓解曝光偏差和前缀漂移问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归图像和视频生成器在训练时使用教师强制（teacher-forced）历史，但推理时必须从自身生成的前缀中采样，导致曝光偏差（exposure bias）和前缀漂移（prefix drift）。现有方法要么修改训练过程，要么施加基于外部语义条件（如类别标签或文本提示）的采样时引导，而非检验下一步预测是否为已生成前缀本身提供强后验支持。

Method: VPG是一种无需训练（training-free）的推理时引导方法。它通过对比模型在生成前缀和损坏前缀下的输出，然后外推logits，使其趋向于能够增强生成前缀后验支持的候选预测。

Result: 在基于VAR的类别条件图像生成、基于Infinity的文生图和基于InfinityStar的文生视频任务中，VPG均提升了生成质量，无需重新训练基础模型。在VAR上将FID平均降低0.36，并在图像和视频生成的基准测试中均提升了性能。

Conclusion: VPG作为一种训练免费的推理时引导方法，能有效缓解自回归视觉生成中的曝光偏差和前缀漂移问题，显著提升生成质量。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive image and video generators are trained with teacher-forced histories but must sample from their own generated prefixes at inference time, making them vulnerable to exposure bias and prefix drift. Existing remedies either modify training or apply sampling-time guidance aimed primarily at external semantic conditions, such as class labels or text prompts, rather than testing whether a next-step prediction provides strong posterior support for the generated prefix itself. We propose Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG), a training-free inference-time guidance method for autoregressive image and video generation. VPG improves next-step prediction by contrasting the model's output under the generated prefix with its output under a corrupted prefix, then extrapolating logits toward candidates that strengthen the posterior support of the generated prefix. Across class-conditional image generation with VAR, text-to-image generation with Infinity, and text-to-video generation with InfinityStar, VPG improves generation quality without retraining the base model, reducing FID on VAR by 0.36 on average and improving benchmark performance on both image and video generation.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归图像和视频生成器在训练时使用教师强制（teacher-forced）历史序列，但在推理时必须从自身生成的前缀中采样，因此容易受到曝光偏差（exposure bias）和前缀漂移（prefix drift）的影响。现有的补救方法要么修改训练过程，要么施加主要针对外部语义条件（如类别标签或文本提示）的采样时引导，而非检验下一步预测是否为已生成前缀本身提供强后验支持。我们提出视觉前缀引导（Visual Prefix Guidance, VPG），一种无需训练（training-free）的推理时引导方法，用于自回归图像和视频生成。VPG通过对比模型在生成前缀与损坏前缀下的输出，然后外推logits，使其趋向于能够增强生成前缀后验支持的候选预测。在基于VAR的类别条件图像生成、基于Infinity的文生图和基于InfinityStar的文生视频任务中，VPG在不重新训练基础模型的情况下提升了生成质量，在VAR上将FID平均降低0.36，并在图像和视频生成的基准测试中均取得了性能提升。

</details>


### [91] [MonoPhysics: Estimating Geometry, Appearance, and Physical Parameters from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30320)
*Daniel Rho,Jun Myeong Choi,Matthew Thornton,Biswadip Dey,Roni Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoPhysics提出了一种从单视角视频进行可变形物体逆向物理估计的框架，通过可微MPM仿真和3D高斯泼溅联合优化几何、外观和物理参数，利用三种视觉-物理桥梁克服单目设置中的尺度模糊和几何不准确问题，性能接近多视角方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有逆向物理方法依赖多视角视频中的几何约束来恢复物理参数，但在单目设置下缺乏此类约束，导致严重的尺度模糊、不准确的几何结构以及外观优化与物理仿真之间的弱耦合问题。

Method: 提出MonoPhysics框架，结合可微MPM（物质点法）仿真和3D高斯泼溅，通过三个视觉-物理桥梁实现单视角观测下的联合优化：(1) 全局尺度对齐，(2) 物理感知的几何精化，(3) 可微位置映射。

Result: 在Vid2Sim和新构建的弹性与塑性物体数据集上评估，MonoPhysics在单目设置中优于现有基线，且仅使用单相机即可达到与多视角基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: MonoPhysics有效解决了单目逆向物理估计中的关键挑战，通过精心设计的视觉-物理桥梁实现了仅从单视角观测即可准确恢复物理参数，为实际应用提供了更经济的方案。

Original Abstract: Existing inverse physics methods recover physical parameters from multi-view videos, where geometric constraints across views resolve scale and 3D structure. In monocular settings, however, such constraints are absent, leading to severe scale ambiguity, inaccurate geometry, and weak coupling between appearance optimization and physical simulation. We propose MonoPhysics, a framework for monocular inverse physics estimation of deformable objects using differentiable MPM simulation and 3D Gaussian Splatting, which jointly optimizes geometry, appearance, and physical parameters from a single camera view. We address these challenges through three visual-physical bridges: global scale alignment, physics-aware geometry refinement, and a differentiable position map, which together enable accurate optimization from monocular observations alone. We evaluate on Vid2Sim and our new dataset of elastic and plastic objects, showing that MonoPhysics outperforms existing baselines in monocular settings and achieves performance comparable to multi-view baselines using only a single camera. Our project page is available at https://daniel03c1.github.io/MonoPhysics/

中文摘要翻译: 现有的逆向物理方法通过多视角视频恢复物理参数，其中跨视角的几何约束解决了尺度和三维结构问题。然而，在单目设置中，此类约束缺失，导致严重的尺度模糊、不准确的几何结构，以及外观优化与物理仿真之间的弱耦合。我们提出了MonoPhysics，一个基于可微MPM仿真和3D高斯泼溅的单目可变形物体逆向物理估计框架，它从单一相机视角联合优化几何、外观和物理参数。我们通过三种视觉-物理桥梁来解决这些挑战：全局尺度对齐、物理感知的几何精化以及可微位置映射，这些方法共同实现了仅从单视角观测就能进行精确优化。我们在Vid2Sim和我们新构建的弹性及塑性物体数据集上进行了评估，结果表明MonoPhysics在单目设置中优于现有基线，并且仅使用单相机即可达到与多视角基线相当的性能。我们的项目页面可在 https://daniel03c1.github.io/MonoPhysics/ 查阅。

</details>


### [92] [Veda: Scalable Video Diffusion via Distilled Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30325)
*Shihao Han,Hao Yang,Xinting Hu,Xiaofeng Mei,Yi Jiang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Veda提出了一种蒸馏稀疏注意力框架，通过统计感知的瓦片评分和头感知瓦片划分实现高稀疏度下的注意力近似，在视频扩散模型中达到5.1倍端到端加速，且生成质量无明显下降。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩展扩散Transformer生成高分辨率长视频时，自注意力的二次计算成本成为瓶颈；现有稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏度下生成质量严重退化。本文发现生成质量取决于稀疏掩码与全注意力瓦片级几何结构的对齐程度，而非稀疏率本身。

Method: Veda框架将瓦片选择建模为从全注意力的显式重构问题，包含统计感知的瓦片评分（减少估计误差）和头感知瓦片划分（减少结构失配）；设计硬件高效的瓦片跳过内核将理论稀疏性转化为实际加速。

Result: 在Waver-T2V-12B上生成720P 10秒视频：端到端加速5.1倍，自注意力加速10.5倍，注意力开销从92%降至50%；加速比随序列长度增加，具有良好的时空分辨率扩展性。

Conclusion: Veda通过显式对齐全注意力的瓦片几何结构，实现了高稀疏度下无损生成质量的显著加速，且加速优势随视频时空分辨率增长而扩大，适用于多种大规模视频扩散模型。

Original Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers to generate high-resolution, long videos is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention, and existing sparse attention methods degrade under high sparsity. We show empirically that generation quality is determined not by the sparsity ratio itself, but by how well the sparse mask aligns with the tile-wise geometry of full attention. Based on this insight, we propose Veda, a distilled sparse attention framework that formulates tile selection as an explicit reconstruction problem from full attention.
